iṉiyamutu patippakam
pi.11 kulmokar kuṭiyiruppu,

to.pē. 2433 9030
patippurai
oḷavai cu.turaicāmip piḷḷai
tamatu ōyvaṟiyā uḻaippāl  tamiḻ āyvuk kaḷattil uyarntu niṉṟavar. 20 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tamiḻ maṟumalarccikku puttuyir ūṭṭiya tamiḻc cāṉṟōrkaḷuḷ muṉ varicaiyil niṟpavar. naṟṟiṇai, puṟanāṉūṟu, aiṅkuṟunūṟu, patiṟṟuppattu ākiya caṅka nūṟ celvaṅkaḷukku uraivaḷam kaṇṭavar. caiva peruṅkaṭalil mūḻkit tiḷaittavar. uraivēntar eṉṟu tamiḻulakam pōṟṟip pukaḻappaṭṭa oḷavai cu.turaicāmip piḷḷai 1903il piṟantu 1981il maṟaintār. 
 vāḻnta āṇṭukaḷ 78. eḻutiya nūlkaḷ 38. itaṉai  poruḷ vaḻip pirittu “uraivēntar tamiḻttokai” eṉum talaippil 28 tokutikaḷāka veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷōm.
illaṟa ēntalākavum, urainayam kaṇṭa uravōrākavum , naṟṟamiḻ nāvalarākavum, caiva cittāntac cemmalākavum , niṟaipukaḻ eytiya uraivēntarākavum, pulamaiyilum perum pulamaipeṟṟavarākavum tikaḻntu viḷaṅkiya ipperuntamiḻācāṉiṉ nūlkaḷ aṉaittaiyum oru cēra veḷiyiṭuvatil perumai koḷkiṟōm. ivaruṭaiya nūlkaḷil  em kaikkuk kiṭaikkap peṟāta nūlkaḷ 5. maṟṟum ivar eḻutiya tiruvaruṭpā  nūlkaḷum it tokutikaḷil iṭam peṟavillai. 
  “ palvēṟu kālat tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ, uraikaḷ, varalāṟu, kalveṭṭu, camayaṅkaḷ eṉṟiṉṉa tuṟaip palavaṟṟil niṟaipulamai peṟṟavar oḷavai cu .turaicāmi avarkaḷ” eṉṟu mūtaṟiñar 
va.cupa. māṇikkam avarkaḷālum,
  “iravupakal tāṉaṟiyāṉ iṉtamiḻai vaittu
	 varavu celavaṟiyāṉ vāḻvil - uramuṭaiyāṉ
	 taṉkaṭaṉ tāynāṭṭu makkaṭ kuḻaippatilē
	 muṉkaṭaṉ eṉṟuraikkum ēṟu” 
eṉṟu pāvēntar pāratitācaṉ avarkaḷālum pōṟṟip pukaḻap paṭṭa ipperuntakaiyiṉ nūlkaḷai aṇikalaṉkaḷākak kōrttu, muttumālaiyākak  koṭuttuḷḷōm.   
 avar kālattil vāḻnta camakāla aṟiñarkaḷāl pōṟṟip pukaḻap paṭṭavar.  caiva ulakil taṉakkeṉat taṉiyiṭattaip peṟṟavar.  ivar eḻutiya aṉaittu nūlkaḷ maṟṟum malarkaḷ, itaḻkaḷil veḷivanta kaṭṭuraikaḷaiyellām tēṭit tēṭi eṭuttu orē vīccil veḷiyiṭukiṟōm. 
ittokutikaḷ aṉaittum mikacciṟappāka veḷivaruvataṟku muḻuottuḻaippum utaviyum nalkiyavarkaḷ avaruṭaiya tirumakaṉ oḷavai tu.naṭarācaṉ, marukar irā.kumaravēlaṉ, makaḷ vayiṟṟup peyartti tirumati vēṉilā sṭāliṉ ākiyōr āvar. ivarkaḷ it tamiḻttokaikku takka matippuraiyum aḷittu eṅkaḷukkup perumaic cērttu uḷḷaṉar. ivarkaḷukku neñcam niṟainta naṉṟi
taṉ matippu iyakkattil pērīṭupāṭu koṇṭu  uḻaitta ivvaruntamiḻaṟiñar tamiḻp pakaivarait tam pakaivarākak koṇṭa uyar maṉattiṉarāka vāḻntavar eṉpatai niṉaivil koṇṭu it tokai  nūlkaḷai  ipperuntamiḻ aṟiñariṉ 
107  ām āṇṭu niṉaivāka ulakat tamiḻarkaḷukkup payaṉpaṭum vakaiyil  veḷiyiṭuvatil perumakiḻvu koḷkiṟōm. tamiḻ nūl patippil eṅkaḷiṉ inta ariya muyaṟcikkut tōḷ tantu utavuṅkaḷ.

1. aṟivu
aṟivāvatu colvāratu iyalpu nōkkātu collap peṟum poruḷiṉ payaṉōkkik koḷḷutal oḻital ceyvatām. colvār tām uyarntōr, tāḻntōr, naṭṭōr, pakaivar eṉap palvakaiyiṉarātalōṭu, mukkuṇa vayattarātalāṉum, ivaruḷ ovvoruvariṭattu ovvoṉṟu orō vaḻik kēṭkap
paṭutalāṉum. avaratu iyalpu nōkkāmai vēṇṭum eṉpatu pulappaṭum. aṉṟiyum, vēṇṭappaṭuvatu poruḷēyaṉṟip piṟitaṉṟu. ākavē  "epporuḷ yār yār vāyk kēṭpiṉum apporuḷ - meypporuḷ kāṇpataṟivu" eṉṉum tamiḻmaṟai tuṇiyappaṭṭatu. paṭavē, colvāratu iyalpu nōkkip poruḷiṉ payaṉkāṇātu oḻital aṟiyāmai eṉpatum, ataṉāleytum payaṉ tuṉpamēyeṉpatum peṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivvuṇmai kīḻkkāṇum poruṭ kataiyil viḷaṅkutal kāṇka.
akaliru vicumpiṟ pāyiruḷ parukip pakalkāṉṟu eḻutarum parutiyañ celvaṉ kīḻkkaṭal mukaṭṭil eḻumuṉ, veḷḷi muḷaippa viṭiyal vantatu; ōtal antaṇar pāṭiṉar; kūviṉa pūṅkuyil; kūviṉa kōḻi; kurukukaḷ iyampiṉa; iyampiṉa caṅkam; iruñcēṟṟakaṉvayal muṭṭāṭṭāmaraittuñci, vaikaṟaik kaṭkamaḻum neytalūti. eṟpaṭak kaṇpōl malarnta kāmarcuṉai malarai añciṟaivaṇṭiṉ arikkaṇa molittaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉiṭaiyē, mukaiyaviḻnta murukkalaroṉṟai vaṇṭiṉañ cūḻvantu muralāniṟpa, "aiyakō! aiyakō!!"  eṉum aḻukuraloṉṟu, avvalarkkīḻ viḷaṅkiya aḻakiya taḷiriṭaiyeḻuntu, kēṭpōr cevip
pulampukku, avar tam maṉattiṭai aruḷūṟṟeḻac ceytatu. iṅṅaṉam, evvuyirkkum iṉpak kāṭci nalkum iḷañāyiṟu tōṉṟuṅkālait tōṉṟiya tuṉpak kāṭci āṇṭuṟṟa uyirp poruḷaṉaittum maruḷac ceytatu. yāṇṭum amaiti nilaviṟṟu. ciṟu curumpun tēṉāṭiṟṟaṉṟu. puṉaiyāvōviyam kaṭuppa yāvum pōntaṉavāka, mīṭṭum appulampicai kallum pullum kaṉinturuka eḻuntatu. ōcaiyiyaluṇarntāruḷarāyiṉ, atu, iṟantupaṭu
nilaikkaṇ palpoṟip pūcciyoṉṟiṭum mellicai yāmeṉa vuṇarvar. maṟṟu, yāṉ avvaṟivilēṉ ātaliṉ, neṭuṅkālan tāḻttup piṉṉarē yuṇarntēṉ. uṇarntēṉ, avvōcaiyai nuṉittukkēṭkumavā mīkkoṇṭēṉāka,  āṇṭeḻunta poruṇmoḻi piṉ varumāṟu kēṭṭēṉ. atu,
"irunilampurakkum iṟaiva! evvuyirkkum nalam payakkum iyavuḷ!! icciṉaikaḷai yāṉ īṇṭu iṭṭiṟattal ollumā! ā! ivai tōṉṟiya nāḻikaikūṭa iṉṉum kaḻintatiṉṟē! antō! itaṟkuḷ yāṉ iṟakkiṉṟēṉē! iṉṉātamma vulakam, iṉiya kāṇka itaṉ iyal puṇarntōrē eṉṟellām periyōr kūṟutal uṇmaiyē! iṟaiva! iṟattaṟkup paṇitta nī eṉakku ippacciḷañciṉaikaḷai yaḷittal eṟṟukkō? itu muṟaikol! yāṉ iṟantupaṭiṉ, ivvarumanta ciṉaikaḷaip purakkavallunar yāvar kollō? uyirkkuṇavaḷittaṟku nī uḷaiyeṉiṉum, oru paṟṟukkōṭumiṉṟi uyir nīttaṟkaṉṟē eṉ uḷam uḷaikiṉṟatu! antō! ē! ulakē! eṉṉē niṉ iyaṟkai!! uṉait tuṟakkum nilaikkaṇuṟṟa eṉ uḷḷattai iṅṅaṉam taḷaiceytalē niṉ paṇpupōlum!! naṉṟu! naṉṟu! ammavō! pulaṉkalaṅka poṟi
kalaṅka neṟimayaṅkukiṉṟatē! aṟivu aḻikiṉṟatē! āviyum alamarukiṉṟatē!! aiyakō! yāroṭu nōkēṉ, yārk
keṭutturaippēṉ! ācā karuḷiṉar yārkolō. ā!..." eṉpatu
iṅṅaṉam kūṟip pulampikkoṇṭirukkum palpoṟip pūcci (vaṇṇattuppūcci) oṉṟiṉarukē paccilaip puḻuvoṉṟu pōntu, ivvuraikēṭṭu eriyiṭaiyiḻuteṉa iṉaintu niṉṟatu. kaṇṭa appūcci "uṭaikalappaṭṭōrkku uṟupuṇai tōṉṟi yāṅku, iṭarppaṭum eṉ muṉṟōṉṟiya aṉṉāy! vāḻi! āruyir alamara aḻutu cāmpuvēṉukku nī ōr utaviyaruḷal kūṭuṅkollō! uṟṟuḻi utavutal uravōr kaṭaṉātaliṉ, uṉṉaik kāṇṭalum, utavi nāṭiṟṟu eṉ uḷḷam" eṉṟalum, puḻuvum "piṟaruṟu viḻumam tuṭaittal cālpeṉap periyōr kūṟuvar. avar vaḻi niṟṟal kaṟpavai kaṟpatiṉ aḻaku. ātaliṉ, niṉmāṭṭu eytiya tuṉpam yātu?
"	kaṭalpāṭavintu tōṇi nīṅki
	neṭunī riruṅkaḻi kaṭumīṉ kalippiṉum."
iyaṉṟaṉa ceytaṟ kiṭaiyēṉ. kūṟuka" eṉṟatu.
ikkūṟṟu pūcciyiṉ cevippulampukkatum, atu oruvāṟu maṉantēṟi, "paintaḻai vāḻkkai parittōy! āviyō nilaiyiṟ kalaṅkiyatu. atu eṉ yākkaiyiṉ akattatō puṟattatō aṟiyēṉ. innilaiyil iṟaivaṉ eṉakku ippacciḷañciṉaikaḷai yaruḷi
yuḷḷāṉ avaṟṟaiyum aṟiviliyāya yāṉ im murukkilaiyi
liṭṭuḷḷēṉ. avaṟṟai eṉakkuppiṉ kaḷaikaṇāy niṉṟu purappār oruvaraiyum kāṇātu maṟukukiṉṟēṉ. ivaṟṟaiyaruḷiya iṟaivarkku aḷittal kūṭum eṉpatu uṟutiyē eṉiṉum, eṉ uḷḷantaṭumāṟukiṉṟatu. ākaliṉ, uṟṟāriṉṟi yuyaṅkum eṉ muṉ orutaṉittōṉṟiya nīyē kaḷaikaṇāy niṟpāykol! aṅṅaṉam niṟṟal uṉakku aritaṉṟē. aṉṟiyum āvi tuṟakkum nilaiyilirukkum eṉṉuṭaiya ippulliya vēṇṭukōḷai maṟātu ēṟṟaruḷka. itu niṉakkup pēraṟamākum," eṉṟatu.
puḻu : ām. pēraṟamē. āyiṉum ivaṟṟai yāṉ purattal yāṅṅaṉam? ivaiyum eṉ iṉattaic cārntaṉa vallavē!
pūcci : cārāmaiyāl varum kuṟṟamoṉṟumillai. yāṉ 
kūṟum uṇavaimaṭṭil nī aruttiyoṭu aḷittuvariṉ, oruvakai iṭaiyūṟum nērātu. itu muṟṟum uṇmai.
puḻu : aṟṟēl, uraittaruḷ. niṉ uṭal nilaiyum kuṉṟi varukiṉṟatu. kālam tāḻttaṟka.
pūcci : kūṟuval. ceṭikaḷiṉ iḷantaḷirum paṉinīrum tām avaṟṟiṟkut takka uṇavām. vēṟu evaiyēṉum aḷikkap
peṟiṉ, avai uṇṇātu iṟantupaṭum. ātaliṉ ikkūṟiya uṇavai...yē...a...
eṉṟu kūṟikkoṇṭē uyirai viṭukiṉṟatu.
kaṇṭukalaṅkiya puḻuviṟku acciṉaikaḷaiyum, avaṟṟiṟ keṉap paṇikkappeṟṟa uṇavaiyum niṉaittoṟum uḷḷam nekkuruka, niṉaivu taṭumāṟaluṟṟatu. itu iyaṟkaiyē. cettāraikkaṇṭu cāvārum varuntutal iyalpaṉṟē! tāntāmum tam āṟṟalaṟintaṉṟō ovvōr paṇiyai āṟṟa muṉ varal vēṇṭum. eṇṇittuṇika karumam. tuṇintapiṉ eṇṇuvam eṉpatu iḻukkaṉṟō! niṟka, ceyakkaṭampūṇṭa paṇiyaiyev
vāṟṟāṉum ceytu muṭittalum vēṇṭumaṉṟō. iṟantataṉai eṇṇutalāl iṉip payaṉ yātēṉum eytumō? meyvaruttam pārttal, pacinōkkal, kaṇṭuñcal, cevviyarumai pārttal, avamatippuk koḷḷal ākiya ivaṟṟaik karumamē kaṇṇāyiṉār kaṇṇāraṉṟē. ciṟuciṉai purakkum perumpaṇi mēṟkoṇṭa puḻu oruvāṟu tēṟi, ''aiyakō! ivaṟṟai yāṉ eṅṅaṉam purattal kūṭum? iḷantaḷirum paṉi nīrum evaṇ kiṭaikkum? kiṭaippiṉum yāṉ evvāṟu koṇarvēṉ? icciṉaikkuṭpiṟaṅkum pūccikaḷ paṟakkuntaṉmaiya; yāṉ avviṉattaic cārēṉ. iyaṟkaiyāṉ māṟupaṭṭayāṉ purattaṟku uṭaṉpaṭṭēṉē! eṉṉē eṉ aṟiviruntavāṟu!! ivaṟṟiṉiṉṟeḻum pūccikaḷ ciṉṉāṭkaḷiṟ paṟanteḻumē, aññāṉṟu yāṉ avaṟṟai evvāṟu aṭakkutal kūṭum! ā! eṉṉa kāriyam ceytēṉ! 'āyntiṭum uṇarvoṉṟillār alamāl iyaṟkai ammā!' eṉap periyōr kūṟutal ettuṇai yuṇmaiyuṭaittu," eṉa vīḻntu cōrntu, piṉṉarc ciṟitu teḷintu, ''iṉiyāṉ iṅṅaṉam varuntutal kūṭātu.  itaṉai muṉṉarē naṉku ārāyntiruttal vēṇṭum 'viḻaiyāvuḷḷam viḻaiyumāyiṉum... taṟṟakavuṭaimai nōkki, maṟṟataṉ, piṉṉā kummē muṉṉiyatu muṭittal' eṉpar nallicaic cāṉṟōr. kaḻintaṉa paṟṟik kalaṅku tuyaruḻattal eṉa nalattiṟkē kēṭutaru moṉṟākaliṉ,. ceytal mēṟkoṇṭa ippaṇiyai, aṟiviṉ mikkār poruḷurai kēṭṭut takkaṉa tērntu ceyval. ituvē yāṉ iṉic ceyaṟpāṟ ṟaṉṟip piṟitoṉṟu maṉṟu" eṉat tērntu niṉṟatu. akkālai, tēṉūṇ
cuvaiyiṉum ūṉūṇ cuvaiyē uyiriṉum virumpivāḻum pūñai oṉṟu āyiṭaip pōtantatu. kaṇṭapuḻu ataṉai yaḷavāḷāvit tāṉ mēṟceyaṟkēṟṟa virakiṉaiyaṟiya avākkoṇṭatu. koḷḷiṉum, ataṉ uḷattac ciṟitu nāḻikaikkuḷ appūñai taṉ vēṇṭukōṭkuc cevi cāykkātu, taṉakkum. taṉṉuḻaik kaiyaṭaiyāt tarappeṟṟa ciṉaikaṭkum ūṟiḻaikkiṉumiḻaikkum eṉṉum ōr eṇṇam eyta atu ammuyaṟciyaik kaiviṭṭatu.
kaiviṭalum, pōṉa veñcuram puḷiyiṭa vantāṅku, maṟain
tirunta varuttam mīṭṭum ataṉ maṉattē tōṉṟa, atu ciṟitutēṟi, taṉ maṉakkiyainta aṟavurai kūṟum āṟṟaluṭai yār iṉiyāvaruḷar eṉṉumōr ārāycciyai p piṉṉarum ceytal toṭaṅkiṟṟu. avvuḻik kumpiṭappōṉa teyvam kuṟukkē vanteṉa, vāṉiṟ paṟantuḻalum vaṉciṟaipparun toṉṟu ataṉarukē ceṉṟatu. itaṉai yappuḻukkāṇṭalum, taṉ maṉattiṟ palatiṟappaṭṭa eṇṇaṅkaḷaik koṇṭatu. avaṟṟuḷ, "ipparuntiṉum aḵki yakaṉṟavaṟivuṭaiyār yāvaruḷar? maṉattāṉum eṇṇaṟkiyalāta cēṇiṟ paṟattalum, āṇṭiruntē, maṇmītiyaṅkum tiṟṟikaḷai (īṇṭut tiṉṉaṟkuriya ciṟṟuyirkaḷai yeṉak koḷka)k kaṇṭu, avaṟṟiṉmēṟ kuṟipiḻaiyātu ñerēleṉap pāyntu paṭin
tuṇṭalumāya uyariya ceyalkaḷaic ceyalvallār vēṟoru
varumila raṉṟē! ākaliṉ, itaṉiṭai, yāṉ eṉakkāvaṉavaṟṟai yucāviyaṟikiṉṟēṉ" eṉpatu moṉṟeṉka.
iṅṅaṉam eṇṇi muṭivuceytu koḷḷum appuḻu virunta pulattiṟ kayalirunta vayaloṉṟil vēṟu paruntoṉṟu vāḻntu vantatu. ataṟkum appuḻuviṟkum neruṅkiya naṭpu muṇṭu. annaṭpurimai yētuvāka, puḻu, apparuntiṉai varavaḻaittut tāṉ acciṉaikaḷai vaḷarkku māṟṟait 'terintu kōṭalai nāṭi niṟpa, celvakkālai niṟpiṉum allaṟkālai nillā māṭciyamainta kēṇmaicāl paruntum kākatāliyamākap puḻuvaip pārttuc celvāṉ vantatu. varakkaṇṭa puḻuvum kaḻipēruvakaikoṇṭu, ''aṉpu keḻumiya naṇpē! varuka iṉpaṅkaṉiyum niṉmukaṅkaṇṭum yāṉ iṉpamiṉṟiyirut talai yaṟitiyō? iṅṅaṉamirukku meṉakku oru māṟṟukkūṟal vallai kollō?" eṉṟatu.
paruntu : aṟival. maṟṟu niṉṉaiyuṟṟa tuṉpam oṉṟuṇṭeṉa aṟintaṉaṉēyaṉṟi atu ittaṉmaittu, ivvētu
vuṭaittu eṉpavaṉa vaṟṟai yaṟiyēṉākaliṉ, māṟṟukkūṟal yāṅṅaṉam iyalum?
puḻu  : naṉṟu. kēṭṭiciṉ. eṉakku niṉṉoppa uyirt tōḻamai pūṇṭa cēṭappūcci (vaṇṇattuppūcci) yoṉṟu īṇṭuc ciṉṉāṭkaḷukku muṉ vāḻntu vantatu. ataṉ vaḻiyeccamē īṇṭu nī kāṇkuṟum pacciḷañ ciṉaikaḷ. ivaṟṟai atutāṉ iṟakkuṅ kālattu īṉṟu, eṉṉaiyum puṟantarumāṟu paṇittatumaṉṟi, ivai kuṭampai taṉit toḻiyap paṟantēku nāḷkāṟum taṟkuṇavaḷit tōmputal ceyyumāṟum kūṟiyakaṉṟatu. avvōm paṭaiyēṟṟa yāṉ aṅṅaṉam ceyyumā ṟaṟiyātu alamarukiṉṟēṉ.
paruntu : eṉṉai?
puḻu  : av vāṟṟiṉai yāṉ aṟiyēṉākaliṉ.
paruntu : eṉ aṟiyāy?
puḻu : appūcciyō paṟakkumiyalpiṟṟu; maṟṟu, yāṉō ūrvaṉavaṟṟaic cārnta pulliya puḻuvāṉēṉ. paṟappaṉavaṟṟai ūrvaṉa purattal,
''	pāytiraip puṇari pāṭavin toḻiyiṉum,
  kāykati riraṇṭuṅ katitiri yōṭiṉum,"
kūṭātu eṉpatu uṇmaiyaṉṟō. atu paṟṟiyē im mayakkam eytiyatu.
paruntu : mayaṅkal vēṇṭā. nuṉittu nōkkiṉ, ivaṟṟaip 
purattalum, ēṟṟa vuṇavu koṭuttalum niṉṉā leḷitiṟ ceyaṟpālaṉa veṉpatē tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. aṉṟiyum, celvuḻic celvuḻi meynniḻal pōla, niṉait toṟum niṉaittoṟum immuṭipē eytukiṉṟatu.
puḻu :  uyntēṉ! uyntēṉ!! kūṟuka.
paruntu : kūṟuval. taṭaiyumiṉṟi. maṟṟu, yāṉ kūṟuvaṉavaṟṟai nī uḷḷavāṟē yuṭkoḷḷuvāyō veṉṟaiyuṟu kiṉṟēṉ. epporuḷ ettaṉmaittāyiṉum, aṉṟi yāryārvāyk kēṭpiṉum, apporuḷiṉkaṇ meyp poruḷ kāṇṭal kūṭum eṉṉum uṟuti nōkku (Faith) niṉṉuḻai yuḷatāṉālaṉṟi, eṉ moḻiyiṉ uṇmaip poruḷ pulaṉākātu. innōkkiṉṟik kēṭṭal araṅkiṉṟi yāṭal purivāratu nōkkiṉō
ṭokkum.
puḻu : ām. cārntataṉ vaṇṇamātalē maṉaviyalpu. en 
nōkkaṅ koṇṭu epporuṇōkkappaṭukiṉṟatō, annōkkiṟ kamaintē apporuḷun tōṉṟum. oruvaṉaik kuṟṟamuṭaiyaṉē yeṉakkaruti nōkku vārkku avaṉ kuṟṟamuṭaiyaṉākavum,  allaṉeṉak karuti nōkkuvārkku allaṉākavum tōṉṟal kaṇ kūṭaṉṟō! niṟka, yāṉ, avvuṟutinōkku uṭaiyēṉ; veḷip
paṭaiyākak kūṟiṉ, nī kūṟumaṉaittaiyum kūṟi yāṅkē nampuval. itu uṟuti.
paruntu : uṟutiyāyiṉ, kūṟuval: mutaṟkaṇ nī ivaṟṟiṟ
kēynta vuṇavu yāteṉa niṉaikkiṉṟaṉai? niṉ uḷattil tōṉṟuvaṉavaṟṟai maṭṭiṟ kūṟuka.
puḻu : (niṉaittu) yātākum? paṉittivalaiyum paintēṉumām ! ā!
paruntu : allavē. avaṟṟiṟkēṟku muṇavai nī mikka eḷitiṟ koṇartal kūṭumē. aṉṟiyum, atu ivvaṉaittiṉum mikka aṇmaiyiṟ kiṭaittaṟpāṟṟaṉṟō!
puḻu : (tikaittu) eṉṉarukil, eḷitil, peṟakkūṭiyatu immucuk koṭṭaiyilai yaṉṟip piṟitoṉṟumilaiyē.
paruntu : ām! ām!! naṉṟu coṉṉāy! nīyē avvuṇavai naṉku uṇarntu koṇṭaṉai. im mucukkoṭṭaiyilai kaṭām avaṟṟiṟkut takkavuṇavāvaṉa.
puḻu : (ciṉantu) ē! eṉṉē niṉ maṭamai!! ivaṟṟaiyaṉṟō atu tarutal kūṭāteṉa vaṉpuṟai ceytatu. mukanaka voṉṟu moḻitalum, piṉṉar maṟṟoṉṟu ceytalum aṟaṉalla. ataṉāl vilakkappaṭṭa vuṇavaiyē yaḷikkumāṟu kūṟal niṉ aṟivuṭaimaik kaḻakaṉṟu; amaitiyumaṉṟu.
paruntu : aṉpē! poṟuttaruḷ. iṉiya uḷavāka. iṉṉāta kūṟal kaṉi iruppak kāykavarntaṟṟu eṉpatai niṉaittaruḷka. niṟka, ivaṟṟiṉ tāyppūcci iv vuṇmaiyiṉai yaṟiyātu. atu perumpētai. aṉṟiyum yāṉ kūṟuvaṉavaṟṟai yuṟuti nōkkuṭaṉ kēṭpaleṉak kūṟi, kūṟakkēṭṭalum, uṇmai nōkkātoḻital niṉ uyarvukkuk kuṟaiyaṉṟō!aṟivum, amainta nampikkaiyum amaivuṟiṉaṉṟō vuṇmai teḷḷitām.
puḻu : ō! aṟṟaṉṟu. eṉ cevippulaṉām poruḷ pala vaṟṟiṉum amaivuṟum uṇmaipporuḷ kāṇṭalēeṉṉiyalpu. uṟutinōkkum, uṇmai nampikkaiyum uṭaiyāriṉ mikkār
tēriṉ, yāṉalatillai ivvulakattāṉē eṉpatai mīṭṭum niṉakku vaṟpuṟuttukiṉṟēṉ. aṟika!
paruntu : aṟivateṉ! niṉcolliṉ valiyiṉmaiyai niṉ coṟkaḷē naṉku kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. yāṉ kūṟum uṇavup poruḷaiyē nī mutaṟkaṇ ēlā toḻiyiṉ, piṉṉark kūṟappōvaṉavaṟṟai nī eṅṅaṉam ēṟṟal amaiyum? niṟka, ippacciḷañ ciṉaikaḷ porittalum eṉṉāmeṉa niṉaik kiṉṟaṉai? itaṉaiyēṉum uṇmaiyākak kūṟuti.
puḻu : eṉṉām! vitaiyoṉṟu pōṭap patamoṉṟu viḷaiyuṅkol “paṇṭu ceyviṉaiyalāṟ paravu teyva
moṉṟuṇṭeṉil, tāṉ payaṉ utava vallatō” eṉṉum cem
poruḷaiyum teḷika. ākaliṉ, cēṭattiṉ ciṉai cēṭamā mēyaṉṟik  kīṭamākā.
paruntu : aṟṟaṉṟu. cēṭattiṉ ciṉai cēṭamākātu mutaṟkaṭ kīṭamē yām. uṇmaiyaik kūṟiṉ, avai porittalum, niṉṉoppap puḻukkaḷāmē yaṉṟi nī niṉaittuk kūṟum vaṇṇattup pūcci kaḷākā. nī ivvuṇmaiyai ēlātoḻiyiṉum oḻika. uṇmai piṉṉarp pulaṉām. yāṉ ceṉṟu varuval.
	iṅṅaṉam kūṟikkoṇṭē paruntu cēṇōkkic civveṉṟeḻuntu paṟantu ceṉṟatu. aṉṟiyum, āṇṭē yiruntu, puḻuviṟku uṇmaiyuṇarttum vakaiyāṟ kalāyttaṟku apparuntu virumpiṟṟaṉṟu. 'iruḷpaṭu neñcattu iṭumpai tīrkkum aruḷ naṉku uṭaiyarāyiṉum, cāṉṟōr, paḻiyoṭu varūum iṉpam veḵkār’ eṉpar periyār. ākavē, paruntum kalāyttup paḻiyeytaṟku añci yakaṉṟateṉka.
iṅṅaṉam viḻumiya naṭpukkaṭampūṇṭa paruntu piritalum. puḻu mikkatōr maruṭcikoṇṭu, ceytoḻilaṟiyātu tikaittu arukukiṭakkum ciṉaikaḷaik kāṇṭalum, kaṇṇīrukuttalum, paṉmuṟai yavaṟṟaic cūḻvaralum ceytu koṇṭu varum. iṭaiyiṭaiyē paruntukūṟiya moḻikaḷai eḻuttaḷavāy āyum; poruṭpayaṉaip paṉmuṟaiyum cūḻntu nōkkum; taṉ yākkaip paṇpaiyum, pūcciyiṉ polivaiyum maṉattāṉoppu nōkkum. atukālaippiṉ varumāṟum niṉaikkum. atu;
“māntar iṉattiyalpatākum aṟivu” eṉpatu tamiḻmaṟai. itupaṟṟiyaṉṟē, uyarntōr kūṭṭamē yuṟavāk koṇṭuḻalum ipparuntum mikka aṟivamaitiyuṭaiyatā meṉṟeṇṇik keṭṭēṉ! orukāl, atu immuṟai neṭitu cēṇiṟ paṟantirukkum, ataṉāṟṟāṉ ikkalakkam! ā! eṉṉa alakait taṉmai! ulakattōr uṇṭeṉpa tilleṉpāṉ vaiyattu alakai yeṉṟu eṇṇappaṭumaṉṟō! ā! uyirk kūṭṭattuḷ, uyarntārōṭu kūṭi vāḻvaṉavum, kūṭi vāḻvatākat tammaiyu
yarttik kūṟik koḷvaṉavumāya pala vuyirkaḷ muṭivil ettuṇaip pētaimaiyum koṭumaiyum uṭaiyavāyt tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. eṉṉa ulakam! peṟutaṟkariya yākkai tamakku eytiṟ ṟeṉṟāl, atu koṇṭu aṟivum oḻukkamum amaiyappeṟṟu, takkaviṉṉa, takātaṉaviṉṉaveṉa okka vuṉṉi, oppaṉavaṟṟaik kōṭalaṉṟō yāvarkkum oru paṭittāy vēṇṭappaṭuvatu.
“	kaṉṟu muṇṇātu kalattiṉum paṭātu 
	nallāṉ ṟīmpāl nilattuk kāṅku.”
ākumaṉṟō, iḵtu ōr uyirkku eytāviṭiṉ ataṉ polivu kūr yākkai. niṟka. ippaṟavai avvicumpaip paṭarntu ceṉṟu ceyyum toḻil yātu kol! neṭuṅ kālamaḷavum iḵtu āṇṭup paṟantu koṭpuṟutaliṉ uṭkiṭaiyeṉṉō? itaṉoṭu aṅku uraiyāṭi nallaṟivu koḷuttavallār yāvarō? ivvaṉaittum, eṇṇun tōṟum mayakkattai yaṉṟō tarukiṉṟaṉa. eṉṉa iṟumpūtu payakkum vāḻvukāṇ itaṉ vāḻvu!” eṉpatu.
iṅṅaṉam, atu, niṉaittavaṇṇam paṇṭēpōṟ ṟaṉ ciṉaikaḷaic cūḻvantu kāval purintu vantatu. atupōtu, paruntiṉ mellicaimaṭṭiṟ puḻuviṉ cevippulaṉākalum, atu, muṉṉaik kaḷippiṉṟeṉiṉum, ciṟitu tēṟi, nōkkak kuḻaiyum eṉpataṟkañci, iṉimai tōṉṟa oruvaṇṇam etirnōkki niṉṟatu. paruntum vantatu. iraṇṭaṉ naṭpum nakutaṟ poruṭṭaṉṟākalāṉum.
“	iṉpamum iṭumpaiyum puṇarvum pirivum
	naṉpaka lamaiyamum iravum pōla
	vēṟuvē ṟiyala vāki māṟetirn tuḷa.”
eṉṉum poruḷai ivai naṉkuṇarntavai yākalāṉum, muṉṉar nikaḻcciyiṟ puḻuviṉ ciṉattai yaṟavē maṟaiya oṉṟōṭoṉṟu ciṟitu aḷavaḷāvip piṉ varumāṟu mīṭṭum avai collāṭal toṭaṅkiṉa.
paruntu :	yāṉ kūṟuvaṉavaṟṟai, muṉṉarē kūṟiyuḷḷavāṟu, uṟutinōkkum, oppaṉa koḷḷum uḷamum koṇṭu kēṭpaiyēṟ kūṟuval.
puḻu : āyiṉ, avai eṉṉiṭat tilaveṉa veṇṟuti pōlum! naṉṟu naṉṟu!! ā! kēḷviyilā teytum evvakaip
poruḷaiyum uṟutinōkkuk koṇṭa, uḷattiṟkōṭarē eṉṉiyalpeṉa yāṉ kūṟi yirukku matu niṉ niṉaiviṉkaṇillai kollō!
paruntu : “uṇarntaṉa maṟakkum, maṟantaṉavuṇarum” eṉpar periyōr. iṉi maṟavēṉ; poṟuttaruḷka. niṟka yāṉ muṉpu kūṟiyaṉa niṉṉaiyum, niṉ ciṉaikaḷai yum paṟṟip  potuvāka vuṇarntaṉa; iṟṟai ñāṉṟukūṟappōvatu niṉporuṭṭu ṇarnta ciṟappup poruḷākum. atu, curuṅkak kūṟiṉ, nīyum orupakal appūcciyē yātal vēṇṭum eṉpatu.
puḻu : eṉṉai kūṟiṉai? ē, maṭapparuntē! niṟuttuka niṉ coṟ poḻiviṉai! niṉṉoṭu nimirntañēyam ippayaṉaip payakkum nīrmaittāyiṉ, eṉ kūṟuvatu. pālākit tōṉṟip parukiṉār āvi koḷḷum ālāla nīrmaittē niṉṉaṉpu eṉpatē cālap poruntuvatu. polivu, tiṭpam mutaliya villā yākkaiyēṉ eṉa eṇṇi iṉṉaṇam eṉṉai iḻittuk kūṟuvāṉ ceyta kūṟṟēyitu. aṟintēṉ. niṉ taṉmaiyai yāṉ iṉṟē uṇarntēṉ. nī peruṅkoṭiyai! aṟivili! tirutta viyalāt tīyai! pētai!! nāṇiṉṟi eṉ muṉṉiṟṟal niṉ māṇpiṟkē kuṟaivām. iṉi eṉakku niṉ poṅku cōṟum vēṇṭā: pūcārittaṉamum vēṇṭā. pōtum! pōtum!!
ivvaṇṇam kūṟiya puḻuviṉ neñcucuṭu neṭumoḻi vaṉciṟaipparuntiṉ maṉattut tiṉaittuṇaiyum ciṉat tī koḷuttiṟṟiṉṟu. eṉṉai, vaṉmaiyuḷ vaṉmai maṭavārp poṟai yākaliṉ. maṟṟu, atu, ataṉmaṉattut “tiṟaṉalla taṟpiṟar ceyyiṉum nōnontu aṟaṉalla ceyyāmai naṉṟu” eṉṉum teyvat tiruvaḷḷuvap payaṉai yeḻac ceytu, uṟuvatu tērāp puḻuviṉmāṭṭu irakkamum, ataṉ aṟiyāmaikkuk kavaṟciyumē tōṉṟac ceytatu. vēṟu eṉ ceyum!! atu appuḻuviṉai, “aṉpa,  nī vekuṇṭu kūṟuvaṉayāvum veḷiṟṟuraikaḷē. oru
puṭai, avai, nī icciṉai māṭṭup pūṇṭoḻukum pērātaraviṉ uṟutiyaip pulappaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa veṉiṉum, āṉṟa kēḷviyāṟṟōṭṭa ceviyai yallai yeṉpataik kāṭṭātu kaḻiyavillai. eṉ coṟkaḷai nī ēṟṟuk koḷḷayeṉap paṇṭē yāṉ kūṟiṉēṉaṉṟō,” eṉṟu kūṟiṟṟu.
puḻu : ām. yāṉ kēṭpaṉavaṟṟil uṟutip poruḷaik kāṇṭal kūṭum eṉṉum nōkkattuṭaṉ ēṟṟuk koḷva
leṉpataip paṇṭu kūṟiyatē yaṉṟi, iṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟēṉ. iṉi eṉṟum kūṟuvēṉ. uraippār uraippaṉa vaṟṟai yuraitta vāṟṟāṉuṭkoḷḷumiyalpiṉai yuṭai yārkku, avar uṇmai kalantavaṟait tāmē yuraittal vēṇṭum. oru vāṟṟāṉu miyaipillāta voṉṟai yiyaittukkūṟiṉ yāvar tām ēṟpar. nilaiyāta vaṟṟai nilaiyiṉa veṉṟuṇarum pullaṟivāṇmai kaṭaiyātal pōla, iyaipillaṉavaṟṟai yiyaipuṭaiyavākkik kūṟutalum, kūṟakkēṭṭalum pullaṟivāṇmai yāta lōṭu kaṭai
yātalum tōṉṟiṟṟaṉṟē. naṇpa! cēṭap pūccikaḷiṉ ciṉai poriyiṉ kīṭaṅkaḷāka māṟu meṉpatum, kīṭaṅkaḷun tam iyaṅkutaṟ ṟaṉmai nīṅkip paṟattaṟṟaṉmaiyiṉai yeytu meṉpatum muṟṟilum poruntāk kūṟṟukkaḷē. ivaṟṟōraṉṉa puṉmoḻi kaḷai nī yāṇṭu, yāṅṅaṉam, kēṭṭaṟkut tuṇintaṉai? ivai yollumō veṉpatai nīyē āytu nōkkuka. aṅṅaṉamātal oru kālum muṭiyātaṉṟō!
paruntu : iṉi vēṟu kūṟal eṉakku iyalātu. vēṇṭumēl yāṉ aṟintavaṟṟaik kūṟuval; kēḷ; yāṉ vayal vaḻiyum, vāṉāṟum paṭarntu koṭpuṟum pāṉmaiyēṉ. aṅṅaṉam, paṭaruṅkāl, iṭaiyil nikaḻum nikaḻccikaḷaik kāṇātu celvatu illai. avaṟṟuḷ ovvoṉṟum niṉaivuvallār neñcum kaṭantu niṟkuntaṉmaittu. avaṟṟaik kaṇṇuṟṟut teḷinta yāṉ ivvāṟu nikaḻtaluṅ kūṭumeṉat tuṇintēṉ. ōr kuṟukiya ellaikkaṇiyakkamum, tāṉvāḻum ilai yaṉṟip piṟa
kaṇṭaṟiyāmaiyumuṭaiya niṉakkup piṟa vaṉaittum ariyavum, iyaipillavum, muṭiyātavumākavē tōṉṟum, kiṇaṟṟuttavaḷai puṇariyiyalpai yaṟiyuṅkol!
puḻu : antō! āyntiṭu muṇarvutaṉṉai niṉakku avvayaṉ paṭaittilaṉ kol! avaṉaik kāṇpaṉēl, aṟṟatalai pōka, aṟātatalai nāṉkiṉaiyum paṟṟittirukip paṟippēṉ! aṟitiyō!! ikkūṟṟu ittuṇaiyuṟutiyuṭaittu; uṇmai yuṭaittu eṉpaṉa
vaṟṟaiyumō yāṉ aṟiyēṉ! ā!! ittuṇaikkālam ivvulakiṭaip piṟantuḻaṉṟa yāṉ ītaṟiyātu oḻivalō? eṉṉē niṉmaṭamai! eṉ paciya neṭiya vuṭalaiyum, eṇṇiṟanta kāl
kaḷaiyum nōkkum ivaṟṟai nēriṟ kaṇṭum, eṉakku ivvuṭaṉīṅku meṉpatum, palvakai vaṇṇamamainta puttuṭaloṉṟu eytu meṉpatum ettuṇai iḻittakka moḻikaḷā kiṉṟaṉa. iḵtaṟiyāmaiyē pētamai yeṉpatu. kūṟuvōrkku uṇarvu kuṉṟiṉum, kēṭpōrkkum atu kuṉṟuṅ kolō?
paruntu : ciṉantu) eṉṉai kūṟiṉai? “yākāvā rāyiṉum nākākka, kāvākkāṟ cōkāppar colliḻukkup paṭṭu” eṉṉum poyyil moḻiyiṉai maṟattiyō? eṉṉai nī matiyili yeṉpatu uṇmai. kallā eṟivil kayavarpāṟ kaṟṟuṇarnta nallār tamatu kaṉam naṇṇutaluṇṭukol! itaṉaic ciṟitēyu maṟintiruntum, yāṉ ivvuṇmaiyaik kūṟiya vantaṉaṉaṉṟē! niṉ aṟivuk
keṭṭātavaṟṟai vaṟpuṟuttik kūṟaniṉaittalum, atu paṟṟi niṉṉuḻai yeytalum eṉṉilaikku ēṟpaṉavalla vaṉṟō! vāṉōkki yeḻuṅkāl eṉvāyvaḻi varum iṉṉoli kēṭṭōrkku iṉpam payantu, eṉakku matuvaḻiyēyōr ciṟappiṉaiyum nalkutal orutalai. itu kiṭakka, nī iṉiyēṉum varuvaṉa vaṟṟaiyum, piṟar kūṟuvaṉavaṟṟaiyum oṉṟiya vuḷḷamoṭu ēṟṟukkoḷka.
ivvāṟu, puḻuviṉ puṉmoḻikaḷ palavum paruntiṉ ceviyiṟ pukkum atu vekuḷātu, nāṇātu, aṉpuṭai naṉmoḻi kūṟalum, puḻu taṉ aṟiyāmaikkum, ataṉāṟ ciṉantu pitaṟṟiya moḻikaṭkum uḷam veḷki, “ataṟku nī kūṟum annampikkaiyum uṟuti nōkkum eṉ māṭṭila veṇpāyō? eṉṟatu.
paruntu : uḷavēl, ittuṇai neṭumoḻikaḷ piṟakkuṅ kollō!
puḻu : aṟṟēl, avvuṟuti nōkku eṉṉuḻai yuṇṭātaṟku yāṉ ceyyakkaṭavateṉ? aṉpu kūrntu kūṟuka.
iṅṅaṉam amaiticāṉṟa vuraiyāṭal iṭainikaḻuṅ
kālattup paruntu cēṭpulam paṭarntatu. puḻuvum taṉṉaip paṉmuṟaiyum nōkki, acciṉaiyirunta murukkilaiyaic cūḻvantu koṇṭiruppa, avvilaiyait tuḷaittukkoṇṭu ēḻeṭṭup paccilaip puḻukkaḷ aṅkumiṅku miyaṅkat toṭaṅkiṉa. avai yāṇṭiruntu pōntaṉa? avai acciṉaikkaṇiṉṟu pōntaṉavē! pōtarakkaṇṭa puḻuviṉ puntiyiṟ kalakkamum māṉamuṅkalantu tōṉṟiṉa: kaṇṇakaṉ ñālam koṭpuṟal ceytatu; uṭal cumantu niṟkum taṉṉaiyē māyamō veṉṟeṇṇiṟṟu. paruntu kūṟiya
moḻi yovvoṉṟum ataṉ uḷattut tōṉṟiṟṟu. iṉṉaṇam paruntiṉ mutalurai yuṇmaiyātal kāṇṭaliṉ, maṟṟatum avvāṟāl kūṭumeṉum tuṇipum uṭaṉeytiya eytalum, atu paṭṭapāṭu yārāṟ kūṟappaṭun takaittām!!
piṉṉar nāṭkaḷ cila ceṉṟaṉa; parantaviv vulakiṟṟōṉṟum poruḷiṉāṉām kāṭciyaṟivaip paruntu uṭaṉuṭaṉ puḻuviṟ kuraittalum, uṇmaiyāytalum iraṇṭiṟkum marapāyiṟṟu. puḻuvum taṉṉiṉaṅkaṭkut taṉakku nēravirukkum nikaḻcciyaiyum, atuvē yavaṟṟiṟkumā meṉpataiyum aṟivuṟuttum; uṟuttiṉum, ataṉaiyavai ēlātē yoḻintaṉa. puḻumaṭṭiṟ ṟāṉṟaṉ meyn
naṭpukkuriya  paruntuḻaik kaṇṭa meypporuḷ nikaḻtaṟ kēṟṟa kālam eytiṟṟāka, pakalcey maṇṭilam paṉikkaṭal mukaṭṭeḻa, paṟavai yiṉṉicaipāṭa, curumpuntēṉum cūḻntārppa, kaṭikkamalam vāyviḷḷa, pūcciyiṉam poliveyta, puḻuviṉaṅkaḷ kuḻīi maṟuka, ippuḻuvum pūcciyāyiṟṟu; yākkaiyum kāloṭumitakkum kaviṉeytiṟṟu; iyaṅkiyavuṭal paṟattal ceytatu; irumaruṅkum ciṟakukaḷeḻuntaṉa; avaṟṟiṉkaṭ palvakaiya voḷiteṟikkum vaṇṇaṅkaḷ tōṉṟi yaḻaku ceytaṉa.
ivvaṇṇam aḻakutikaḻap puttuṭal peṟṟup pūntēṉuṇṭu polivukūr cuṟṟamoṭu purintu vāḻnta cēṭap pūcci, piṉṉarc ciṉṉāṭkaḷuṭ ṭaṉ ṉiṉṉuyir nīṅkuṅ kāla meytalum, eṇṇaru miṉpaṅkaṉiya, “im maṟuviṉṟu viḷaṅkum māyiru
ñālattu, aṟivu vaḷarkkumāṟṟā ṉamainta poruḷkaḷ mikap
palavuḷa; avaṟṟai yāyntu kōṭalaṟiviṉ kaṭaṉ: ataṟku iṉṟiyamaiyāta vuṟuppāvatu uṟutinōkkē (kuyavaiā). ataṉai ataṉaruḷālē yaṭaiyappeṟṟēṉ. yāṉ peṟṟa iṉpam peṟuka ivvaiyakam!! iṉi evai variṉum varuka. yāṉ viṇṇakam paṭarkuval” eṉṟeḻuntu ceṉṟu viṇṇil maṟaintatu.
(karantait tamiḻc caṅkak kallūriyiṉiṉṟu veḷivanta pañcāpakēcaṉ eṉṉum kaiyeḻuttut tiṅkaḷ veḷiyīṭṭiṉkaṇ muṉṉar veḷivantatōr moḻipeyarppu. āṅkila nūṟ kaṭṭuraikaḷuḷ amiḻntu tiḷaitta nilaiyil ikkaṭṭurai āṅkilam taḻuviya tamiḻākkamāka amaintatu)

2. uḻaippu
"	viṉaiyē āṭavark kuyirē vāṇutal
	maṉaiyuṟai makaḷirkku āṭavar uyireṉa
	namakkurait tōrum tāmē
	aḻāaltōḻi avar aḻuṅkuvar celavē..."
- kuṟuntokai : 135
naṟkuṇamum naṟceykaiyum ciṟanta naṅkaiyorutti, aṟivum āṇmaiyum perukiya tōṉṟal oruvaṉai maṇantu illaṟam pūṇṭu vantaṉaḷ. orunāḷ avaṉ, tāṉ ceyvataṟ kuriya viṉaiyoṉṟu kuṟittut taṉ maṉaiyiṉ nīṅkic cella vēṇṭiyavaṉāṉāṉ. maṇantanāḷ toṭṭut taṉ kaṇavaṉaip pirintaṟiyāta annaṅkaikku itu teriyiṉ, avaḷ peruvaruttam eytuvaḷ eṉpatai attōṉṟal eṇṇiṉaṉ. uṇmaik kātalvaḻi yoḻukuvārkkup pirivupōlap peruntuṉpam taruvatu piṟitu kiṭaiyātu.  "iṉṉātu iṉaṉillūr vāḻtal, ataṉiṉum iṉṉātu iṉiyārp pirivu" eṉṟu tiruvaḷḷuvarum kūṟiyuḷḷār. ataṉāl taṉ karuttiṉai, avaṉ, avaḷatu tōḻikku uraittu, piṉṉar mellat taṉ maṉaiyāṭkut terivikkumāṟu paṇittāṉ. avaḷum ataṟku oruvāṟu uṭaṉpaṭṭu, kālam vāytta pōtu avaṉatu karuttiṉaip paiya vuṇarttiṉāḷ. kēṭṭāḷō illaiyō, naṅkaikkup peruntuyar uṇṭākiviṭṭatu; kaṇṇīr viṭṭuk kataṟiyaḻat toṭaṅkiṉāḷ. avaḷai āṟṟuvikkat toṭaṅkiya tōḻi,  "naṅkāy, kaṇavaṉ collaik kāppatu maṉaivikkuk kaṭaṉ. avar orunāḷ coṉṉatu oṉṟuṇṭu. atu niṉakkum terintatē. aḵtāvatu: 'āṭavarkkut tāṅkaḷ ceyyum viṉaiyē uyir; maṉaiyuṟaiyum makaḷirkku avvāṭavarē uyir. avarkku uyirākiya viṉaimēṟ ceṉṟāltāṉē, avar niṉakku uyirāka iruttalkūṭum; nī aḻutāl avar viṉaimēṟ
celvataik kaiviṭuvarē. ataṉāl ētamaṉṟō viḷaiyum," eṉṟaṉaḷ. atu kēṭṭa annaṅkaiyum tēṟi avaṉ pirivai āṟṟit taṉakkuriya aṟattiṉaiyum āṟṟivantaṉaḷ.
icciṟiya pāṭṭiṉaip pāṭiyavar pālaipāṭiya peruṅkaṭuṅkō eṉṉum cāṉṟōrāvar.
ivvāṟu viṉaimēṟcella niṉainta talaimakaṉ orunāḷ mālaippōtu varakkaṇṭu,  "ā, ippōtu nam kātali nam maṉaiyakattu viḷakkēṟṟi ataṉ muṉ niṉṟu, 'iṉṉum kātalar vantilarē' eṉṟu niṉaikkum pōtu aṉṟō?" eṉṟu taṉakkuḷ niṉaittāṉ. niṉaittavaṉ, immālai,
"uḷḷiṉēṉ allaṉō yāṉē, uḷḷiya
	viṉaimuṭit taṉṉa iṉiyōḷ
	maṉaimāṇ cuṭaroṭu paṭarpoḻu teṉavē."
- naṟṟiṇai : 3
eṉat taṉakkuḷ niṉaintu iṉaintāṉ eṉa iḷaṅkīraṉār kūṟiyuḷḷār. ivviru ceyyuṭkaḷaiyum nōkkuvōmāyiṉ, namakku ōr uṇmai pulappaṭum. aḵtāvatu, ōr āṇ makaṉukku avaṉ ceytaṟkuriya viṉaitāṉ uyir; ataṉai muṭittavaḻi yuḷatākum iṉpam kātaliṉpam pōlvatu eṉpatām.
viṉaiyeṉpatu nām ceyyum ceykaiyākum. ovvōruyirum ulakil tōṉṟumpōtē viṉai ceyvataṟkeṉṟē piṟakkiṉṟatu. viṉaiyē oruvaṉ eytum iṉpattukkum tuṉpattukkum kāraṇamāvatu. iṉpattaiyē evvuyirum peṟa virumpum; tuṉpattai virumpum uyir etuvu millai. tuṉpam eṉṟa collaik kēṭkumpōtē nam uḷḷam cuḷikkum; collutaṟkum nākkuk kūcum. iṉpattait tarum viṉaiyē yāvarum virumpum toḻilākum. ataṉāltāṉ iṉpam tarakkūṭiyataṉai nalviṉai, naṟceykai eṉṟum, tuṉpam taruvataṉait tīviṉai, tīcceykai eṉṟum collukiṉṟōm.
iṉi ivviṉaiyaic ceyyumpoḻutu nam uṭalum uyirum peritum pāṭupaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivviraṇṭum oruṅkē kūṭic ceyyātavaḻi, entat toḻilum naṭaipeṟuvatu illai. ettoḻilaic ceyvatāyiṉum ivai uḻaikka vēṇṭiyiruk
kiṉṟaṉa. uyir uṭaloṭu kūṭiyiruppatu ivvuḻaippukkē; ivvuḻaippāl, uyir uṭalpaṭum tuṉpamaṉaittaiyum tāṉ paṭukiṟateṉṟālum, uyirkku uṭaliṉmēl uḷḷa paṟṟiṉāl uḻaikka uḻaikka, ataṟku ataṉmēl paṟṟu mikukiṉṟatu; "tuṉpam uḻattoṟum kātaṟṟu uyir" eṉat tiruvaḷḷuvar kūṟukiṉṟār. eṉavē, uṭalōṭu kūṭi vāḻum ovvōruyirkkum uḻaippu iyalpāka amaintiruppatai aṟikiṟōm. āṉāl, ivvuyirkaḷuḷ makkaḷuyirmaṭṭil, ēṉai uyirkaḷaiviṭac ciṟitu mēmpaṭṭatākum. taṉ uḻaippāl āṇṭavaṉ paṭaippil uḷḷa ēṉai uyirkaḷaiyum uyiril poruḷ kaḷaiyum muṟaiyē aṭimaiyākavum uṭaimai yākavum ākkik koḷḷaṟku vēṇṭiya urimai makkaḷuyir peṟṟirukkiṟatu. ātalāṟṟāṉ iṉṟu maṉitaṉ nilam, nīr, tī, kāṟṟu, vicumpu ākiya pūtam aintum tāṉ virumpumāṟu amaiyac ceykiṉṟāṉ. nilattiṟkuḷ nuḻaintu kuṭaintu celkiṉṟāṉ; nīriṉ mēlum kīḻum nimirntu celkiṉṟāṉ; kāṟṟai ēval koḷḷukiṉṟāṉ; vicumpil paṟantēku kiṉṟāṉ. curuṅkac coṉṉāl, maṉitaṉ āṇṭavaṉukku aṭuttapaṭiyāka viḷaṅkukiṉṟāṉ eṉṉalām.
maṉitaṉ ittuṇai ēṟṟamuṭaiyavaṉāka iruttaṟkuriya urimai avaṉpāl nilaittirukka vēṇṭumāyiṉ, avaṉ calippiṉṟi uḻaittal vēṇṭum. uḻaippiṉāl avaṉ uṭal tūymaiyaṭaikiṟatu. ataṉāl avaṉ uḷḷam oḷipeṟukiṟatu; appōtu āṇṭavaṉ avaṟku aruḷiyuḷḷa urimai katirviṭṭut tikaḻkiṉṟatu. atuvaḻiyāka avaṉpāl uyariya eṇṇaṅkaḷum, ulavāta iṉpamum uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa. makkaḷuṭ cilar, avvurimaiyaik kāttuk koḷḷum valikuṉṟiyirukkiṉṟaṉar; urimaiyai nilaippikkum uḻaippiṉai nekiḻttuc cōmpaluṟu kiṉṟaṉar. cōmpal uṭalaik keṭukkiṉṟatu; ataṉāl uyir maṭaṅkukiṉṟatu; urimai nilavum uḷḷam maṭikiṉṟatu. ākavē, cōmpal maṭimaikku uyirai iraiyākki, uḷḷattē iruḷaiyum uyirkku iṟutiyaiyum viḷaivikkiṉṟatu. oru periyāratu uṭaṉpiṟantār iṟantupōṉār eṉpatu kēḷviyuṟṟu, vēṟoru periyār avarai nōkkit "taṅkaḷ tampi evvāṟu iṟantār?" eṉṟu kēṭṭataṟku, avar, "avaṉ uḻaittaṟku iṉṟi iṟantāṉ" eṉṟārām. ākavē, uḻaippiṉṟic cōmpiyiruppavaṉ, viraivil taṉ uyirai iḻakkiṉṟāṉ; avaṉ uḷḷattē, uyirkkut tīṅku ceyyum tīya eṇṇaṅkaḷum aṟiyāmaiyumē kuṭikoḷḷum eṉa aṟiyalām.
iṉṟu nām aḻakiya nakaraṅkaḷaiyum, periya periya māṭa māḷikaikaḷaiyum, pacum kampaḷam virittatupōlat tōṉṟum paranta paṇpaṭṭa vayalkaḷaiyum kāṇkiṉṟōm. kāṭaḻintu nāṭākiyirukkiṟatu; karuṅkaṭalil peruṅkalam celkiṉṟatu;  kampiyiṉṟiyē tanti pēcukiṟatu; viraivil, "kaṇṭēṉ avar tiruppātam, kaṇṭaṟiyātaṉa kaṇṭēṉ" eṉa nam nāvaracar pāṭiyatai nāmum pāṭappōkiṉṟōm. ivaiyāvum uḻaippiṉ payaṉaṉṟō! "uḻaippiṉ vārā uṟutikaḷ uḷavō?" eṉap paṭṭiṉattaṭikaḷ uraittatu uṇmai moḻiyaṉṟō! uḻaippiṉṟi uṭal cōmpi, uyir ōyntu oṭuṅkiyirukkum oruvaṉaip pāruṅkaḷ! avaṉ uḷḷattil oḷiyillai. ataṉai avalam, kavalai, kaiyāṟu, aḻuṅkal, ācai, vekuḷi, aḻukkāṟu mutaliyaṉa cūḻntu arikkiṉṟaṉa; avaṉum urukkulaintu melikiṉṟāṉ. avaṉai ētēṉum oru toḻilaicceyya viṭuvōm: uṭaṉē, avaṉatu uṭalum uyirum oruppaṭṭu attoḻiṟkaṇ uḻaikkat toṭaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. avaṉ vaṉmai muḻuvatum attoḻilai muṭittaṟkaṇ oṉṟiviṭukiṉṟatu. avaṉ uḷḷattai yalaitta aḻukkuṇarvukaḷaṉaittum akalappōy eṅkēyō maṟaintu pōkiṉṟaṉa. avaṉ maṉitaṉ, maṉitaṉāka vayaṅkukiṉṟāṉ. avaṉatu uḷḷam pēroḷi viṭṭu miḷirkiṉṟatu; avvaḻuk
kuṇarvukaḷ avvoḷiyiṉkaṇ pukaintu erintu puttoḷi ceykiṉṟaṉa. ākavē, oruvaṉatu uḷḷattait tūymai ceyvataṟku uḻaippē ciṟanta maruntāka irukkiṟateṉpatu eḷitil viḷaṅkukiṟatu.
nām vāḻum immaṇṇulaku ātiyil ñāyiṟṟiliruntu pōntatu eṉṟum, aṉṟumutal iṉṟu varaiyil cuḻaṉṟu koṇṭēyirukkiṟatu eṉṟum nām aṟintirukkiṉṟōm. itaṉai ivvāṟu cuḻalvitta paramaṉ karuttu yātākal vēṇṭum? iṭaiyaṟātu cuḻalum cuḻaṟciyiṉāl nām vāḻum maṇṇulaku, viṇṇulakil vaṭṭamāka iyaṉṟu, pala vikāra uruvaṅkaḷaip peṟṟu, muṭivil, iṟṟaiya uruṇṭai vaṭivattaip peṟṟirukkiṟatu. cuḻalalāvatu niṉṟupōmāyiṉ, innilavulakam yātāy muṭiyum? orucār pakalum maṟṟaiya pakuti iruḷumāy evvuyirum vāḻtaṟkiṉṟi, epporuḷum nilaipeṟuvatiṉṟik keṭṭaḻiyu meṉpataṉaic collavum vēṇṭumō! aḷittakka immaṇṇakam, iṭaiyaṟa viṉṟic cuḻaṉṟuḻalum uḻaippiṉāltāṉ itaṉpāl muṟai tirumputalum, nērmai kōṭutalum piṟa tīmaikaḷum ilavāyiṉa; vikāraṅkaḷ nīṅkic cīriya ivvuruvamum amaivatāyiṟṟu.
kulālaṉatuḻaippum, avaṉatu taṇṭa cakkaramum maṉitaṉ uṇavaṭṭu uṇṇattoṭaṅkiya nāṇmutal aṟintaṉavākum. camaya nūlkaḷum ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷum, avaṉaiyē avaṉ ceyyum viṉaiyaiyum, viṉaikkuriya karuvi karaṇaṅkaḷaiyum aṭikkaṭi eṭuttukkāṭṭit tam aṟivup poruḷai vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. accakkarattiṉ naṭuvil vaikkappaṭum maṇtiraḷaip pāruṅkaḷ! ataṉiṭattil ētēṉum pāṇṭavamaippukkuriya vaṭivamuḷatō? aḻakiya uruvamum tiruntiya vaṭivamumuṭaiya maṭkalaṅkaḷ accakkarattiṉ cuḻaṟciyāl piṟakkiṉṟaṉa. aḵtu illaiyāyiṉ akkulālaṉ maṭkalaṉkaḷai yākkutal kūṭumō? ettuṇait toḻiṟciṟappum, ulaiyāvuḻaippu muṭaiyaṉāyiṉum avaṉ yātuceyya muṭiyum? avaṉ ceyya muyalum kalaṅkaṭku aḻakum vaṭivum amaiyuṅkol! iyaṟkaiyulakum, cakkarattuṇaiyillāta kulālaṉaippōla, ōyntu oṭuṅki niṟkumāyiṉ ataṉ payaṉ eṉṉāy muṭiyum! avvāṟu ceyyātu calippiṉṟi, uḻaikkumāṟu aruḷiya āṇṭavaṉai eppōtum vaṇaṅkuvōmāka. uḻaitta liṉṟic cōmpik kiṭakkum oruvaṉ ettuṇaip poruṇalaṅkaḷ uṭaiyaṉāyiṉum, avaṟku avai oru payaṉaiyum ceyyā. cakaṭacakkaramiṉṟik kaiyaṟṟu niṟkum kulālaṉ
pāl uḷḷa maṇtiraḷ pōla, uḻaippuc ciṟappillāta oruvaṉum veṟuntacaip potiyum ciṟappil piṇṭamumāvatallatu piṟitillai. kulālaṉai makaṉākavum, avaṉatu cakkarattai uḻaippākavum koṇṭu nōkkuka. uḻaippu uyarvu tarum; uḻaippāḷi ulakāḷvāṉ. ākavē, uḻaippup peṟṟavaṉ uṟutipeṟṟavaṉā
kiṉṟāṉ. uyir vāḻkkaiyiṉ uṟutippayaṉ avaṉ pālēyuḷatu. uyarvu vēṇṭuvōṉ uḻaippu vēṇṭuka. karuvaraivīḻ aruvikaḷum kāṉyāṟum kaikalantu kalittōṭukiṉṟa. iṭaippaṭṭa māṇporuḷai varaṉṟiyēkum uḻaippu vaṉmaiyāl avai kalittuc cerukkukiṉṟaṉa. aḻukkum, mācum, kuppaiyum kūḷamum avaṟṟiṉ neṟiyiṟ paṭṭu alaittukoṇ ṭēkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. kāl kālāy vayaliṭaippaṭintu uramākit tām paravum nilap parappaip pacum pullum, naṟumalarum kaṇkavar vaṉappumuṭaiya vaṇpoḻilākkum ciṟṟāṟukaḷiṉ māṇṭoḻilai eṉṉeṉpatu! avaṟṟiṉ teṇṇīr uṇṇīrākiṟatu. atu cumantu koṇarnta aḻukkukaḷum avaṟṟōraṉṉa mācukaḷum māyntu pōkiṉṟaṉa. itaṉālaṉṟō, "uṇaveṉap paṭuvatu nilattoṭu nīrē; nīrum nilaṉum puṇariyōr, īṇṭu, uṭampum uyirum paṭaittici ṉōrē" eṉak kuṭapulaviyaṉārum pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭuñ ceḻiyaṟkup pāṭiyaṟivuṟuttiṉār. ittakaiya nīr yātē uḻaippeṉa vaṟika.
palatiṟamāy, palvakaip poruḷkaḷaiyākki, nāṭukaḷai nāṭā vaḷamuṭaiyavākac ceytu, makkaḷai iṉpamum, poruḷum, aṟamum, vīṭum iṉitu eytuvittu, avartam vāḻkkaiyiṉ perumpayaṉai nukarvippatu uḻaippēyākum. ākavē, uḻaippē uyir eṉa aṟikiṉṟōm. uyir uṭalōṭu kūṭi vāḻkkai naṭattuntōṟum, ataṉpāl pēriṉpam peṟutalpōla, uḻaippāḷiyum uḻaikkun tōṟum uvaṭṭāta pēriṉpamum ūkka mikutiyum peṟukiṉṟāṉ. āṇṭavaṉ tiruvaruḷ avaṉ uḷḷattē ūṟukiṟatu. uyar nōkkum, oḷḷaṟivum, perun taṉmaiyum  avaṉpāl uḷavāki yōṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
nōṉmai, tuṇivu, muyaṟci, aṟivup pēṟṟiṉkaṇ ārvam, taṉkuṟṟam tērntu kaṭital mutaliyaṉa iṉpa vāḻvukku ākkamākum nallaṟaṅkaḷ. uṇartta uṇarvatilum, ivaṟṟai nāmē nam uḻaippuvāyilāka uṇarntu naṉṉeṟikaḷiṟ celuttuvatē nam uyir vāḻkkaiyākum. uḻaippu vāyilāka ivaṟṟai nām uṇartal vēṇṭum. nōṉmai eṉpatu ettuṇai iṭukkaṇkaḷ aṭukkivariṉum  avaṟṟiṟku aḻiyātu, avaṟṟāl varum tuṉpattaip poṟuttu mēṟkoḷḷum taṉmaiyākum. aṅṅaṉam mēṟ koḷḷutaṟkuriya tuṇaiyākum. maṉattiṭpamē tuṇivu eṉpatu. tuṇintavaḻi, iṭaiyil vantutakaiyum iṭaiyūṟukaḷāl uḷḷamuṭaiyāmal, ceyviṉaiyaik kaṭaipōka uḻaittu veṟṟikāṇum tiṟam muyaṟciyākum ataṉkaṇ aṟiyātaṉavaṟṟai aṟintāṟṟum viraku vēṇṭiyiruttaliṉ, aṟivaṟiyum ārvam vēṇṭuvatāyiṟṟu. avvārvattāl, mutaṟkaṇ tāmē tērtalum piṟar kāṭṭak kāṇṭalum ākiya iru neṟikaḷāl tam kuṟṟattaik kaṇṭu ataṉaik kaṭivatum, ceyta kuṟṟattai iṉic ceyyā vakaiyil tammaip pātukāttu, aṭutta muṟaiyil naṉkāṟṟal vēṇṭum eṉṉum naṟpaṇpuṭaiyaṉāvatum uḻaippāḷikku iṉṟiyamaiyātaṉavākum. ip paṇpukaḷaik kaikkoṇṭu uḻaippōrmuṉ, vāṉṟōy malaiyaṉaiyapēriṭarkaḷ vantu niṟkum. "naṟceyaluk kaṉṟē nāṉūṟu iṭaiyūṟu." āyiṉum uṇmai yuḻaippāḷiyō, ēṉaiyavaṟṟāl varum "iṉpam viḻaiyāṉ; iṭumpai iyal peṉpāṉ; tuṉpam uṟutal ilaṉ." karumamē kaṇṇāka uḻaikkum avaṉ, "mey varuttam pārāṉ; paci nōkkaṉ; kaṇ tuñcāṉ; evvevar tīmaiyum mēṟkoḷḷāṉ; cevviyarumaiyum pārāṉ; avamatippum koḷḷāṉ." cuṭac cuṭarum poṉpōl, avaṉ uḷḷam tuṉpaṅkaḷ toṭarntu pōntu cuṭaccuṭa, oṭpamum tiṭpamum peṟṟu ōṅki yoḷirum. nilavulakiṉum, avaṉ pukaḻē ōṅkip poṉṟātu niṉṟu nilavum, attuṉpaṅkaḷum "eri muṉṉar vaittūṟu pōlak keṭum."
ilaṇṭaṉ nakarattil uḷḷa ceyiṉṭ pāl katīṭral eṉṉum peruṅkōyil ulaiyā vuḻaippiṉ uruvuṭaip payaṉākum. ataṉai yeṭuttaṟku muyaṉṟa car kiṟistapar reṉ eṉpār, kaṇitanūl, vāṉanūl, ciṟpanūl mutaliyavaṟṟiṟ perum pulamaiyum viṉaittiṭpamum uṭaiyar. āyiṉum, avar itaṉaik kaṭṭiyamaittaṟku muyaṉṟa kālattu uḷavākiya iṭaiyūṟukaṭku aḷavēyillai. urōm nakarattuc ceyiṉṭ pīṭṭars peruṅ kōyil pōlavē ikkōyilaiyum eṭuttal vēṇṭumeṉa eṇṇi muyaṉṟa avartam eṇṇam tūrntu pōmāṟu, araciyaṟ pēratikārikaḷum camayap paṇiyāḷarum piṟarum maṇṇaḷḷi eṟinta vaṇṇamē iruntaṉar; poruḷutavi purivōr iruḷil maṟaintaṉar; kiṟistaparukkum iṭukkaṇ pala aṭukki varalāyiṉa. āyiṉum eṉ? "maṭuttavāyellām pakaṭaṉṉāṉ uṟṟa iṭukkaṇ iṭarppāṭuṭaittu" eṉṟa tiruvaḷḷuvap perumoḻipōla, avviṭaiyūṟukaḷum iṭarppaṭṭut tāmē yaḻintaṉa. katīṭral kaṇkavar vaṉappum maṇmakiḻ tōṟṟamum peṟṟu iṉṟum niṉṟu nilavum peruñ ciṟappuṭaṉ tikaḻum cemmai yeytiṟṟu. iṉṟum ataṉaik kāṇpōr, "periyār" eṉap poṟittuḷḷa perum peyaraik kāṇuntōṟum, nam kiṟistapariṉ viṉaittiṭpam, muyaṟci mutaliya viṉaimāṇpukaḷai niṉaintu pārāṭṭa niṟkiṉṟaṉar.
tiruvum kalviyum ciṟantu viḷaṅkum nāṭukaḷuḷ ippōtu amerikka nāṭu ettuṟaiyilum īṭumeṭuppumiṉṟi viḷaṅku vataṉai nām aṟikiṉṟōm. itaṉ uṇmaiyiṉaiyuṇarntu kaṇṭu uraitta kolampas eṉṉum periyārai mēṉāṭṭavar aṉaivarum, nam nāṭṭavaruṭ kaṟṟavarum naṉkaṟivar. nam nāṭṭiṟkup putuneṟi yoṉṟu kāṇappukunta apperiyār avvamerikka nāṭṭiṉaik kaṇṭaṟintaṉarāyiṉum, ataṉai mutaliṟ kāṇṭaṟ kaṇ avar uṟṟa iṭukkaṇ uraikkun taramuṭaiyataṉṟu. putuneṟi kāṇṭal kūṭumeṉṟa eṇṇam avar uḷḷattiṟ pukunta nāḷmutal, putupperu nāṭākiya ataṉait tam kaṇkaḷāṟ kāṇun tuṇaiyum, avarai, avamatippu, accuṟuttal, aṟiyāmai, ikaḻcci, ēmāṟṟam, vacaivu, kolai, accam mutaliya pala ceyalkaḷ pallāṟṟālum arittu alaittaṉa. nāṭṭiṉ kāṭciyai yeytutaṟku iraṇṭoru nāḻikai yirukkumpōtum, avar ceṉṟa vaṅkattiliruntōr avaraip paṟṟik kolaiceytu viṭavum tuṇintaṉar: malaipōl alaiyeḻuntu muḻaṅkum maṟikaṭal, muṉṉē kiṭantu, maruṭci viḷaivittatu; piṉṉē avaratu nāṭu neṭuntolaiviṟ kiṭantu, vaṟitē tirumpiṉ avarai ikaḻnteḷḷi iṉṉaṟ paṭuttaṟku etirnōkkiyiruntatu; arukil cūḻa viruntōr maṭitta vāyum, veṭitta collum, kaṭutta nōkkum uṭaiyavarāyt tīviṉaiyē cūḻntu koṇṭiruntaṉar. kaṇantōṟum, nilaiyiṉṟip puraḷum alaikaḷ avartam kalattaic celavoṭṭātu taṭuttaṟ keḻuntaṉa pōla veḻuntu alaippa, avaṟṟiṭaiyē oṭuṅkā vuḷḷamum kalaṅkāk koḷkaiyu mallatu piṟitillāta avar maṉat tiṭpam, avvalaikaḷiṉ talaiyai mitittēkiyatu; virikaṭalaic curukkiṟṟu; neṭumpōtu kuṟumpōtāka, nīrkkāṟṟu nilakkāṟṟāka, pakal māya, irā vaṇuka, viṉai muṭiviṉ tuṉaivu mika, amerikkap putu nāṭṭiṉ aṭaikarai pulaṉāyiṟṟu, aṭart taṟkeḻunta alaikaḷ avarait talaimēṟ cumantu tālāṭṭiṉa; vaṉkāṟṟāy maṭittaṟku niṉṟa kaṭaṟkāṟṟu, meṉkāṟṟāy avar melivu tīrttu makiḻvittatu. ītaṉṟō viṉaimāṇpu! eṉṟum poṉṟāta iṉpavicai nalkum viṉaiyiṉum, uḻaippiṉum ēṟṟamuṭaiya toṉṟu uṇṭukol! illai! illai! eññāṉṟum illai!!
iṉi ivvuḻaippiṉai nam paṇṭaic cāṉṟōr āḷviṉaiyeṉṟē vaḻaṅki yirukkiṉṟaṉar. uḷḷattiṉ āṭci vaḻi niṟkum uṭal puriyum viṉai uḻaippātalāl, ataṉai āḷviṉai yeṉṟatu ettuṇai aḻakuṭaittu! kāṇmiṉ. avvāḷviṉaiyē āṭavarkku uyir eṉpataṉāl, āṭavaṉai āṇmakaṉ eṉṟal ettuṇai aṟivuṭai moḻi! kāṇmiṉ āṇmakkaḷē! uṅkaḷatu āṇmai, āḷviṉaiyē, ayarā uḻaippē, ataṉaiyuṭaiya nīvir eṉ ceykiṉṟīrkaḷ? ayarntu uṟaṅkukiṉṟīrkol? uṟaṅkaṉmiṉ; uṟaṅkutal uyvaṟṟārkkē uriyatu, ōyvu ulaiyā uḻaippuṭaiyārkkē uriyatu. uḻaippavar ōyvu kōṭaṟkuriyar, ōyvuṟṟavar uṟaṅkutaṟkuriyar; uṟaṅkuvōr mīṭṭum uḻaittaṟku uṭaṟkum uḷḷattiṟkum urantarupavarāvār. uṅkaḷatu uṟakkam mīṭṭum uḻaittal vēṇṭi uṅkaṭku ūkkam ceytaṟkamaintatu; ataṟkuriyapōtu iravuppōtē, ākavē, pakaṟpōtu uḻaippuk kuriyatākum. uḻaippukkuriya appakaṟpōtu uṟaṅkutaṟkuriya taṉṟu; ataṉāl pakaṟpōtil naṉku uḻaimiṉ.
ulakam virintu kiṭakkiṉṟatu; itaṉpāl uyariya payaṉ uḷatu; uḻaippavar appayaṉai nukartaṟkuriyar; ulakaiyaḷitta oruvaṉ uṅkaṭku uṭampu nalkiyuḷaṉ; avvuṭampiṉai ulakil iyakki uṟupayaṉ peṟutaṟkuriya uṟukaruviyāka uṅkaṭku, avaṉ uḷḷattaiyum utaviyuḷaṉ, uḷḷattāl uḷkiyavaḻi uḻaittaṟ kurimaiyum, āṟṟalum uṅkaḷpāl uḷa eṉpatu viḷaṅkum. avaṟṟai veḷip paṭuttal uṅkaḷ kaṭaṉ. āṟṟalum urimaiyu muṭaiya nīvir uḻaikkumiṭattu, eṅkeṅku muṟai piṟaḻcci tōṉṟu kiṉṟatō, aḵtu uṅkaḷ uyirkkiṟuti payakkum uṟupakai yātaliṉ, aṅkaṅku muṟaimaiyai niṟuvutal vēṇṭum; nel viḷaiyum vayaliṭattup pul muḷaiya viṭuvirkol? turum pāyiṉum virumpi yīṭṭumiṉ. paruttiyiṉ pañcu kāṟṟiṟ paṟakkum puṉmaittāyiṉum viṭātu paṟṟumiṉ; īṭṭu miṉ; pāṅkuṟa nūṉmiṉ; aḵtu aṟṟam maṟaikkum āṭaiyākumē; "aṇiyellām āṭaiyiṉ piṉ."
uḻaippiṟku "ūṟu ceyvaṉa piṟavum cilavuḷa, avai aṟiyāmaiyum, maṭamaiyumākum. aṟitaṟkariya amaiti peṟṟum, aṟiyavēṇṭuvaṉavaṟṟai aṟiyaviṭātu, maruṭciyum accamum payappittuc ceyviṉai citaṟuvittaliṉ, aṟiyāmai uḻaippiṟku ūṟu ākum. aṟivuṭaiyōr aṟivuṟuppavum, āṟṟaṟkuriya viṉaiyum urimaiyum arukil iruppavum, āḷ viṉaikkaṇ karuttai iruttātu, maṭimaikkaṇ maṭintu kiṭappittaliṉ, maṭamai yiṉait takart teṟitalum āṇmakaṟku aṟamākum ataṉaic ceyyavēṇṭu meṉpatu āṇṭavaṉ āṇai. "iṉṟu," "ippoḻutu," "ikkaṇam" eṉṟu vaḻaṅkum kālamē, ataṉai ayarātu āṟṟaṟkamainta arumaik kālamākum ayarntavaḻi iravu vantu viṭum; akkālattil evarum uṟaṅkuvarē yaṉṟi uḻaittalaic ceyyār.
ellā vakai uḻaippum ēṟṟamuṭaiyavaṉākum; avaṟṟuḷ kaiyāl uḻaikkum uḻaippil kaṭavuṭṭaṉmaiyuṇṭu eṉak kārlail eṉṉum periyār kaviṉak kūṟukiṉṟār; ikkaṭṭuraik kaṇ aṭaṅkiya karuttukaḷai vaḻaṅkiya perum pulavarum avarē. uḻaippu ulakēpōl paranta paṇpiṉaiyuṭaiyatu; itaṉ uyarvu vīṭṭulakiṉ ucciyiluḷatu.. mutaṟkaṇuṭalilum, piṉ mūḷaiyilum, muṭivil uḷḷattilum oṉṟiyulavip payaṉ uṟuvikkum uḻaippē, kepḷar kaṇṭa kōṇilaik kaṇakkum, niyūṭṭaṉ nikaḻttiya neṭum poruḷārāycciyum, aṟiviyaṟkalaikaḷum, āṇmaic ceyalkaḷum iṉṉuyir vaḻaṅki icai naṭum iyalpum yāvum tōṟṟuvitta ēṟṟamuṭaiyatu. ātalāl, āṇ kaṭaṉ iṟukkum māṇpuṭaiya makkāḷ! uḻaippē āṇṭavaṉ uruveṉa vuṇarmiṉ. ataṟku aḻiyātu āṟṟalē avaṉatu tiruppaṇiyeṉa aṟimiṉ; paranta ulakil ciṟantōṅkiya perumakkaḷ kaṇṭuṇarn turaitta mutuporuḷum ituvēyām.
uḻaippiṉai veṟuttu nonturaikkum oruvaṉ uḷaṉō? uḷaṉāyiṉ, avaṉai atu ceyyavēṇṭāveṉa uraimiṉ. oṭuṅkikkiṭakkum avaṉ muṉ ceṉṟu, "aṉpa, oṭuṅkutal oḻika. itō, uṉpōl uḻaitta tōḻarkaḷ uyarnilai yulakattil iṉpañ ciṟantu iṉitē uṟaikiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷaṉṟō āṅkē iṉpuṟukiṉṟavar; piṟar evarum ilarē! in nilavukiṉum, uḻaippu nalattāl uyarnta varaṉṟō, makkaḷ maṉanilattil maṉṉi, vāṉavarum muṉivararum pōlak kālaṅkaḻiyiṉum kōlaṅ kaḻiyātu eṉṟum niṉṟu nilavukiṉṟaṉar," eṉat teruṭṭit teḷivuṟuttumiṉ.
					


3. varaṉeṉṉum vaippu
uṭampoṭu kalantu tōṉṟum uyirttokaiyuṭ ciṟappuṭaiyaveṉa uyarntōrāṉ uṇarttappeṟum makkaḷuyirkku uṟutipporuḷeṉap periyōr vakuttuk kūṟum nālvakaip poruḷkaḷuḷ vīṭeṉpatum oṉṟu. atu cintaiyum moḻiyum cellā nilaimaittu eṉap periyōr kūṟuvar. kūṟiṉum, atu ivvulakam pōl aḻitaṉmālaittāya iṉpat tiṉait tarātu, eññāṉṟum kuṉṟā viṉpam tarum pērulaka meṉavum, ataṉai nāṭiyē uyirkaḷ niṟṟal vēṇṭumeṉavum nam āṉṟōr kūṟuvataṉōṭu amaiyātu, avvulakanāṭṭam uyirkaṭku iyaṟkaiyilē amaintuḷa teṉṟum, atu, āyum uṇarvuṭaiyārkkē pulaṉāmeṉṟum kūṟiṉar; kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. akkūṟṟiṉai oruvāṟu ārāyntu kaṇṭa vaḻiyē, īṇṭuk kūṟappeṟukiṉṟatu.
puviyiṉukku aṇiyāy āṉṟa poruḷtantu, caviyuṟat teḷintu, taṇṇeṉṟu oḻukkamum taḻuvic cāṉṟōr kaviyeṉak kiṭanta kāṉyā ṟoṉṟiṉ aṭaikaraiyiṉkaṇ, veyiṉuḻai paṟiyāk kuyiṉuḻai potumpa roṉṟiruntatu. ataṉ kaṇ eṇṇiṟanta puḷḷiṉam, maṇṇuṟu vāḻkkaikku māṇpuṟuttum ceviccuvaiyamutam kēṭpōr cevippulam vaḷam peṟa aḷittu vāḻtara, avviṉattu ḷoṉṟāya iṉṉicaic *cempuḷoṉṟu peṭaiyuṭaṉ kiḷaimalka avvaṭaiciṉaip potumparuḷ, peṟṟatukoṇṭu, pēraṟam nērntu, peṟalarum iṉpamē tuyttu vantatu. ataṉāl, atu, eṅkeḻileṉ ñāyiṟu eṉṉum - iṟaivaṉaruḷā leytum - neñciṟumāppum, paḻiyañci yīṭṭum poruḷiṉpa muṭaimaiyiṉ, vaḻiyeñcā vaṉporuḷum uṭaiya tāyiṟṟu. ākavē, ataṟku iṉi vēṇṭaṟpāla teṉṉām? oṉṟumillai yaṉṟō!
niṟka, orunāḷ, ic cempuḷḷiṉ vāḻkkaittuṇai yāy, ottuṇarvum pāṉmaiyu muṭaiyatāy, tuṇaic cēvaṟku oruvāṟṟāṉu muyarvutāḻvillāk kaviṉuṅ kāṭciyu muṭaiyatāy viḷaṅkiya peṭaippuḷ, taṉ kūṭṭakat tamarntu, pārppukkaṭku uṇavāvaṉavaṟṟai yaḷittu uvantirukkaiyil, pakalceyyum ceññāyiṟu pāvaiyiṟ paṭiya, paiya iruḷ vantu parava, iṉpatteṇkāl iṭaivantulava, arukōṭiya aruvikka ṇeḻunta ciṟutirai potuvīḻtaliṉ eḻunta viḻumiya vōcai yāṅkaṇum parava, virikatirmatiyam viṇṇakat teḻuntu, maṇṇaka maṭantaiyiṉ mayakkoḻippatupōlat taṉ oṇkatir parappiṟṟu. atu kālai, ap pārppukkaḷu ḷoṉṟu, taṉ ciṟutalaiyai nīṭṭi, aṉpukaṉiyum āymuka nōkki, "aṉṉāy! vāḻi! nam paṭappait tēṉmayaṅku pāliṉum iṉiya it teṇṇīr yāṟu celliṭam yātukol" eṉa viṉava; kēṭṭa tāyppuḷ, viṭaiyaṟiyāmaiyiṉ, viḷampal kūṭātu, kuṟunakai kāṭṭi, "makkāḷ yāṉ! aṟiyēṉ; 'kaliṉ' eṉak kalikkum tūkkaṇaṅ kuruviyaiyātal, kaṭpulaṅkatuvā viṭpulam paṭarum vāṉampāṭiyai yātal kēṭṭal vēṇṭum. āṇṭut tōṉṟum kōṭṭeṅkiṉ kulaimītu avai orukāl vantamariṉum amarum. atu pōḻtu nīvir maṟavaṟka. nīvir kēṭṭatum, ataṉōraṉṉa piṟavum aṟitalām" eṉṟu kūṟi, vāṉampāṭiyiṉ vaḷḷicaiyait taṉ meṉkuralāl, vaṭittum untiyum uṟaḻntum icaippa, kurī ikkuñcukaḷ kuḷirppeyti, "vāṉampāṭikol ik kāṉam pāṭiyatu" eṉṟeṇṇalāyiṉa.
maṟṟu, av vāṉampāṭum vaṇkuruku, taṉ mellicai koṇṭu, kāṭum, kāvum, kaviṉpeṟu turuttiyum, yāṟum kuḷaṉum, vēṟupal vaippum, ūrum nakaramum uṟaitarum illamum, ūṟucey vilaṅkum, ūṉūṇ  vēṭṭuvar virikkum kaṇṇiyum, piṟavum meypeṟa vicaittal marapu. ataṉai yorukālēṉum arukamarntu naṉku kēṭṭaṟiyā ic cempuḷ, pāṭuṅkāl, av vicaipporuḷ muṟaiyiṟ ciṟitu piṟaḻtalum kuṟaitalumāyiṉa. iḻukkuṭaiya pāṭṭāyiṉum, icai naṉṟāyiṉmai koṇṭu pēruvakai mītūrat tāmum, tam aṉṉaiyoppa, mellicai miḻaṟṟal toṭaṅkiṉa. iṅṅaṉam kuravarāyiṉār ceyyum ceyviṉaiyai makkaḷāyiṉār, nuṇuki nōkki, ammuṟaiyē ceytal uyirttokaiyuṭ ciṟantu viḷaṅkum iyalpukaḷ palaviṉu ḷoṉṟu. ituvē, ēṉaiyaṟivu vaḷarccikkum piṟavaṟṟiṟkum orutalaiyāka vamainta aṭippaṭai yeṉpatu kalviyāḷar kōḷ. itaṉai yīṇṭuraippiṟ perukum.
veḷḷi muḷaittatu; viṭiyal vantatu; paṟavai pāṭiṉa; palluyirum paravukkaṭaṉ ceytaṉa; viḻunta ñāyiṟum eḻuntatu; meṉkāṟṟu vīcaluṟṟatu; pūnāṉkum potuḷi veṟi ticai nāṉkum pōyulava, karumpu tēṉārntaṉa. cēkkaiyuḷuṟaṅkik kiṭanta cempuṭkuñcukaḷ, uṟakka nīṅki, orupuṭai vantu, uvakai kūrntaṉa. atupōtu, tāyppuḷ, taṉ celvac ciṟu kuñcukaḷiṉ maruṅka marntu, taṉ mellicaiyait toṭuttu, yāṟṟunīrccelaviṉmēṟ pāṭiṟṟu. akkālai karaiyāk kallum pullum kaṉinturukiṉa. curuṅkakkūṟiṉ, avvicai yiṉpattāl, 
"	maruviyakāl vicaittacaiyā; maraṅkaḷmalarcciṉaicaliyā;
	karuvaraivīḻ aruvikaḷum kāṉyāṟum kalittōṭā;
	perumukiliṉ kulaṅkaḷpuṭai peyarntoḻiyappuṉalcōrā;
	iruvicumpiṉ iṭaimuḻaṅka; eḻukaṭalum iṭaituḷumpā."
eṉun tīṅkaviyē amaiyum.
iṅṅaṉam ciṟantōṅkiya icaiyoṉṟil, ap puḷ vīṟuṟṟatē yoḻiya, ataṉuḷattil, ciṟukuñcu viṉaviya poruḷmāttiram pulaṉāyiṟṟaṉṟu. āyiṉum tāṉum taṉpārppukkaḷum orupakal avvāṟu celliṭam cēṟalvēṇṭu meṉṉum tippiyavuṇar voṉṟu ataṉ neñcat takalātu nilavuvatāyiṟṟu. ivvāṟu nāṭkaḷ pala ceṉṟaṉa. appuḷḷum yāṟṟunīrccelavu poruḷākap pāṭi vantamaiyai nīkkip piṟitōr iṭamoṉṟaṉai nāṭip pāṭutal toṭaṅkiṟṟu. akkālai avviṭamē, ivvāṟu celliṭamām eṉpatu ataṟkut tuṇipāyiṟṟu. ivviṭattaik kēṭkuntoṟum ap pārppukkaḷum tam tāyppaṟavaiyai, ataṉaik kuṟitta viṉākkaḷ pala nikaḻttātu oḻivatiṉṟu.
niṟka. ivviṭam yātāka virukkalām? cilar itaṉai nām yāṇṭiruntu pōntōmō āṇṭaiyatu eṉpar. vēṟu cilar nām iṉic cellumiṭam yātu atu veṉpar. cilar, ittakaiya iṭamē illai yeṉpar. vēṟupalar nāmum piṟayum nīṅkiyatum; nīkkamaṟa iṉic ceruvatu māya iṭam eṉpar. kāṭci yoṉṟaiyē aḷavaiyāk koḷvār tuṇipu. maṟṟu, nām kāṇṭal, karutal, urai yeṉṉu mūṉṟaiyum aḷavaiyāk koḷḷum pāṉmaiyē mākalāṉum, avaṟṟā ṉārāyuṅ kālattu, uḷattiṟ ṟōṉṟum cila vuṇarvukaḷum, uṇarntōr palar kūṟiya uraikaḷum appeṟṟit tāya iṭamoṉṟuṇṭeṉpataṉai valiyuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa. āyiṉum, ataṉai oruvāṟu nām kāṇṭaṟkumuṉ aṟiyāvulakam eṉa vaḻaṅkuvōm. *mutaṉūlārum ivvāṟē vaḻaṅkukiṉṟār. aṉṟiyum, ivvulakaip paṟṟi, iruṇṭa vuṇarvukaḷē appaṟavaiyiṉ uṇarvuṭ kalantu kiṭantaṉa.
iṉi, at tāyppuḷ, tāṉē taṉittamarntu, avvulaku paṟṟiya vuṇarvukaḷ taṉ maṉatteḻuntōṟum, icaittuk kuḷirntu nāvil ūṟāta amiḻtūṟa. uṭal puḷakittu uḷḷamellām urukik kaḷikkum. ik kaḷiyāṭṭiṉaip piṉṉar atu, taṉ pārppukkaḷ kāṇavum kēṭkavum ceytayartal toṭaṅkiṟṟu. iṅṅaṉam ceytaṟkuk kāraṇam yātākum? appārppukkaḷum itaṉait toṭarvaṉa vallavō? avaṟṟiṟkum avvulaka vuṇarviṉai yuṇarttal vēṇṭumaṉṟō? atu pōlum kāraṇam!
iv vaṇṇam nāṭkaḷ cila ceṉṟaṉa. orunāḷ ak kuñcukaḷu ḷoṉṟu, āymukanōkki, av vulaku yāṇṭuḷa teṉṟu kēṭpa, atu, "avvulakam uṇṭeṉpatu uṇmai; maṟṟu, yāṉ aḵtu uṟumiṭam aṟiyēṉ. ākaliṉ, avviṭattai yāṉ kūṟutal kūṭātu" eṉṟatu. eṉṟalum, avaṟṟuṭṭalaik kuñcu muṉṉiṉṟu, "iv yāṟu cellumiṭam yātu, atu av aṟiyā vulakam!" eṉṟatu - itu uṇmai kol? aṉṟu. av yāṟu, maṟuvilmāṉavar malinta mūtūr veṟitu cēṟal viḻuppamataṉṟeṉa malaipaṭu poruḷum kāṉpaṭu celvamum talaimaṇantukoṇṭu, perunakaraṭaintu, kālvaḻic ceṉṟu, vayaliṭaip paṭarntu, ataṉkaṇ viḷaiyum paiṅkūḻ vaḷarttu, vaṇikar marakkalam aṇipeṟa mitappa, maṅkaiyar kuṭaintu māṇalam puṉaiya, purinūlmārpar aruṅkaṭaṉāṟṟa, mīṉuṇkurukum, tūṇṭil vēṭṭuvarum, tiṇṭimilvāḻnarum celvam mīkkoḷap parantu ceṉṟu, kōṉōkki vāḻuṅ kuṭiyaṉaittum vāṉōkkaliṉ, ataṟkiṉṟiyamaiyāp perumaḻai piṟakkum peruntiraip puṇariyaip puṇarum! ippuṇariyō aṟiyā ulakeṉap paṭuvatu! pakaliṉ pāṉmaiyum iraviṉ eḻilum puḷḷiṉattukkoru polivum māviṉattukorumāṇpum vayaṅkat taruvaṉavāka, ivaiyaṉaittum tanta voruvaṉ, ivai celliṭa moṉṟaic ceyyātu viṭuvaṉō?
ituniṟka, "aṉṉāy! aṟiyāvulakiṉ aḻakiṉaik kūṟuvatāl ākumpayaṉ yātukol! ataṉaip palakālum pāṭip pukaḻtal eṟṟukku?" eṉṟu toṭaṅkiya oru kuṟumpuḷ.  "tēṉūṟṟeṭuppa maṇanāṟum pūmpoḻilakattaiviṭṭu nām vēṟiṭam cēṟal eṉṉai? meṉṟaḷirum naṟumalarum mikaipaṭac ceṟinta kūṭoṉṟākki, nām ēṉ eṉṟum īṇṭē vāḻtal kūṭātu? vāṉkuyilka ḷicaipāṭa varivaṇṭu pāṇmiḻaṟṟa, māṉkaṉṟu payiṉṟuḷḷa, palluyirum poliveyta viḷaṅkum ivviḷamarakkāviṉum iṉitukol av aṟiyāvulakam? āruyiraṉṉāy! nām vēṟiṭañcēṟal vēṇṭā. itupōtu, cintāmaṇi teṇkaṭalamirtam tillaiyāṉaruḷāl vantālikaḻap paṭumē! muyaṟciyiṉṟiyē perum payaṉ eytutaṟkuriya iṭam atuvāyiṉum, yāṉ ivviṭam viṭṭup peyarēṉ. oḻika! iṉi vēṟupulañ cēṟal. paintāṭ kōraiyum, paciya taḷir niṟainta putalum mikka iv vaṭaikaraiyiṉum iṉitukol av aṟiyāvulakam! ākaliṉ, aṉṉāy! iṉip pāṭutaloḻika! pāṭutaṟku uḷam oruppaṭutalum oḻika!!" eṉṟatu.
piṉṉar attāyppuḷ, taṉmaṉattup palavēṟuvakaip paṭṭa eṇṇaṅkaḷ paṭarnteḻun taṭaṅka, veytuyirttu, vēṟukūṟā toḻiya, maṟṟoru ciṟukuruku kūṟum. atu, "iṟutta iruḷ keṭa, yāṇṭum iḷaṅkālum, iṉṉoliyum eḻa, poṉṉurukkeṉṉa iḷaveyil parappi, viṭiyaliṟ kīḻkkaṭaliṉ mukaṭṭeḻum iḷañāyiṟṟiṉaik kāṇāy! teṇṇīriṉ tiraiyiṭai, acceññāyiṟṟiṉ ceṅkatirkaḷ tavaḻtalum, āṇṭup pal vaṇṇaṅkaḷ tōṉṟi miḷirtal kāṇāy! varikkeṇṭaiyum paruvarālum naṇpakaloḷiyil miṉceytu maṟaitalum, iḷantaḷirkaḷ oḷiteṟittalum piṟavum kāṇāy! taṇṭeṉṟal taḷiracaippa, pārppukkaḷ kiḷaipaṭarntu icaipāṭa, putumalariṉ maṇam parava, kālaiyaṇintu pakalellām oḷiyiṉṟiyirunta kaviṉaṉaittum, mālaiyiṟṟuvara muṭittut tōṉṟum iyalaṇaṅkiṉ eḻiṟ cevviyai maṟattalum kūṭumā? īṇṭāyiṉum, āṇṭāyiṉum, yāṇṭāyiṉum amarka. veṇmatiyiṉ taṇkatirkaḷ paintaḻaikaḷiṉmītu paravi, kalittōṭum aruvinīril āṭal payiṉṟu, pūṅkāvai vāṉamākki, kāṉyāṟṟai, attiṅkaṭ puttē ḷūrntu cellun tērvaḻiyākka, viḷaṅkum iravup polivai niṉaittoṟum neñcam vēṟōriṭattaiyum niṉaikkuṅ kol! tiṇkalamum ciṟunāvāyum celvuḻiccelvuḻi yeḻum oyyeṉum ōcai nam uṟakkam keṭuppa, neṭuntaruviṉ paiṅkiḷai paṭarntu pāṭalai nam iṉam payila, iṉpakkāṭciyē yāṇṭum ilaṅkum in nilattiṉum av aṟiyānilam aḻakuṭaittō?
"tuñcuvatu pōla iruḷi, viṇpaka
	imaippatu pōla miṉṉi, uṟaikkoṇ(ṭu)
	ēṟuvatu pōlap pāṭuciṟan(tu) uraii
	nilaneñ(cu) uṭka ōvātu cilaittāṅ(ku)
	ārtaḷi poḻinta vārpeyaṟ kaṭaināḷ."
nām nam paṭaiyamai cēkkaiyaip paṭarntu, oṉṟō ṭoṉṟu pulli irukkiṉ kārceypaṉippum kalaṅkutuyar ceyyātāka, eḻunta vemmaiyāl iṉpam mikum ivvirum poḻiliṉum, nī kūṟum appūm poḻil ēṟṟamuṭaittāmō? ātaliṉ aṉṉāy! vēṟupulam muṉṉutalai viṭṭoḻittu, ippulam paṟṟiya iṉṉicaiyē miḻaṟṟuti!" eṉṟatu.
piṉṉar, attāyāya cempuḷ, iṅṅaṉaṅkūṟiya taṉ celvak kuñciṉ peruñcoṟkaḷaik kēṭṭu, oruvāṟu, uḷattait tirutti, "celvaṅkāḷ! iṉi nīvir virumpiya vaṇṇamē vēṟoru pāṭṭē pāṭuval. pōṟṟik kēṇmiṉ eṉṟu oru mellicai, poruḷ ceṟiyap pāṭutal toṭaṅkiṟṟu. apporuḷ: 
"paranta ulakiṭait tōṉṟiya yāvum taṉ poṟipulaṉ kaḷiṉ vaḻiyē maṉattiṟkuk kuṉṟāviṉpamum, accamaṟiyā viyalpum nalkat tāṉ peṟṟuvāḻntamaiyum, avvāḻkkai yil nāṭpala cella, taṉakku uḷḷiruntē, 'iḵtu niṉṉiṭa maṉṟu' eṉa vōr poruṇmoḻi yeḻunticaittamaiyum; ammoḻi, piṉ palamuṟaiyum icaippak kēṭṭatāṉ, orukāl uvakaiyum, orukāl veṟuppum, palakālum kēṭṭaṟ koruppaṭātu cēṟalum koṇṭiruntamaiyum; irukkaiyil, tāṉ vāḻumiṭamē mētakka iṭamāvatu, ataṉai nīṅkutal aṟavē kūṭātu eṉṉum uvakai yuṇarvu tōṉṟiṉamaiyum; piṉṉart taṉ maṉakkiṉiya tuṇaip paṟavai pōtantu, kātal ceytu kaḷittuk kalantamaiyum; atu kālai, ammoḻip poruḷ paṉmuṟaiyum eḻuntu, ūṉkalantu, uṭalkalantu, uyirkalantu, piṉṉar uṇarvuṅ kalantu orō vōcaiyāy uruveṅkuṅ kalantuniṉṟamaiyum; akkalappē, taṉ tuṇaippuḷḷiṟkum nikaḻa, iraṇṭum uḷaṅkalantu ikkāṉyāṟṟaṭaikarai yaṭaintu, ciṟṟiṟ camaittuc cevviya vāḻkkai naṭātti vantamaiyum piṟavumām.
itaṉaikkēṭṭa pārppiṉuḷ oṉṟu, "aṟṟēl, aṉṉāy! nī paṇṭē yirunta iṭam yāṇṭaiyatu? arukuḷatāyiṉ, yām aṉaivarum āṇṭē paṭarntu, iṉpantuyttu iṉitu vāḻalāmē!" eṉṟatu. eṉavum, tāyppuḷ uḷaṅkalaṅki, āruyirc celvamē! ataṉai yeṅkuḷateṉpēṉ! aṟiyāvulaka meṉpēṉō! aṉṟi mikamikac cēymaiyiluḷḷatōr viṇṇiṭam eṉpēṉō yātu kūṟuvēṉ! yāṉ aṟiyēṉ. āyiṉum, aṉṟeḻun ticaitta poruṇmoḻi iṉṟumeḻuntu īrkiṉṟatu. atu aṉṟiṭṭa āṇaiyai mēṟkoṇṭaṭaṅkiya yāṉ iṉṟu viṭuttal kūṭumō? ayarāviṉpattiṟṟiḷaittirunta aṉṟē avvāṇaiyaiyēṟṟa yāṉ, iḷamaiyum, ataṉāṉām iṉpamum iḻanta innilaiyiṟ koḷḷātoḻital muṟaiyō? ākavē, eṉ arumaic celvaṅkaḷē! av aṟiyāvulakam yāṇṭamaintiruppiṉum irukka. nām aṉaivarum ataṉainōkkic celvam. vammiṉ!! yāṉ kūṟuvatu uṇmaiyē eṉṉum uṟutinōkkaṅ (Faith)  koṇṭu oruppaṭumiṉ” eṉṟatu.
eṉṟalum, “nīyum emmoṭu varutiyaṉṟē. āyiṉ, yāṉ iṉṉē varuval” eṉṟu mikacciṟiyatōr pārppu viḷampit taṉ tāyicaitta taṉiyicaiyiṟ ṟāṉuṅ kalantu pāṭal toṭaṅkiṟṟu.
iṉṉaṇam, collāṭalālum icaiyālum poḻutu kaḻiya, 'veñcuṭar veppan tīrat taṇṇaṟuñcōlai tāḻntu niḻaṟceyyavum, taṇpatampaṭṭa teṇkaḻi mēyntu palvēṟu vakaippaṭṭa puḷḷellām kuṭampai nōkki uṭaṅkupeyaravum, puṉṉai mutaliya pūviṉāṟṟam muṉṉiṉṟu kañaṟṟavum, neṭuntirai yaḻuvattu nilākkatir parappavum’ mālai vantatu. varakkaṇṭa tāyppuḷ, taṉ pārppukkaḷait tamittuviṭṭu iraitēṭuvāṉ ceṉṟiruppa, avaṟṟuḷoṉṟu aṇmaiyilōṭiya aruviyiṉ maruṅkuniṉṟa putaṟkaṇamarntu, aruviyiṉ kalippum, aṭaiciṉai yacaitali ṉeḻu mārppum kēṭṭalum kaḷimīkkūrntu, aṅku miṅku mōṭiyōṭit taṉṉallicai koṇṭu iṉṉicai miḻaṟṟat toṭaṅkiṟṟu. akkālai atu icaikka muyaṉṟa icaikaḷ palavum iyaipupaṭātoḻiya, iṟutiyiṟ ṟaṉṟāy pāṭiya aṟiyāvulaka vicaiyai iyakkippārkka, atu naṉkaṉam kaivaravuvantu ataṉaiyē eṭuttum paṭuttum iṉitu pāṭik koṇṭiruntatu.
“ā! yāḻō! kuḻalō!! iṉṟēṉ perukkō!!! eṉpelām urukkum iṉṉicai miḻaṟṟavallatu yātukol! ā! tarukkaḷum caliyā; munnīrc calatiyum kaliyā; nīṇṭa poruppiṉiṉṟum iḻiyum aruvikkālum natikaḷum puraṇṭutuḷḷā; epporuḷaiyum taṉvayamākki, uḷniṟai yuyirum meyyum urukkukiṉṟatē!” eṉap pukaḻvatu pōlum iḻittakka kūṟikkoṇṭē puṟavamoṉṟu āṇṭu ataṉarukē vantatu. itu puṟaṅkuṉṟi kaṇṭaṉaiyatēṉum mūkkiṟ karumaiyu muṭaittu; aṉṟiyum oṇmai yuṭaiyam yām eṉṉum veṇmai talai ciṟantatu. ekkuṭikeṭukkavō, etaṉalam tūṟṟavō īṇṭu iḵtu vantuḷatu. itaṉiyalpaṟiyāc ciṟucempuḷ, tāṉ kēṭṭa itaṉ poyyuṟu pukaḻcciyai meyyuṟu pukaḻcciyeṉ ṟeṇṇi, peritum muyaṉṟupāṭalum, immaṭappuṟavam, taṉ ciṟutalaiyaip paiyattuḷakki, “naṉṟu, naṉṟu, yāṉum pala viṭaṅkaṭkuc ceṉṟuḷēṉ. pala iṉṉicaikaḷāṟ ṟōṭkap peṟṟu muḷēṉ. orukāl, makkaḷakattup pōṟṟi vaḷarkkappeṟum pēṟum peṟṟuḷēṉ. eṉavē, icainalaṅ kāṇṭaliṟ ciṟanta vaṉmai peṟṟuḷēṉ eṉpatu naṉku tōṉṟum. yāṉaṟintavaḷavil, āṇaiyiṭṭuk kūṟuvēṉ, niṉṉoppār nallicaivallār makkaḷiṉum ilar.  āyiṉum oṉṟu, niṉṉāṟ pāṭappeṟum poruḷkaḷ yāvai? avai yāṉ itu kāṟuṅ kēṭṭaṉavallavē!!” eṉac ciṟitu cuḷittu viṉaviṟṟu.
kuṟumpuḷ	: ā! avai yāvum aṟiyāvulaku paṟṟiyaṉa.
puṟavam : evai? aṟiyā...
kuṟumpuḷ	: avai aṟiyā vulakiṉ aḻaku, iṉpam,  vāḻkkaiyiyalpu, ataṉaiyaṭaiyumāṟu mutaliyavaṟṟaik kūṟuvaṉa?
puṟavam	: (nakaittu) atu aṟiyāvulakāyiṟṟē! ataṉaip  paṟṟi nī aṟintatiyāṅṅaṉam? ammamma! muṟṟāk kāyellām muḻutta kēḷvi vallavāyirukkiṉṟaṉa. kāṭum ceṭiyum avā vaṟuttal meyyuṇartal mutaliyaṉa ceykiṉṟaṉavē! meyyāka, yāṉ ivaṟṟai yaṟital vēṇṭum. mūttu mutirnta mutumai mikka yāṉ, avvulakiṉaiyaṟintu āvaṉa ceytukōṭal vēṇṭumē! naṉṟu! aṟiyāvulakameṉṟu nī kūṟuvatu yātu? yāṇṭuḷatu? aruḷceytu kūṟuka.
kuṟumpuḷ	: yāṉ aṟiyēṉ. (uṇarvukalaṅki) orunāḷ avvulakiṉukkuc cēṟalkūṭum eṉpataiyaṉṟi vēṟu oṉṟum yāṉ aṟiyēṉ.
puṟavam : naṉṟu. aṟintēṉ. pētaiyōr pētaiyōr eṉa nūlkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. avar yāvar, yāṇṭuḷar eṉa āyntu koṇṭē varutal eṉ iyaṟkai. aṉpē! iṉṟu kaṇṭēṉ. niṉmāṭṭē an nūlkaḷiṉ kūṟṟu vāymaiyātal kaṇṭēṉ. nīyē ataṟkut takka cāṉṟu. ākavē, nīviṟ aṟiyā vulakiṟkuc cellum celavu mēṟkoṇṭuḷḷīr. āṟṟuppaṭaiyiṉṟiyē pōlum! appeṟṟit tāya celavu nī nayantaṉi rāyiṉ, num naṉṉar ceñcattu iṉṉacai vāyppa, muṉṉiya yāvaiyum iṉṉē peṟuka!! yāṉ varuval eṉṟu kūṟikkoṇṭē paṟantu ciṟitu cella, ak kuṟumpuḷ, virutuppaṭṭikku viraintu pōṅ kaliyaik kuṟukkiṭṭu uṟuvilai koṭuttu vāṅkiyāṅku, “niṟka! ciṟitu tāḻttal vēṇṭum naṉṟu, tāṅkaḷ kūṟiyatu yātu? eṉ aṟivu mayaṅkukiṉṟatu” eṉṟatu.
puṟavam : aṟiyāvulakattiṟku amaintuḷḷa nuntam  celavu mētakkatē. āyiṉum, iṭaippaṭṭa eṉakkum uṉakkumē ac celavu ceytaṟkēṟṟa neṟi tōṉṟātu pōlum.
kuṟumpuḷ  : ō, ataṉai viṭuka. avvulakupaṟṟiya ummuṭaiya kōḷ yātu? ataṉai yēṉum kūṟuka.
puṟavam : eṉṉai? ē turumpē! eṉṉaiyō ikaḻ  kiṉṟaṉai. naṉṟu! uṉṉotta cīriya icaip pulamaiyum, nuṭpamum tiṭpamumamaintu aṟivu muṭaiya evvuyirēṉum aṟiyāta oṉṟiṉmāṭṭu veṟitē niṉaintu cāmpuṅkol! vēṇṭiṉ, nī  niṉṉaiyē koḷḷalām. uṉṉaippōl, vāḷātu poḻutu kaḻippatāyiṉ eṉṉaiyum koḷḷalām. oṉṟu kūṟuvēṉ; yāvarukkum olluva tollum eṉpatē ciṟantatu. mikuttuk  kūṟal mikka ētamē tarum.
kuṟumpuḷ	: aṟṟēl, nīr av vaṟiyāvulakiṟkup pōkīr pōlum!
puṟavam :	orukālum illai. mutaṟkaṇ, yāṉuṟaiyu miṭam,  muḻaṅkukaṭal ñālam muḻuvatum variṉum, vaḻaṅkattavā vaḷappamum, viḻaivu viṭutta viḻumi yōrkkum viḻaivu tōṟṟuvikkum viḻuppamum vāyntatu. ākaliṉ yāṉ vēṟiṭam virumpēṉ.  mēlum, peṟṟatu koṇṭu peṟum pēṟē pēṟeṉap paṭuvatu, iraṇṭāvatāka, kēṭkap paṭuvaṉa yāvum vāymaiya eṉa eḷitu ārāyātu koḷḷum nummōraṉṉōr pōlum  ciṟṟaṟivuṭaiyēṉ yāṉalaṉ. aṉṟiyum, nīr kūṟum ulakamoṉṟu uṇṭeṉpatai yēṉum yāṉ eṅṅaṉam aṟivatu?
kuṟumpuḷ	 : eṉṉai īṉṟōr eṉakku aruḷiṉar.
puṟavam : īṉṟōrkaḷō kūṟiṉar? aṅṅaṉamā!aṟṟēl, nī mikamikac cīriyatōr paṟavaiyē! niṉ īṉṟōr kūṟuvaṉavaṟṟaik kūṟiyavāṟē uḷaṅkoḷum nīrmai vēṇṭaṟpālatē! avarkaḷ orupakal, nī ceññāyiṟṟaic ceṉṟu cērntu vāḻtaluṅ kūṭum eṉparāyiṉ, nī āmeṉṟē koḷḷuvāy pōlum!!
kuṟumpuḷ	: (cīṟiyatu pōl) īṉṟu puṟantanta eṉ kuravar  orupoḻutēṉum eṉṉai vañcittatiṉṟē!
puṟavam : 	eṉṉai, yeṉṉai? vekuḷuvateṉṉai? eṉṉai ceyti? niṉpeṟṟōr niṉṉai vañcittaṉar; vañcar eṉṟu oruvarum kūṟiṟṟilarē! avar, niṉṉai vañcittilarāyiṉ,  oṉṟu kūṟuvēṉ, avar pētaiyarē, aṉṟēl, poruḷaṟiyātārē. nummōraṉṉōr māṭṭu oṉṟukūṟal viḻaivōr, uṇmaiyil,  uḷanōy kōṭal ulakiyal, 'ēvavum ceykalāṉ, tāṉtēṟāṉ, avvuyirpōomaḷavum ōr nōy.’ niṟka, avar kūṟṟu vañcaṉaiyiṉpāṟ paṭuvatō, poruḷaṟiyāmai yiṉpāṟ paṭuvatō, nīyē ārāyka. ulakiyalum uṇmaiyaṟivu milarōṭu uḷḷōr uraiyāṭal oruvāṟṟāṉum ollātākaliṉ, yāṉ ceṉṟu varuval. vāḻka! vāḻka!! (maṟaikiṉṟatu.)
iṅṅaṉam am maṭappuṟavaṅ kūṟiya coṟkaḷāṟ ṟaṉ uṇmaiyaṟivu kalakkaṅ koṇṭa kuṟumpuḷ oruvāṟu teḷitaṟkuḷ, atu kaṭpulaṅ kaṭantatu.
maṟupakal, vēṉil veppamum, vevviya kāṟṟum, ilavāka, taṉ taṉmaiyilōr tiripuṇmaiyaik kāṭṭiṟṟu. nīṉiṟa vāṉam māṉiṟaṅ koḷḷak koṇmūviṉam viṇ mūṭiṉa. kārkōḷ mukanta kamañcūl māmaḻai vaḷḷuṟai citaṟit talaippeyal peytatāka, karaiporu tiraṅkum kāṉyāṟu vāṉ yāṟoppap perukkiṭṭōṭi, kuḷirppu mikutalum, uṟaintu paṭa, māmēyal maṟantaṉa; manti kūrntaṉa; paṟavaipaṭivaṉa vīḻntaṉa; kaṟavai kaṉṟukō ḷoḻintaṉa; kuṭāvaṭi uḷiyam mutaliyaṉa peruṅkal viṭaraḷaiyiṟ ceṟintaṉa; āmā nallēṟu cilaittaṉa; kāṟṟuṟ ṟeṟitalāl mayiliṉam vīṟṟuvīṟ ṟōṭiṉa. iṭampeṟa vamainta kuṭampaikaḷ, paṟavaivum pārppum uṟaiyuḷiṉṟoḻiya, nilampaṭa vīḻntaṉa. varaṉeṉum vaippil uraṅkoṇṭa aṉpu pūṇṭa cempuḷḷiṉ cēkkaimaṭṭil ūṟoṉṟiṉṟi iṉitiruntatu. ataṉuḻai yirunta kuñcukaḷ iv vaṉaittuṅ kāṇṭalum, peruviyappuṟṟu, pakalōṉ karantaṉaṉ; paṉi mikuntuḷatu eṉkol! eṉum eṇṇan talaikkoṇ ṭiruppa, oṉṟu, “peruvāṉam virikati riṉṟiyum, mukiṟkulam paravappeṟṟum uḷatu! teṇṇīryāṟu maṇṇīrmaiyuṟṟu acaiviṉṟi yuḷatu! pakalavaṉ oḷiyē yiṉṟip, pārmuṟṟum paciya iruḷ ceṟiyap peṟṟuḷḷatu! eṉṉō!” eṉṟatu.
maṟṟum oṉṟu : nerunal uḷaṉoruvaṉ iṉṟillai yeṉṉum  perumai ivvulaku uṭaittaṉṟō!
tāyppuḷ : (nakaittu) ām! āyiṉum, virikatirc celvaṉai  nām nāḷai kāṇṭal kūṭum. iḵtuṇmai! niṟka. ikkālattup pakaṟpoḻutu kuṉṟivarutalai nīvir aṟintilīr pōlum! iṉṟeḻunta puyalē ippakaṟ poḻutiṉaik kuṟaittatu;  ātalāṟṟāṉ, ceññāyiṟṟiṉ veṅkatirum, im mukilvaḻi nuḻaintu veyileṟittal kūṭātu pōyiṟṟu. itukiṭakka. it  talaippeyal nam kuṭampai muṟṟum īrañceytiṟ ṟiṉṟākaliṉ, uḷḷē vammiṉ kuḷirppu nīṅka; yāṉ ap perumpeyarc  celaviṉmēṟ pāṭṭoṉṟu pāṭukiṉṟēṉ. pētaiyīr, nīvir ip pakaṟ poḻutē eṉṟum kāṇṭal kūṭu meṉa niṉaikkiṉṟīrkollō?
in nilavulakiṉ kāṭcinalaṉait tūkki yuraitta ciṟupuḷ muṉpōntu, “aṉṉāy! yāṉ, paṇṭu, eṉṟum kāṇṭal kūṭumeṉṟē eṇṇiṉēṉ, niṉaittēṉ; maṟṟu, iṉṟē yāṉ ataṉkaṇṇum tiripuṇmai kaṇṭēṉ. āyiṉum yāṉ ataṟku iṉi añcēṉ! paruti paravai paṭiya, karumukil peru maḻai poḻiya, vāṉaṅ karuka, kāṉyāṟu perukiṉ, varum nōytīr maruntum aṟintuḷēṉ. atu, nām aṭaiya virumpum aṟiyāvulakiṉ āynta nallicai.
kēṭṭa tāy, uvakai kiḷarnteḻa, nuṉikkompa rēṟi, kēṭkum ōraṟivuyir mutal āṟaṟivuyir uḷḷiṭṭa yāvum meypaṉikkumvaṇṇam, tēmpiḻipōlum iṉṉicai miḻaṟṟiṟṟu. atukālai, ataṉōṭu cēvaṟpuḷḷum, ciṟupuḷḷum otticaittu uvakaittēṉ cuvaittaṉa. yāvum otticaittaṉa veṉiṉum oṉṟumaṭṭil antō! uḷam oruppaṭātu, uvakaiyuṅ kūrātu veṟutta nōkkoṭuniṟpa, icaipperukku niṉṟatu. niṟṟalum atu, ēṉaic ciṟupuṭkaḷai viḷittu, “ivvicai, itaṉiṉum pattaṭuttatām, ataṉuṭ kūṟappeṟum ulakupaṟṟiya yāvum uḷḷa vāṟu uṇarappeṟiṉ” eṉṟatu. ivvāṟē, pirivu kāraṇamākat tuyaruṟ ṟirunta peṭaicciṟupuḷḷoṉṟu, nakaittu, “ataṉaip paṟṟi nām mikaipaṭa vuṇarvēmāyiṉ, īṇṭai nalattāṉ namakku maṉavamaiti yuṇṭākā taṉṟō!” eṉṟatu. ivaṟṟaik kēṭṭa maṟṟoṉṟu, “ām, nām avvulaku cārpāka oṉṟum aṟintilē maṉṟō! uṇmaiyil, aṅṅaṉamōr ulaku uṇṭeṉpatai yēṉum eṅṅaṉam aṟitalām?” eṉṟalum, maṟṟoru peṭaippuḷ, “aṉṟu, aṅṅaṉa moṉṟu uṇṭeṉpatai nām naṉku uṇarkiṉṟōm. yātō? nam aṉṉai kūṟiya uḷḷoli eṉṉuḷ eḻunticaittatu!yāṉ uṇarntēṉ” eṉṟu kūṟiṟṟu.
ik kūṟṟiṉaik kēṭṭa talaikkuñcu, “uṉṉuḷ eḻun ticaittatāka nī niṉaittaṉaikollō! atu tāṉē! ā! ivaiyāvum veṟṟuraikaḷē. iṅṅaṉam niṉṉaik kūṟumāṟu nam aṉṉai kūṟiṉāḷpōlum! ammamma! nī kūṟuvaṉavum, aṉṉai kūṟuvaṉavum piṟavum vīṇ poruḷil yāppukkaḷēyaṉṟip piṟitillai. nuṇṇiya kēḷviyaṟivu mikiṉum iyaṟkaiyaṟivu kūṭiṉaṉṟō atu māṭci peṟum! yāṉum appullaṟivē mēṟkoṇ ṭiruppiṉ, av vuḷḷoli eṉṉuḷḷu meḻun ticaitta teṉpēṉ. iṉi yāṉ av vulaku cēṟum celavaiyum niṉaiyēṉ. cēṟaṟku uṭaṉpaṭalum paṭēṉ. celka” eṉac ciṟitu vekuṇṭuraittatu.
“iḵtaṉṟu nī niṟkumiṭam” eṉpatu poruḷāka vamainta nallicai yoṉṟai eṭutticaittavaṇṇam, kuṭampaiyiṉ puṟattē tāy vara, akattirunta pārppukkaḷuṭ cila, tāy pāṭiyataiyē tāmum pāṭat toṭaṅkiṉa. annilaiyil, at tāyppuḷḷiṉ uḷḷum av voli yeḻuntatu. eḻavē, maṟumuṟaiyum, atu an nallicaiyaiyē toṭaṅki, “iḻumeṉōtaiyiṉ aruvi yiraṅkalum, maruṅku karai koṉṟu kāṉyāṟu cēṟalum, viṇṇakaṅ kaṭantu koṇmūvēkalum, mālvarai tuḷaṅkak kālporu tōṭalum, virikatirpparuti paravaiyiṉ maṟaitalum epporuḷ nāṭic ceykiṉṟaṉa. itaṟkuk kāraṇamāvatu, avaṟṟi ṉakatteḻuntu, “iḵtu aṉṟu nī niṟkumiṭam”eṉ ṟolikkum aruḷoli yaṉṟō! yāṟṟoṭu kūṭuka! mukiloṭu moḻika! kāliṉai viṉavuka! nīvir celliṭam yāteṉṟu! aṟiyāvulakamē! varaṉeṉum vaippē yaṉṟō! 'nī celliṭam yā’ teṉa veyilavaṉai viḷittuk kēṇmiṉ. avaṉ cēṟumiṭaṉum appeṟalarum ulakē! kuṟitta nāḷ varuṅkāl nām aṉaivarum āṇṭup paṭarkuvam” eṉa muṭittatu.
iv vicaivayappaṭṭu maṉantiruntiya attalaikkuñcu, “aṉṉāy! aṉṉāy! nī kūṟuvatu vāymaikollō! yāṉ koḷḷumāṟu yātu? av vulakam uṟumiṭam yāṇṭaiyatu? aruḷ ceyka,” eṉak kacintaḻutu, taṉ tāymuka nōkki, taṉakku am maṭappuṟavaṅ kūṟiya aṉaittum muṟaiyum poruḷum vaḻātu viḷampiṟṟu. viḷampuṅkāṟum amaiti koṇṭēṟṟa ac cempuḷ, “arumanta celvamē! alamaral vēṇṭā; yāṉ vēṟōr nallicai yicaippal kēṭṭi” eṉṟu toṭaṅkit tāṉ paṇṭē yakaṉṟa ulakiyalaippaṟṟik kūṟal toṭaṅkiṟṟu.
aṅṅaṉam, avviṭattiṉiṉṟum atu akaṉṟañāṉṟu, tāṉ ēṉ akaṟal vēṇṭumeṉṟu niṉaittatuṇṭō eṉiṉ iṉṟu; cellumiṭam iṟṟeṉa aṟiyāmē, paṇivum, uṟutinōkkum, nīṅkuta ṉalattiṟkeṉum tiṇṇiya veṇṇamum āya ivaiyē ataṉ uḷapporuḷā yamaintiruntaṉa! peruṅkaṭalulakiṟ pirintu varunāḷ, ac celavukkuriya kāraṇap poruḷai yuṇarttu vārēṉum, “iḵtaṉṟu, atu niṉ neṟi” eṉak kūṟuvārēṉum, atu kaṇṭatuṇṭō veṉiṉ, aṉṟu. aññāṉṟu, ammaṭappuṟavam kaṇṭiruttal kūṭumāyiṉ, atu tāṉum ivvāṟu kūṟaṟpālatāmō? ivai yaṉaittum ārāyātu pōnta accempuḷ tāṉum piṉṉar uriya nalattait tuykkā toḻintatō? ataṉai yaṅṅaṉam cēṟal ceyyumāṟu ūkkiya poruḷāya uḷḷoli yātām? atuvē tiruvaruḷ eṉpatu. appaṟavai, ak kāṉyāṟ ṟaṭaikarai yaṭainta nāḷē uṇmaiyai oruvā ṟuṇarntatu. ēṉeṉiṉ, āṇṭut tāṉ atu ciṉṉāḷvarait taṅkutal viḻaintatu. piṉṉarē, ataṉ kātaṟṟuṇaippaṟavai pōtantatum. vāḻkkai yiṉpam nukarntatum piṟavum nikaḻntaṉa. aṉparkaḷē! atu pōtu, kātaṟ cēvaloṭu kalantu atu kuṭampai yamaikkumpōtu, taṉvayiṟ piṉṉart tōṉṟuvaṉavāya pārppukkaṭkeṉa ak kuṭampaiyaic camaikkum pōtu, am maṭappuṟavaṅ kaṇṭirukkumēl, ettuṇai ikaḻcci ceytirukkuṅkollō! piṟavāp pārppuk kaṭkuṅ kūṭamaittal pētaimai eṉ ṟeḷḷiyirukkuṅkollō! aṉṟi, “etirviṉai yaṟital yāṅṅaṉam? nī iṅṅaṉ ceytal vīṇ poruḷilyāppē, iḵtu aṟivuṭaiyār toḻilaṉṟu” eṉṟellām viritturait tirukkumō! yātō? niṟka, ic ceykaikkellā māvatōr kāraṇattaiyēṉum ārāyātu atu ceytatu vañcaṉaiyō? aṉṟu ivaiyaṉaittum at tiruvaruḷiṉ ceyalē yām kūṭamaittuc cēvalōṭu kūṭi vāḻkkaic cākā ṭukaittaṟku, itaṟkup pāṅkā yamaintatu avvuḷḷoli yāya tiruvaruḷaṉṟō ataṉvaḻi niṉṟataṉā laṉṟō, arumanta pārppukkaḷ peṟṟu, ayarāviṉpattil atu tiḷaittiruntatu! itu kālai atu, taṉ celvaṅkaṭkuc celvak kālattum allaṟ kālattum kōṭaṟpālaṉavāya uṟutipporuḷ kaḷai yuṇarttaṟkēṟṟa aṟivamaiti peṟṟatō? iṉṟō? “ivai yaṉaittum poruḷil puṇarcci, kaṭṭukkatai, pulavar puraṭci.” eṉṟellām pitaṟṟiya am maṭappuṟavaiyē viṉavumiṉ, avvuḷḷoli yataṉai vañcittatō veṉṟu. aṉṟiyum accempuḷ “celvaṅkāḷ! eṅku, ēṉ cēṟal vēṇṭumeṉṉum ārāycciyiṉṟi, iṉṉē oruppaṭumiṉ. ikkālai yām aṟintilōmāyiṉum, maṟṟorukālai yaṟital kūṭum” eṉṟum, *“paṇivum (Obedience) *aṟivum (Faith) paṇpaṭṭakālattaṉṟē, meyyuṇarvum, poruṇalamum, viḷaṅkat tōṉṟalum kāṭṭap peṟutalum uḷavām,” eṉṟum kūṟiyataṉai appuṟavam uṇaral muṭiyātu pōlum!
piṉṉar nāṭkaḷ cila ceṉṟaṉa. kārum kūtirum nīṅkiṉa. . maḻaiyum paṉippum mikuntamaiyiṉ, varaṉeṉṉum vaippup paṟṟiya nallicai, avaṟṟiṭai, ēnticaiyum tūṅkicaiyumākip piṉṉarc ceppalōcaiyāy, icaikkuntoṟum kēṭṭavai  meycilirppavum, kaṇṇīr vāravum, ūṉkalantu  uyirkalantu, uḷaṅkalantu uṭalamellām uvaṭṭāniṟkum  tēṉkalantu, tittikkum taṉmaiyiṟ ciṟantatu. iḵtuṇṭātaṟkuk kāraṇam, eṉṉaiyeṉiṉ, avai uṇmai yaṟintaṉa; avvuṇmaiyai yuṇarntaṉa; avvuṇmaiyai uṟuti yeṉavum koṇṭaṉa. ākalāṟṟāṉ, avai āṇṭai yiṉpattaiyum īṇṭē yuṇaralāyiṉa.
niṟka, orunāḷ, ciṟukuñcukaḷ kuṭampaiyiṉiṉṟum veḷic ceṉṟiruppa, avaṟṟiṉ uḷaṅ kalaṅkutaṟ kētuvāya pēroli yoṉṟu arukē yeḻuntatu. eḻa, pēraccaṅkoṇṭu, avai tam kūṭaṭaintaṉa. kūṭu akkālai, nilaikulaintu, taḷarntu, nīrāl naṉaippuṇ ṭiruntatu. iruppiṉum, avai, oṉṟai yoṉṟiṟu kappullic ciṟitupōtu kiṭappa, āyppuḷ illāmai terintatu. etir nōkkiya kaṇkaḷ pūttuppōyiṉa; maṉam peruntuyar pūṇṭatu. eṉceyyum!
piṉṉar avai, kūṭṭiṉiṉṟum veḷippōntu, nōkku miṭantoṟum tam tāyppaṟavaiyait tēṭicceṉṟu, taḷarvum, kaḷaippum mītūra, īṉṟāraik kāṇātu ēkkuṟṟa neñcam tuyarntu cāmpa, kāṇṭal kūṭumō kūṭātō eṉṉum eṇṇam oru puṭayalaippa, perumpētuṟṟakālai cēṟum nīrum kalantu ciṟiya nīrnilaiyum, cuṟṟu mōṅkuta lillāp putalum uḷḷatōr tuṉpanilaiyam avaṟṟiṉ muṉṉart tōṉṟiṟṟu. kūrntu nōkkiya kālattu, paṟavaic ciṟakum ciṟumayirum cāmpiya vōcaiyum āṇṭut tōṉṟiṉa. kaṇṭa kuñcukaḷ. vēṇavāmīkkoḷa, aṅkuc ceṉṟu, iṟanta toṉṟum, iṟakkap pōvatoṉṟumāya iraṇṭu puṭkaḷaik kaṇṭu, iṟakkappōvatu tam yāyātal uṇarntu, paṭṭatai eṉ kūṟuvatu! paṭuvataik kaṇṭa tāyp paṟavai, celvaṅkāḷ! “eḻumiṉ! ceṉmiṉ! tiruvaruḷiṉ kāriyamāya uḷḷoli untam uḷattum iṉṉē eḻunticaikkiṉṟatu! ceṉmiṉ! ceṉmiṉ! paṇimiṉ! paṭarumiṉ! tāḻttaṟkuc ciṟitu pōtum iṉṟu! iṉpanāṭṭamē koṇmiṉ!!” eṉṟatu. eṉakkēṭṭa kuṟum puṭkuḻām, “eṉṉai? entai yāṇṭuḷār aiyakō! aṉṉāy! aṉṉāy!!” eṉṟu alaṟik kūyiṉa. “amaimiṉ! amaimiṉ! yāṅkaḷ āṇṭu ummōṭu cūḻa viruttal muṭiyātu; kūṭātu. iṉi...” eṉṟu kūṟikkoṇṭē taṉ talaiyaik kīḻē cāyttu, varaṉeṉṉum vaippil muḷaittatu.
niṟka, itaṉai yīṇṭu uraittukkoṇṭē celliṉ perukum.
 tiruvaruḷ kaikūṭṭumāṟṟiṉai vāḻkkaimukattāṉ uṇartta, vuṇarnta paṟavaikkuñcukaḷ, iṉpavulakai yeṇṇit tāmum pēraṟam nērntu, ap pērulakiṉai yaṭaital vēṇṭi muṭittaṉavāka, palavum palavēṟu neṟiyiṟ paṭarntu ceṉṟaṉa. avai yāṇṭuc celkiṉṟaṉa veṉṟēṉum, ēṉ celkiṉṟaṉa veṉṟeṉum oruvaṟēṉum kaṭṭuraittal kūṭātu. kaṉaviṟ kaṇṭaṉa voppa, tam tāyniṉaivum, tam paṇṭaik kūṭṭi ṉuṟaivum piṉṉar avaṟṟiṉ pulattut tōṉṟalum maṟaitalu māyiṉa. peṟṟatukoṇṭu pēriṉpantuykkuṅ kālattut tam tāyniṉaivu tōṉṟic ciṟitu mayakkalum, avai piṉṉar teḷitalum āya iṉṉōraṉṉa pala nikaḻuṅkālattu, oṉṟu kūṟiyatu, “itaṉiṉum ciṟantatōr iṉpavulakātal vēṇṭum nam tāy paṭarnta vulakam. aṉṉatēyāka, aṅṅaṉam nam tāy kūṟiyatum uṇmaiyē pōlum” eṉpatu.



4. piṟappokkum : ciṟappovvā
vaṭuvilā vaiyattu maṉṉiya mūṉṟaṉuḷ naṭuvaṇateyta iru talaiyumeytum eṉṉum cemporuḷuṇarntu, annaṭuvaṇatāya poḷīṭṭal karutit tiraikaṭalvaḻiyum, maivaraivaḻiyum pala nāṭukaḷukkuc cellum makkaḷ uḷarātalpōla, ciṟṟuyirkaḷuḷ tēṉ tēṭippala ticaikaḷukkuñ celvaṉa palavuḷa. avaṟṟiṟkut tēṉ eṉavum, aḷiyeṉavum, vaṇṭeṉavum, curumpeṉavum palapeyarkaḷ uṇṭu. niṟka, īṇṭut tēṉ tēṭicceṉṟa vaṇṭoṉṟiṉ varalāṟṟiṉaiyē yāṉ kūṟattoṭaṅkukiṉṟēṉ.
pututtaḷir tōṉṟa, pūkkaḷ malara, naṟumaṇam eṅkum naṉku parava, malaiyānilam maruṅkuvantacaiya, yāṇṭum iṉpamē ilakiktēṉāṟum iḷavēṉiṟkālattu mutupakaloṉṟiṉ piṟpakalil, tēṉīyoṉṟu naṟuntēṉ vēṇṭik kāvum, cōlaiyum, kaviṉpūnturuttiyum pukku, āṇṭāṇṭu malarntu vayaṅkum pūttoṟum ceṉṟu, koḷaṟkariya tēṉ mikaippaṭakkoṇṭu, ataṉāleḻunta uvakaiyāl viraintu eḻuntu, taṉkūṭu nōkkic cellat toṭaṅkiṟṟu. āyiṉum, atupōtu, atu pūviyal naṟavam māntip punti mayakkuṟṟiruntamaiyāl, vaḻiyaṟiyātu mēleḻuntu ceṉṟu, arukirunta ōr pērakattiṉ mēṟcāḷarattiṉūṭē ataṉ uṭpuṟam aṭaintatu.
akkālai appērakattiṉkaṇ ētō oruciṟappu naṭai peṟṟatu: iṉiyavum maṇamikkavumāya paṇṭaṅkaḷ pala malintiruntaṉa. mikappalamakkaḷum kūṭiyiruntatōṭamaiyātu collāṭalālum, icaikkaruvikaḷālum piṟavaṟṟāṉum pērōcaiyum ceyvā rāyiṉar. ivaṟṟaikkaṇṭa tēṉīyiṉ karuttil accantōṉṟi oru puṭaivaruttiṉum, paṇṭaṅkaḷiṉ cuvaiyum maṇamum ataṉai veḷiyēkaviṭātu orupuṭaittakaintaṉa. takaiyavē, tēṉīyum naṟumaṇappaṇṭaṅkaḷ vaittuḷḷa taṭṭoṉṟiṉ puṟattētaṅki, avaṟṟiṉ maṇattai nukarntum cuvaiyait tērntum ciṟitupōtu kaḻittu niṟka, "ā! itō pār. tēṉī! tēṉī!!" eṉakkūṟik koṇṭē ciṟuvar iruvar ataṉarukē vantaṉar. iccoṟkaḷai kēṭṭa avvīyum, uṭaṉē accattāl uṭal naṭuṅka veḷic celvāṉ muyaṉṟu orucāḷarattiṉ mēṟpāyntatu. pāvam! ataṉ katavu kaṇṇāṭiyālāyatākaliṉ, akkaḷivaṇṭu ataṉaiyuṇara viyalātu mayaṅki, āṭiyil mōtuṇṭu kalaṅkik kīḻēvīḻntu, piṉṉarc ciṟitu tēṟi, cāḷaraccaṭṭattiṉ kīḻppuṟattē tāṉ mayakkattālum accattālum iḻanta maṉavamaitiyum vaṉmaiyaiyum mīṭṭum peṟaṟporuṭṭu viḻuntu kiṭantatu.
niṟka, itaṉaikkaṇṭa ciṟuvarkaḷiruvarum arukē pōntu, itaippaṟṟiya oru collāṭal nikaḻttuvārāyiṉar.
ciṟuvaṉ : 	taṅkāy! itutāṉ tēṉī. itaṉait toḻilpuri  tēṉī eṉpatum vaḻakku. itaṉ irutuṭaikaḷukku miṭaiyiluḷḷa meḻukkup paiyiṉaiyum kāṇ. ā! eṉṉa ūkkamum uḻaippu muṭaiyatu itu teriyumā!
ciṟumi : aṇṇāl! meḻukkaiyum tēṉaiyum ākkuvatu  ittēṉī tāṉō?
ciṟuvaṉ : 	ām pūkkaḷiṉ uḷḷirukkum iṉiyatēṉai itu  avaṟṟiṉ uṭceṉṟu koṇarkiṟatu. muṉṉoru nāḷ nām nampūmputariṉ akattum puṟattum "kamkam" eṉa eṇṇiṟanta īkkaḷ  moyttu icaittatu kaṇṭō maṉṟō! avai aṅkumiṅkum paṟantu tirintataik kaṇṭu nakaittōmallavā? iḷantaḷirkaḷiṉ mītu ivai paṭaruṅ kālattu ivaṟṟiṉ meyyaḻaku nam  uḷḷamellām koḷḷai koḷḷa villaiyā? niṟka, itu iṉṟu kālaiyil tēṉīṭṭuṅ kālattum yāṉ pārttatuṇṭu.  itaṟkut tēṉīṭṭaloṉṟu tāṉ toḻileṉal ākātu. itaṉoṭu vēṟupala kāriyaṅkaḷum itu ceytal vēṇṭum. tēṉ kūṭu kaṭṭutal, ataṉaiyaḻaku ceyal mutaliya ellāk kāriyaṅ kaḷaiyum itutāṉ ceytal vēṇṭum. itu paṟṟiyētāṉ  itu toḻilpuri tēṉī eṉṟaḻaikkap peṟutalum kāṇka. pāpam! pāpam!!
ciṟumi : toḻilpuritēṉī eṉpatu yātu? aṇṇā! tāṅkaḷ "pāpam! pāpam!" eṉṟu itaṉmāṭṭu iraṅkuvatēṉ!
ciṟuvaṉ : 	ēṉ teriyātu? nam attāṉ nēṟṟu namakkuc  coṉṉatai maṟantuviṭṭāy pōlum: tammaic ciṟitum ōmpātu piṟarnalamē yōmpi yavarkkē āṭpaṭṭuc ciṟu toḻil puriyum makkaḷaṉaivarum iraṅkaṟpālarē! avar vakaiyiṉ pāṟpaṭuvatē  ittēṉīyumākaliṉ, yāṉ itaṉ māṭṭum maṉamiraṅkal vēṇṭiṟṟu. ivvīkkaḷukku araca ī oṉṟuḷatu. ataṉai īyaraci yeṉpatuvē peru vaḻakku. atu oru ciṟutoḻilum, ceyyātu,  eññāṉṟum toḻilīkkaḷ amaitta kūṭṭiṉkaṇṇē iruntu, avai koṇarum tēṉaiyuṇṭalum, koṇarnta īkkaṭku  vēṟupaṇi paṇittalum, tāṉpayanta ciṟṟīkkaḷaip purattalum, ivaṟṟōraṉṉa piṟavumē ceytu koṇṭuvarum. ataṟku vēṇṭum ciṟupaṇikaḷai, maruṅkiruntu ārkkum vaṇṭukaḷ ceyyāniṟkum. ivaṟṟiṟku vēṟāka veṟṟīkkaḷ palavuḷa. avai  oru toḻilum ceyyātu vīṇē aṅkumiṅkun tirintu koṇṭiruppavai. ēṉaiyavai yāvum ivvīyaippōṉṟu tēṉīṭṭip piṟavaṟṟiṟ kaḷittatōṭu, avaṟṟiṟkām ciṟu toḻilaip puripavaiyē yākum. ikkūṟiyavaṟṟai nam attāṉ  aṟivārēl avarāl nakaittu aṭaṅkutal muṭiyātu.
ciṟumi : ataṉāl icceytikaḷ attāṉukkut teriyāvō?
ciṟuvaṉ	:-illai. avarukkut teriyum eṉṟu yāṉ  niṉaikkavillai. teriyumāyiṉ, avar ivaṟṟaip piṭittalum piṭittut tuṉpuṟuttalum ceyyā  raṉṟē, yāṉ evvāṟu aṟintēṉeṉiṉ, eṉakku nam cōlaikkāvalaṉ kūṟiṉāṉ. niṟka, nerunal avar kūṟiyavaṟṟai nōkkiṉaiyā? ivvulakil,  aracar, araciyār eṉa uyirkaḷuṭ cilariruttal iyaṟkaikkē māṟāṉatām; ellārum orē accāka iruppatē itaṟkup pōtiya cāṉṟām; iyaṟkaiyiṟ ṟōṉṟum ovvoru poruṭkum piṟappiṉāl oruvakai vēṟupāṭum iṉṟām;  eṉavē, aracar eṉavum araciyareṉavum iruppavar naṭunilai varampaik kaṭantavarē yāvarām.
ciṟumi :āṉāl, ivaṟṟai uyttuṇarntu kōṭal  ivvīkkaḷukku iyalāteṉa yāṉ niṉaikkiṉṟēṉ.
ciṟuvaṉ :niṉaippateṉṉa! uṇmaiyilē atu avaṟṟiṟkuk  kiṭaiyātutāṉ. cōlaikkāvalaṉ kūṟiyavaṟṟai maṭṭil ittoḻilīkkaḷ kēṭṭirukkumāyiṉ, ettakaiya ciṉaṅ koṇṭirukkum! nīyē niṉaittuppār!!
ciṟumi :	avaṉ kūṟiyateṉṉa?
ciṟuvaṉ : 	araciyaiyum āṭpaṭṭoḻukum ciṟṟīkkaḷaiyum  cīr tūkkuṅkāl piṟappiṉāl avaṟṟiṟkiṭaiyil  oruvakai vēṟupāṭum illai. piṟappum tōṟṟamum oṉṟē. eṉiṉum ivvēṟu pāṭṭait taruvaṉa	uṇṭivakaiyum, uṟaiyuḷiṉ  taṉmaiyumēyām. icciṟṟīkkaḷai  vaḷarkkum peṭaivaṇṭukaḷ, cilavaṟṟiṟku oruvakai uṇavum, vēṟucilavaṟṟiṟku oruvakai uṇavum; cilavaṟṟiṟku oruvakaikkūṭum, vēṟu cilavaṟṟiṟku vēṟuvakai yāṉa kūṭum tarukiṉṟaṉa. ataṉāl cila īyaraci kaḷākavum, cila toḻilīkkaḷākavum māṟukiṉṟaṉa. ikkaruttō nerunal nam attāṉ mēṉmakkaḷaiyum toḻil makkaḷaiyum paṟṟik kūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ  amaintu kiṭantatu. "piṟappokkum ellāvu  yirkkum." eṉpatu tamiḻmaṟai. niṟka, uṇṇuṅkālam āyiṟṟu. vā, pōkalām.
ciṟumi :aṇṇāl! ciṟitu nilluṅkaḷ. yāṉ ivvīyai veḷiyē viṭuttuvarukiṟēṉ.
iṅṅaṉam kūṟikkoṇṭē, acciṟumi taṉ kaittuṇiyāl ataṉaip paiyaveṭuttu aruḷoḻuka nōkki, "antō! ē! koṅkutēr vāḻkkai añciṟait tumpi! kuḻavip paruvattē uṉṉai vaḷarttōr uṉakku uṇṭiyum uṟaiyuḷum uyarntaṉa koṭuttirupparēl, ā! iṉṟuniyum oru īyaraciyāka iruppāyaṉṟō! eṉṉa iḻinta nilaiyai uṉakku avar tantuḷḷār! aṟitiyō? kiṭakka, nī iṉi veḷic ceṉṟu, uṉvāṇāḷ muṟṟilum meḻuku ceytalilum, tēṉīṭṭalilum celaviṭal vēṇṭum. celka. celka. nī mēṟkoṇṭa ciṟu toḻil cīrttoḻilāmāka!" eṉa vāḻtti, cāḷarattiṉ puṟattē viṭuttaṉaḷ. veḷip pōnta vaṇṭu, kaḻipēruvakaikoṇṭu taṉ vaḻiccelkai toṭaṅkiṟṟu.
ā! eṉṉa iṉiyamālaikkālam! kāṭcikkiṉiyamālaik kālam! kātalarkūṭik kalanturaiyāṭum mālaikkālam! iṅṅaṉam iḷavēṉiliṉ mutumālai, maṉṉuyirkkup pēriṉpam payantu iṉitāyt tōṉṟa, attoḻilīkku maṭṭil attaṉmaiyiṉ māṟupaṭṭut tōṉṟiṟṟu. ataṉ maṉam ētō ōr poruḷāṟ paṟṟappeṟṟut tuyarpūṇṭiruntatu; aṭikkaṭi atu oruvakai yuṇarcciyāl nāṇi mika varuntat toṭaṅkiṟṟu. ivvāṟu varuttamum tuyaramum mayaṅkiya uḷḷamoṭu atu taṉ kūṭṭai - paṇiceytu kiṭappatē taṉṉaṟamākum eṉṉum koḷkai yuṭaṉ aṉṟu kālaiyil viṭṭuppirinta taṉ kūṭṭai - aṭaintatu. aṭaitalum, aṭakkalākā veṟuppum vekuḷiyum ataṉ uḷḷattut tōṉṟa, atu tāṉ koṇarnta tēṉpaiyai nekiḻtteṟintu, maṉam calippaṭaiya, "ellāvuyiriṉum kaṭaippaṭṭēṉ yāṉē kāṇ" eṉa aṟaintatu.
akkālai, aṅkuc ciṟutoḻil ceytukoṇṭirunta maṟṟoru toḻilī itaṉaik kēṭṭu, "eṉṉai ceyti? eṉṉē niṉ ceykai yiruntavāṟu! veṟimikka vēṅkaiyiṉ tēṉ uṇṭaṉaikollō? aṉṟi akkoṭiya paccilaippuḻu nam kūṭukaḷil taṉ muṭṭaiyiṭṭuḷatupaṟṟic ciṉaṅkoṇṭaṉaiyō? eṉṉaikāraṇam?" eṉa viṉaviṟṟu. ataṟku itu, "nī kūṟiya yātēṉum vēṇṭuṅ kāraṇamākātu. aiyō pāvam! avaiyeṉ ceyyum? tāntāmuṉ ceytaviṉaiyait tāmē tuyppa tallatu piṟar ceyvateṉ?" eṉaviṭaiyiṟuppa, atu, "piṉṉar eṉṉaiyō kāraṇam. uraitti" eṉa mīṭṭum viṉava, itu kūṟum:- "illai. aṉpa! iṉṟu kālaiyiṟ ceṉṟa yāṉ mikka cēṇiṟ ceṉṟēṉ. ceṉṟaviṭattu yāṉ, itukāṟum kēṭṭaṟiyā taṉavellām kēṭṭēṉ. kēṭṭavaṟṟiṉ karupporuḷ "nāmellōrum iḻitoḻilpuriyum iḻittakka uyir kaḷāvōm eṉpatē."
ivvurai ataṉ cevippaṭalum, atu tiṭukkiṭṭu, "nī itukāṟum aṟiyāta itaṉai uṉakku uṇarttiyavar yāvar?" eṉakkēṭpa, itu cālavekuṇṭu, uṇmaiyai yuṇarttupavar yāvarāyiṉumeṉ! yāṉ iṉṟu aṟintatu muṟṟilum uṇmaiyē. itumaṭṭil uṟutiyē." eṉṟatu.
eṉavē, atu, "oru kālum nī kūṟiyatu uṟutiyuṭait taṉṟu. pētaicciṟṟuyiroṉṟu iṅṅaṉam pitaṟṟiyatu koṇṭu, piḻai paṭṭoḻukutal peruntakaimai yēyaṉṟu. nī iḻittakkōy eṉa uṇarvuṇarttiṉārā luraikkap paṭṭa acciṟupoḻutu kāṟum nī appeṟṟiyai yallai yeṉpatai nīyē naṉkaṟivāy. ākavē, avar uraittāreṉa nī kūṟum urai veṟṟuraiyeṉpataṉiṉum veḷiṟṟurai yeṉavē yāṉ kūṟuvēṉ. iṉi itaip paṟṟik kūṟutalum aṟamākātu." eṉak kūṟikkoṇṭē tāṉ muṉṉark kuṟaipaṭaviṭṭa kāriyattaic ceyyat toṭaṅkiṟṟu.
eṉiṉum, ivvuraimuḻuvatum ceviṭaṉ kātil ūtappeṟṟa tivavoliyē pōlap payaṉiṉṟi yoḻintatu. āṉāl, itu maṭṭil, taṉṉotta īkkaḷ palavaṟṟai oruṅkaḻaittut tāṉ tēṉṟēṭic ceṉṟaviṭat taṟintavai yaṉaittaiyum teḷivākak kūṟiṟṟu. kēṭṭavaṟṟuṭ cila peruviyappum, cila peruṅkalakkamuṅ koṇṭaṉa; muṭivil avvīkkuḻātti ṉiṭaiyil oru peruṅkuḻappam uṇṭāyiṟṟu.
piṉṉarc cila īkkaḷ eḻuntu, uṇmaiyil tām piṟappiṉāl oppāṉavai yeṉiṉum, tammai araca īkkaḷ naṭāttu muṟai iḻivāṉatē yeṉavum, atu tammaiyaṭimaip paṭuttuva toṉṟeṉavum, ataṉai viraiviṟ pōkkuvatu mutaṟkaṭaṉ eṉavum muṭivu ceytaṉa. iṭaiyiṟ cila īkkaḷ muṉvantu tām toṭaṅkiya iyakkattukkut talaimai tāṅkit tammai uriya neṟiyil naṭattuvataṟkēṟṟavar acciṟuvar tam attāṉē yāvar eṉavum kūṟiṉa. atupōtu, vēṟu cila eḻuntu, muṉṉark kuṟikkappeṟṟavarē talaivarāyiṉ, avar tammai oruṅkē yaracaīkka ḷākkutalō ṭamaiyātu tam kūṭukaḷaiyum cevvaṉam purantu, tamakku oru mētakka ciṟappurimaiyum tantaruḷvāreṉavum vaḻimoḻiya, okkum okkum eṉṉum pēroli kammeṉa akkuḻāttiṭaiyeḻuntatu."
ā! itu takumā? yāvarum civikaiyūrat toṭaṅkiṉ, ataṉaik kāvuvār yār! innilaiyil, ciṟitu nāḷ mutirnta toḻilīyoṉṟu, akkūṭṭattiṭait taṉ talaiyai nīṭṭi, "piṟar nalattukkeṉat toḻilpuriyum īkkaḷaṉaittum araca īkkaḷāka māṟiṉ, naṉṟu! naṉṟu!! avaṟṟiṟku āmpaṇiyaip puripavai yāvō?" eṉṟatu. kallā aṟiviṟ kayavarpāṟ kaṟṟuṇarnta nallār tamatu kaṉam naṇṇuvarō? naṇṇāraṉṟē! ākaliṉ, avvīkka ḷaṉaittum, itaṉaik kēṭṭatum cālavekuṇṭu, matiyili eṉavum, taṉṉurimai yaṟiyāttaṟutalaiyeṉavum kūṟi, "nām aṉaivarum aracaīkkaḷākuṅkāl, iṟṟaip pōtu aracu pūṇṭiruppaṉavum, avaṟṟiṉ vaḻittōṉṟalkaḷum tām uḷḷaḷavum namakkup paṇiceytu kiṭakkalāmē!" eṉṟaṉa. eṉṟalum, av ī maṟumuṟaiyum eḻuntu, "avaṟṟiṟkup piṉṉar attoḻilai mēṟkoḷḷuvōr yār" eṉak kēṭpa, kam, kam eṉṉum pēroli āṅku mikkeḻuntu. uṭaṉē, atuvum taṉ nāvaiyaṭakkik koṇṭatu.
maṟṟoru ī eḻuntu, "aṉpuṭaiyīr! eṉakku muṟpēciya īkku nikaḻnta vacai aḻakaṟṟatākum. atu namakkuṭ piḷavaiyuṇṭu paṇṇiṉum paṇṇum. paṇṇuṅkāl, nām kaikkoṇṭa iyakkam veṟṟipeṟātu. nam koḷkaikku māṟākavuḷḷa, paḻaiya nilaiyilē tam vāṇāḷaic celavaḻittārkkup putiya koḷkai kaḷum, uṇarccikaḷum, iyakkaṅkaḷum, piṟavum veṟuppait taruvaṉavākaliṉ, avveṟuppai yuṭaiyavaraiyum nam iyakkattiṟkuk koṇartal iṉṟiyamaiyātu.
"niṟka, tēṉīṭṭalum meḻuku ceytalumāya toḻilkaḷaic ceyyum nām aṉaivarum īyaracikaḷāka māṟiviṭuvōmāyiṉ, namakkāka attoḻilkaḷaip puripavai yilavāka, nam nilai mikka tuṉpa nilaiyāy muṭiyum. itu nīṅkaḷ naṉkaṟinta toṉṟu. itaṉai nīṅkaḷē eṇṇip pāruṅkaḷ.
"eṉṟālum, eṉakku oru eṇṇam tōṉṟukiṟatu; ataṉai acaṭṭai ceyyātu kēṭṭu, amaivuṭaittāyiṉ ēṟṟuṅ koḷḷuṅkaḷ. atu yāteṉiṉ, nām aṉaivarum īyaracikaḷā taliṉum, avvaracurimaiyaiyē muṟṟilum kaḷaintu viṭṭu, aracaī mutal veṟṟīvaraiyuḷḷa aṉaittīkkaḷum toḻilīkkaḷākamāṟi, namakkuḷ oruvakai yuyarvu tāḻvumiṉṟi orumittu vāḻtal vēṇṭum" eṉṟōr coṟpoḻivu ceytatu.
iṭaiyil, muṉṉar vacaipeṟṟuc ceṉṟa toḻilī maṟumuṟaiyum eḻuntu, "nām aṉaivarum iṉṟuvarait toḻilīkkaḷākavē uḷḷōm. aṟṟākaliṉ, namakku ivviyakkattāl oru vakaip payaṉum eytutal muṭiyāteṉa niṉaikkiṟēṉ! eṉṟatu.
eṉṟalum eḻuntaṉa īkkaḷ. pukaippaṭalam pōlat tiraṇṭeḻuntu kam kam eṉap pēroli ceytu, tam ciṉattai evvemmuṟaiyil ataṟkuk kāṭṭutal kūṭumō avvam muṟaiyil kāṭṭiṉa. kāṭṭakkaṇṭañciya atu muṉpōlavē taṉ iruppiṭam aṭaintatu. aṭaitalum, pakalavaṉum maṟaintaṉaṉ; paṟavaippāṭṭu aṭaṅkiṉa; eṅkum iḷaiyiruḷ parantatu; nīlavāṉattu nittilam patittāṟpōlak kōla mīṉiṉam tam ciṟṟoḷi parappiṉa; piṟpakal muṟṟum kuḻappattiṭaiyuḻanta īkkaḷ tattam kūṭaṭaintu uṟaṅkuvavāyiṉa. piṉṉar avviravu muṟṟum vēṟu kuḻappamiṉṟi yoḻiya yāṇṭum amaitiyē talaiciṟantu niṉṟatu.
veḷḷi muḷaippa, viṭiyal vantatu; tāmaraiyiṟ ṟuñciya kāmar vaṇṭiṉam, kaṭkamaḻum neytalukkuc ceṉṟu, tātūtip piṉṉar, kaṇpōl malarnta kāmarcuṉaimalarai yaṭaintu oli ceyyā niṟpa, muṉṉāḷ nikaḻnta kuḻappattil īṭupaṭṭa īkkaḷ maṭṭil oruṅku kūṭi mīṭṭum tam nilaimaiyaiyum, ataṟkēṟṟa muṟaikaḷaiyum paṉṉippaṉṉip pēcattoṭaṅkiṉa. vēṟu cila, kūṭukaḷil, araciyar ēviya paṇikaḷai iṉitu ceytu vantavai, ikkuḻappattaip paṟṟiyēṉum, ataṉiṭaiyē nikaḻum virivurai kaḷaiyēṉum oru poruḷāka niṉaiyātu tam toḻililē tam karuttaic celutti niṉṟaṉa. niṟka, kuḻappattiṟ kalanta īkkuḻāt tiṉiṭai nikaḻccikaḷ pala nikaḻa, mutirā īkkaḷiṟ cila muṉivu mītūrak koṇṭu, ataṉāl tammaiyum, tam maṉattiṇmaiyaiyum iḻantu, peruṅkalakkattai viḷaivikkum nilaimaiyeytiṉa. atupōtu, ikkuḻappattiṟku mutaṟkāraṇamākavirunta ī avaṟṟiṉ muṉṉar, "aṉparkaḷē! nīṅkaḷ kaikkoṇṭa iyakkam naṉmai payakkak kūṭiyatē. eṉiṉum, iṭaiyilirukkum oru poruḷai nīṅkaḷ eṇṇātu pōyviṭṭīrkaḷē! apporuḷ tāṉ etuveṉiṉ, nīṅkaḷ aṉaivarum mutirntuviṭṭamaiyaṉṟō?
