Landscape painting did much to spread the love of the land among the peoples Europe. What Corot did in France and Gainsborough in England to make the scenery of their Country a delightful memory, Indian painters are now expected to do for the Indian scene.



9. tamiḻ makaḷir
paṇṭai nāḷait tamiḻakattil makaḷiratu nilaimaiyai aṟintukoḷvatu mikavum naṉṟu. makkaṭ paṭaippil āṭavar peṇṭir eṉṟa irupālārkkum uṭalamaippil ciṟitu vēṟupāṭu uṇṭēyaṉṟi niṉaivu, col, ceyal, aṟivu eṉṟa kūṟukaḷil vēṟṟumai kiṭaiyātu. uṭalamaippu vēṟupāṭu makkaṭ pēṟṟukkum makkaḷiṉap perukkattukkum ēṟṟa vakaiyil tuṇaipurikiṉṟatu. ataṉāl ulakiyal vāḻkkaikku āṇum peṇṇum oṉṟaṟ koṉṟu iṉṟiyamaiyāt tuṇaiyātalai nām kāṇkiṉṟōm. puṟattē ceṉṟu vāḻkkaikku vēṇṭum uṇṭi, uṟaiyuḷ mutaliyaṉa nāṭi nalkum vakaiyil āṭavar ciṟantu niṟṟaliṉ avaratu uṭaṟkūṟu ēṉaip peṇṭiratu uṭaliṉum vali mikkatākiṉṟatu. makap peṟutalum avaṟṟai vaḷarttalum ākiya ceyal vakaikaḷil peṇ makaḷir āṭavaratu tuṇaimaiyaip peritum nāṭutaliṉ āṭavarnilai ciṟitu uyarvatāyiṟṟu. iṉṉōraṉṉa kāraṇaṅkaḷāl makkaḷulakil peṇṭiratu nilai ciṟitu aṭaṅkiya taṉmaittāka uḷatu. inta nilaimaiyiṉaip paḻaṅkāla makkaḷil cilar naṉku uṇarntu koṇṭu makaḷirait taṅkaḷ nalaṉukkākap paṭaikkappaṭṭavareṉak karuti oḻukiṉar.
nam tamiḻakam paṇṭai nāḷil piṟa nāṭukaḷōṭu toṭarpuṟṟu vāṇikam mutaliya tuṟaikaḷil ciṟantu viḷaṅkiyapōtu, mēlai nāṭukaḷil kirēkkarum, yavaṉarum, ekiptiyarum, pāricēyarum, pāpilōṉiyarum, piṟarum matippuṭaiya nākarikam koṇṭu viḷaṅkiṉar; avarkaḷuṭaiya varalāṟṟuk kuṟippukaḷum, araciyal neṟi muṟaikaḷum, aṟivuraikaḷum iṉṟum mēṉāṭṭavarāl pārāṭṭappaṭum takuti paṭaittuḷḷaṉa. avarkaḷuṭaiya vāḻvil, makaḷir nilai āṭavarkku aṭippaṇi puriyum tuṟaiyilēyē iruntatu. erōṭāṭṭacu mutaliyōr kuṟippukaḷāl āṇ makkaḷāl īṭṭappaṭum poruḷ vakaiyuḷ oṉṟākavē makaḷir karutappaṭṭamai terikiṟatu. makkaḷaip peṟutalum vaḷarttalum kuṭumpat toḻilkaḷaic ceytalumē avarkaṭkuc ciṟanta paṇikaḷākak kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ekiptiyarkaḷ makaḷir palarai maṇantukōṭal marapākak koṇṭaṉar. toṭakkattil aḵtu aracarpāl tōṉṟi nāḷaṭaivil yāvarkkum eytuvatāyiṟṟu. iculām camayam paraviya piṉ ekiptu maṉṉar makaḷir palarai maṇattal kaṭamaiyāk koṇṭaṉar. oruvaṉ tāṉkoṇṭa maṉaiviyar palarum avar vayiṟṟiṟ piṟakkum makkaḷum taṉakkuk kiṭaitta celvamākak karutiṉāṉ. avar aṉaivaruṭaiya uḻaippum kuṭumpattukkup poruḷ varuvāyāka ilaṅkiṟṟu. veṉṟa vēntar tōṟṟōṟpāl peṟappaṭum tiṟaip poruḷāka makaḷiraip peṟuvatu annāḷaiya iyalpu. vēntaraik kāṇac cellum talaivarkaḷum celvarkaḷum iḷamakaḷir palarai avarkaṭkup paricup poruḷākavum vaḻaṅkiṉareṉa ekiptu nāṭṭu varalāṟu kūṟukiṟatu. im maṉaiviyarkku vēṟāka iṟparattaiyarāka nūṟṟuk kaṇakkil makaḷirait tokuttu vaittal mēlai nāṭṭu aracar ceyalmuṟaiyāka iruntirukkiṟatu. iv vaḻakkam kirēkkar, yavaṉar mutal pāpilōṉiyar, kiciyar īṟāka ellōriṭaiyum iruntuḷatu. arapiyar māttiram it tuṟaiyil ōraḷavu uyarnta nōkkam koṇṭaṉar. āṇ makkaḷaip pōlap peṇmakkaḷum araciyal vakaikaḷilum pōrt tuṟaiyilum ciṟanta paṅku koṇṭaṉar. muṟai purital. paṭaikkalam payiṟal, paṭaittalaimai tāṅkal mutaliyavaṟṟil makaḷir āṭavarai yoppa viḷakka muṟṟaṉar. aṟivārāycci, icai, kūttu, ilakkiyap pulamai, iṉiya col vaṉmai eṉṟa nalampala vaṟṟil makaḷir ciṟappuṟṟut tikaḻntaṉar. āṭavar aṉaivarum maṇam purintukōṭal iṉṟiyamaiyātatu; maṇamākāmai ōr āṭavaṉukku iḻivaiyum paḻippaiyum nalkiṟṟu. makkaṭ pēṟṟuḷ āṇmakappēṟu kaṭavuḷpāl peṟalākum pēraruḷākak karutappaṭṭu vantatu.
mēlaināṭṭup pālai nilappakutikaḷil nāṭōṭikaḷāka vāḻnta makkaḷ peṇ makkaḷaip peruñcumaiyākavum vēṇṭāp poruḷākavum karutiṉar. paḻantamiḻariṭaiyē nilaviya niraikōṭal eṉṟa pōrttuṟaipōla, pālai nila maṟamakkaḷ pakaivaruṭaiya makaḷiraik kavarntukōṭalum piṉpu aṭimai kaḷāka avarkaḷai mēṟkoṇṭu naṭattalum ceytu vantamaiyiṉ, avarkaṭku makap pēṟṟuḷ peṇkaḷaip peṟutal veṟuppaiyum accattaiyum viḷaivittatu. peṇṇiṉattiṉ perukkattaik kuṟaippatu karutip peṇ makavukaḷait tām vaḻipaṭum teyvaṅ kaṭku uyirppali ceyvatum orucila makkaḷiṭaiyē oḻukalāṟāka iruntatu.
annāḷil namatu nāṭṭiṉ vaṭapakutiyil vāḻnta makkaḷum kirēkka yavaṉa ekiptiyarkaḷaip pōlap peṇkaḷai makappeṟuṅ karuviyum kaṇavaṉukku maṉaikkaṇ iruntu paṇipuriyum aṭimaiyu mākavē karutiṉar. oruvaṉ pala makaḷirai maṇattal vilakkappaṭa villai; aracar aintu makaḷirai maṉaiviyarākak koḷḷalām. avarkaṭku mēlum makaḷirai vēntar varaintu koḷvar; āyiṉum avarkaḷ maṉaiviyarākum takutiyiṉṟi urimai makaḷir eṉṟa peyarāl nilavuvar. mutal maṉaiviyirukka vēṟoruttiyai oruvaṉ maṇakka virumpiṉ, mutal maṉaiviyiṉ icaivaip peṟṟut tāṉ maṇantu koḷvāṉ. ivvaḻakku mēlai nāṭṭilum iruntatu. āpirakām eṉpavaṉ cārā eṉṟa maṉaivi yorutti iruppavum avaḷatu icaivu peṟṟu iraṇṭām maṉaiviyāka ākar eṉpavaḷai maṇantu koṇṭa varalāṟu itaṟkuc cāṉṟu pakarukiṉṟatu.
oruvaṉ oḻukkamum naṟpaṇpum ettuṇaic ciṟitum ilaṉāyiṉum pollāta kāmukaṉāyiṉum avaṉ maṉaivi avaṉaiyē teyvamākak karutip paṇiyuriya vēṇṭum eṉṟum, makaḷirkkeṉat taṉitta muṟaiyil vēḷviyō tavamō viratamō illaiyākalāṉ, kaṇavaṉukkuc ceyyum toṇṭum paṇiyumē makaḷirkku mēlulaka iṉpavāḻvu peṟutaṟku vāyilām eṉṟum, maṇamāṉa orutti taṉ peṟṟōr maṉaiyil iruntukoṇṭu taṉ kaṇavaṉukkup paṇintu avaṉ viruppiṟkēṟpa naṭavāḷāyiṉ, nāṭāḷum vēntaṉ cāṉṟōr kūṭiya pēravaiyil avaḷai niṟutti vēṭṭai nāykaḷ kaṭittut tuṉpuṟuttumāṟu avaḷai avaṟṟiṟku iraiyākka vēṇṭumeṉṟum, taṉ kaṇavaṉ naṟkuṇa naṉmāṇpukaḷai iḻantu pollāk kuṭiyaṉāyum nōyuṟṟa vaṉāyum māṟiyatu kāraṇamāka avaṉai veṟuttup puṟakkaṇip pāḷāyiṉ avaḷ mūṉṟu tiṅkaṭku uyariya āṭaiyaṇikalaṉiṉṟu vēṟōr taṉiyiṭattē otukkappaṭa vēṇṭumeṉṟum maṉunūl kūṟukiṟatu. kaṇavaṉ colvaḻi yaṭaṅki yoṭuṅki ōḻukuvatē makaḷir kaṭaṉ; atuvē avarkaṭku uyarnta aṟamām eṉa yāññavalkiyar iyampukiṉṟār. kaṇavaṉ uyirōṭu irukkumpōtē tāṉ pēṟu viratam mēṟkōṭalum vēḷvi ceytalum uṭaiyaḷāyiṉ, avaḷ taṉ kaṇavaṉ talaiyai veṭṭiṉavaḷākiṉṟāḷ eṉavum, puṇṇiya nīrāṭum orutti, taṉ kaṇavaṉuṭaiya aṭikaḷaiyō uṭal muḻuvatumō nīrāṭṭi annīraiyē uṭkoḷpavaḷ puttēḷir vāḷum ulakil peruñ ciṟappup peṟuvāḷ eṉavum attiri yeṉpār aṟivikkiṉṟār. maṟumaiyil kaṇavaṉ eytum katiyiṉum mēṟkatiyai maṉaiviyāvāḷ peṟuvatillai yākalāṉ, kaṇavaṉukku aṭaṅkātoḻukupavaḷ iṟantāl iṉpavulaku eytāḷ; eṉavē, avaḷ eṉṟum taṉ kaṇavaṉ maṉam nōka naṭavāḷātal vēṇṭum eṉṟu vāciṭṭanūl vaṟpuṟuttukiṉṟatu. kaṇavaṟku aṭaṅkātu oḻukupavaḷ kaiyil evarum uṇavu tarappeṟutal kūṭātu; avaḷ kāmi eṉak karutappaṭuvāḷ eṉa āṅkīracar kaṭikiṉṟār.
mēlai nāṭṭavaraip pōla nam nāṭṭu vaṭamoḻiyāḷarum makaḷirai uṭaimaip poruḷākavē karutiṉar. ēṉaip poruḷ kaḷaip pōla makaḷiraiyum cūtāṭu paṇaiyamākavum, viṟkavum oṟṟivaikkavum kūṭiya poruḷākavum matittoḻukiṉar. itaṟku itikācaṅkaḷum purāṇaṅkaḷum mikap pala ceytikaḷaic cāṉṟu kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. uyirkku iṟuti nērum kālattāyiṉ, cāṉṟōr iḻikkattakka ceyalkaḷaic ceytāyiṉum uyir uytal vēṇṭum eṉpatu annāḷaiya aṟamākaliṉ, an nilaiyil makaḷir aṟamallaṉa ceyyiṉum avar vilakkappaṭār. maṇamāṉa makaḷirkup pati viratamē ciṟanta aṟam. taṉ kaṇavaṉaiyaṉṟip piṟar tammait toṭiṉum tampāṟpaṭiṉum tammai virumpiṉum, maṉaivi yāvāḷ avarkaḷai maṉattāl virumpāmaiyē pativiratattiṉ paṇpu. kaṇavaṉ, āṇmaiyiḻappiṉum aṟivu tiṟampiṉum vēṟu nāṭēkiṉum perunōy eytiṉum maṉaiviyāyiṉāḷ vēṟōr āṭavaṉai maṇantu koḷḷalām. koṇṭa koḻunaṉ kuṭivaḻi yeñcum nilaiyil maṉaivi vēṟoruvaṉaik kūṭi makap peṟuvatu kuṟṟamiṉṟu eṉṟoru koḷkai paṇṭai nāḷil iruntamaikku itikācamākiya pāratam ēṟṟa varalāṟoṉṟaic cāṉṟu kūṟukiṟatu.
iṉi mēlaināṭṭu āṅkilariṭaiyē makaḷir irunta nilaiyai nōkkiṉ aḵtu ēṉai mēlai nāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟu vēṟupaṭṭa tākat tōṉṟavillai. araciyal vāḻvil araca makaḷirmaṭṭil aracikaḷāka muṭi cūṭik koḷḷum urimai peṟṟiruntaṉar. kiṟittu camayam paraviya piṉpē annāṭṭil oruvaṉ pala makaḷirai maṇakkum iyalvu nīṅkuvatāyiṟṟu. neṭuṅkālam varaiyil makaḷirkku annāṭṭavar kalvi nalkavillai. camayattuṟaiyil tāpata makaḷirkku lattīṉ moḻi kaṟpikkap peṟṟamaiyiṉ, ki.pi. āṟām nūṟṟāṇṭukkum pattām nūṟṟāṇṭukkum iṭaiyē tāpata makaḷir cilar lattīṉ moḻiyil ciṟanta pulamai peṟṟiruntaṉar. ēṉai yāvarum makaḷirkku maṇamum makappēṟumē vēṇṭappaṭuvaṉa eṉṟa koḷkaiyilē ūṉṟi niṉṟaṉar. maṇamākumuṉ oruttik kirukkum urimai muṟṟum maṇattiṟkup piṉ avaḷuṭaiya kaṇavaṉ pāl cērntu oṉṟiviṭṭaṉa. makkaḷaip pēṇutal kalvi kaṟpittal mutaliyaṉa paṟṟiya urimaikaḷ tantaiyiṭamē irun toḻintaṉa. varalāṟṟuk kālamtoṭṭu āṅkila naṭṭava riṭaiyē kaṇavaṟku vēṟāka makaḷirkku evvakaic cotturimaiyum illāmalē iruntu vantatu; ki.pi. 1882-il tāṉ makaḷirkkuc cottil urimai vaḻaṅkap peṟṟatu. aracāḷum vēntaṉ makkaḷuḷ makaḷirkku araciyal urimai paṇṭaiyiruntatu; āyiṉum am makkaḷuḷ āṇ makkaḷ iruppiṉ, avarkkē mutalurimai nilaviṟṟu. ceṉṟa 1918-il tāṉ makaḷirkku vākkurimai vaḻaṅkiṉar; ataṉ piṟakē āṅkila makaḷir pārāḷumaṉṟa uṟuppiṉarākum takuti peṟṟaṉar. amerikkar 1920-il avvurimaiyai makaḷirkku vaḻaṅkiṉar. uyariya kalviyum kalaiyum peṟutaṟkēṟṟa vāyppum āṅkila nāṭṭu makaḷirkkup pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutip pakutiyiltāṉ uṇṭāyiṟṟu. ivarkaḷaiyellām nōkkiṉ paṇṭai nāḷait tamiḻ makaḷiriṉ nilai peritum viyattaṟkuriya tāka viḷaṅkukiṟatu.
paḻantamiḻ makkaḷiṭaiyē āṭavar, peṇṭir eṉṟa irupālārkkum piṟappu, aṟivu, oḻukkam mutaliya kūṟukaḷil vēṟṟumai kāṇappaṭavillai. āṇ makkaḷai yoppavē peṇmakkaḷum pēṇappaṭṭuḷḷaṉar. kūrtta aṟivuṭai maiyē makkaḷil vēṟṟumai kāṭṭiniṉṟatu. tiruvaḷḷuvarum aṟivaṟinta makkaṭ pēṟṟaiyē ciṟanta pēṟāka eṭutturaik kiṉṟār. ataṟkuk kāraṇam, āṇāyiṉum peṇṇāyiṉum, makkaḷ tammiṉum mikka aṟivunalam uṭaiyarāyiṉum ulakiluḷḷa maṉṉuyirkkellām vāḻvu iṉitām eṉpatu karuttu. ataṉāl āṭavar peṇṭir irupālārum tiruntiya kalvi peṟuvatil vēṟupāṭiṉṟi yoḻukiṉar. peṇ makkaṭkuriya nalam kūṟappukka tolkāppiyar "ceṟivum niṟaiyum cemmaiyum ceppum, aṟivum arumaiyum peṇ pālāṉa" eṉṟu terivikkiṉṟār. kalvi kēḷvikaḷāl uṇṭākum tiṇṇiya aṟivoḻukkam kaṟpu eṉṟu tamiḻc cāṉṟōrāl kuṟikkappaṭum. āṭukōṭpāṭṭuc cēralātaṉ eṉṟa vēntaṉatu nalam kūṟappukunta cāṉṟōr, "ulakam tōṉṟiya mēmpaṭu kaṟpiṉ villōr meymmaṟai" eṉṟum, vētaṅkaḷaik kuṟikkak karutiya cāṉṟōr, avaṟṟai "eḻutāk kaṟpu" eṉṟum kuṟippatē ipporuṇmaikku ēṟṟa cāṉṟākum. makaḷirkkuk kalviyaṟiviṉ tiṇinilai yoḻukkattaik kaṟpeṉa vāḷā ōtāmal, "ceyirtīr kāṭcik kaṟpu" eṉṟu tolkāppiyar ciṟappittuk kūṟiyaruḷu kiṉṟār. kaṟpeṉṉum tiṇmaiyuṇṭāvatu makaḷirkkuriya takutikaḷuḷ perumai vāyntatu eṉṟu paṇṭait tamiḻar paṇittuḷḷaṉar.
makaḷir makappeṟutal eṉpatu iyaṟkaiyaṟam; ataṉāl uṭaṟkūṟu vēṟupaṭṭataṉṟi, āṇukku aṭimaiyāyt taṉakkeṉa urimaiyum ceyalumaṟṟiruttaṟkaṉṟu eṉpatu tamiḻ marapu. āṇṭavaṉ paṭaittaḷitta ivvulakil āṇaippōlap peṇṇum vāḻap piṟantamaiyiṉ āṇukku urimaitantu peṇ aṭimaiyāyk kiṭantu maṭiya vēṇṭumeṉpatu aṟamākātu. āṇ makkaḷai pōlātu peṇmakaḷ orutti maṉaiyiṉkaṇ ceṟippuṇṭirunta pōtu urimai vēṭkaiyāl untap peṟṟamaiyiṉ
"	viḷaiyāṭu āyamōṭu ōrai āṭātu
	iḷaiyōr illiṭattu iṟceṟin tiruttal
	aṟaṉum aṉṟu ākkamum tēym."
eṉṟu kūṟuvatu iṅkē niṉaivukūrat takuvatām. āṇukkup peṇṇum peṇṇukku āṇum vāḻkkait tuṇaiyāvaṉa. vāḻkkai irupālārkkum potu; iruvarum kūṭiyē ataṉaic ceytal iyaṟkaiyātalāl iruvarkkiṭaiyē vēṟṟumai kaṇṭu pukuttuvatu iyaṟkai vāḻkkaiyai iṭaiyūṟṟuk kuḷḷākkum eṉpatu kaṇṭē paṇṭaic cāṉṟōr maṉaiviyai vāḻkkait tuṇai eṉṟu vaḻaṅkiṉar. avaḷatu tuṇaimaiyiṉ ciṟappuṇarntu mēṟkoṇṭa kaṇavaṉaik koṇṭāṉ eṉṟu kuṟittaṉar.
paḻantamiḻar kuṭiyil kalvip pēṟṟil āṇum peṇṇum otta urimai peṟṟuḷḷaṉar. maṇṇāḷum vēntarkkuriya nīti naṉṉūl palavum peṇkaḷum kaṟṟiruntaṉar. piṟanta kuṭiyiṉ perumaiyaiyum pukunta kuṭiyiṉ tolvaravaiyum makaḷir naṉkuṇarntu kuṭipperumai kuṉṟāta vakaiyil vāḻkkaiyai naṭattukiṉṟaṉar. kaṇavaṉum maṉaiviyum ulakiyal vāḻkkaikku oruvark koruvar iṉṟiyamaiyāt tuṇaivarātaliṉ, kaṇavaṉ
pāl kāṇappaṭum kuṟaiyai makaḷirum makaḷiratu kuṟaiyaik kaṇavaṉum naṉku uṇarntu kuṇam pēṇi vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. kaṇavaṉ pāl piḻai kāṇumiṭattu aṉpuṭaiya naṉmoḻikaḷāl tāy pōl kaḻaṟikkūṟi nīkkiyum, nalam kāṇumiṭattu makiḻcci kūrntum, makaḷir naṭantu koḷvatu ciṟanta aṟamākak karutappaṭukiṟatu. "tāy pōṟ kaḻaṟit taḻī ikkōṭal, āymaṉaik kiḻattikkum urittu eṉa moḻipa" eṉat  tolkāppiyaṉār cuṭṭik kāṭṭukiṉṟār. koṇṭa kaṇavaṉpāl orukāl piḻai tōṉṟak kaṇṭa avaṉ maṉaivi tīviya iṉiya kūriya coṟkaḷāl avaṉaik kaḻaṟiṉāḷāka, ataṉāl avaṉ neñcu varuntiṉāṉ; an nilaiyil avviruvar vāḻkkaiyiṉ nalam pēṇi niṉṟa naṅkai yoruttipōntu, avaṉait teruṭṭi avaṉaik kaḻaṟik kūṟiya maṉaiviyiṉ urimaic ciṟappai eṭuttuk kāṭṭaluṟṟu,
"oṇṭoṭi maṭamakaḷ ivaḷiṉum, 	nuntaiyum yāyum kaṭiyarō niṉṉē" 1
eṉṟu kūṟiyatāka āciriyar ōrampōkiyār uraikkiṉṟār.
illiliruntu aṟam purintu oḻukumiṭattuk kaṇavaṉ, poruḷ ceytal, pōrviṉai mutaliya mēṟkoṇṭu cēṟal ākiya kaṭamai kāraṇamākap pirintu cellavēṇṭiṉ, kātal vaḻiniṟkum pirivut tuṉpam tōṉṟi maṉaiviyiṉ maṉattai varuttuvatu iyaṟkai. kātalukkum kaṭamaikkum iṭaiyē nikaḻum pōril kaṭamai vīḻcciyuṟiṉ atu kātal vāḻkkaiyil nalkuravaiyum veṟuppaiyum viḷaivittut tuṉpam payakkum eṉpataip paḻantamiḻk kaṇavaṉum maṉaiviyum naṉku uṇarntiruntaṉar. kaṇavaṉ piriyuṅkāl vaṟpuṟuttum coṟkaḷaiyē paṟṟukkōṭākak koṇṭu pirivut tuṉpattai āṟṟi maṉaikkaṇṇē tām ceyvaṉavaṟṟai muṭṭiṉṟic ceytoḻukuvatē maṉaiyuṟai makaḷirkku māṇpum kaṟpumām eṉpatu tamiḻmarapu.
maṉaiyiṉkaṇ vāḻuma makaḷir tām peṟṟamakkaṭku moḻi payiṟṟum mutaṟkalvi nalkumpōtē avarkaḷiṭattē nalloḻukkam vērūṉṟi vaḷarac ceytal tam kaṭaṉ eṉpatai uṇarntu oḻuku kiṉṟaṉar. viḷaiyāṭum paruvattaṉāṉa taṉ makaṉai nōkkiṉa oru tamiḻttāy, avaṉ tantaipāl uḷḷa kuṇanalaṅkaḷaiyum kuṟaikaḷaiyum eṭuttōti, kuṇaṅkaḷaik koṇṭu kuṟaikaḷaik kaiviṭa vēṇṭumeṉṟu kaṟpikkap pukunta,
cemmāl, vaṉappellām nuntaiyai oppiṉum nuntai nilaippāluḷ ottakuṟi eṉ vāykkēṭṭu otti,
eṉṟu muṉmoḻintu, kaṇavaṉ pōrval maṟavaṉ eṉṟum, pōraiyum ataṟkuriya aṟam piḻaiyātu ceytu veṟṟi koḷvatil vīṟuṭaiyaṉ eṉṟum kūṟuvāḷāy,
"	kaṉṟiya tevvark kaṭantu kaḷaṅkoḷḷum 	veṉṟi māṭṭu otti peruma."
eṉṟum, ivvāṟē avaṉuṭaiya naṭuvu nilaimai, īkaic ciṟappu ākiyavaṟṟaiyum,
	"pālkoḷa liṉṟip pakalpōl muṟaikku olkāk
kōlcemmai yotti peruma."	eṉṟum,
"	vītal aṟiyā viḻupporuḷ nacciyārkku
	ītalmāṭṭu otti peruma"			eṉṟum,
kūṟuvatu iṉṟaiya tāyarkku nalla paṭippiṉaiyākum. ivviyalpu paṟṟiyē, poṉmuṭiyār eṉṟa cāṉṟōr, orutāy taṉ makaṉukkuk kaṭamaiyuṇarvu nalkum kaṭṭurai vaṭivil,
"	īṉṟu puṟantarutal eṉtalaik kaṭaṉē
	cāṉṟō ṉākkutal tantaikkuk kaṭaṉē
	vēlvaṭittuk koṭuttal kollaṟkuk kaṭaṉē
	naṉṉaṭai nalkal vēntarkkuk kaṭaṉē
	oḷiṟuvāḷ aruñcamam murukkik
	kaḷiṟueṟintu peyartal kāḷaikkuk kaṭaṉē."
eṉṟu kaṭṭuraikkiṉṟār.
iṅṅaṉam tam kuṭiyil vāḻum makkaḷ kaṭamaiyuṇarvum nalloḻukkamum koṇṭu ciṟattal vēṇṭumeṉak karutum tamiḻ makaḷir, tām vāḻum nāṭṭiṉ nalattaiyum tam neñcil koṇṭu, vāytta pōtellām, "neṟpala polika poṉ peritu ciṟakka" "viḷaika vayalē varuka iravalar" "paci illākuka piṇi cēṉ nīṅkuka" "vēntu pakai taṇikayāṇṭu pala nantuka" 'aṟam naṉi ciṟakka allatu keṭuka" "aracu muṟai ceyka kaḷavu illākuka" "naṉṟu peritu ciṟakka tītu illākuka" "māri vāykka vaḷam naṉi ciṟakka" eṉa viḻaintu vēṇṭuvar.
makaḷiruṭaiya maṉaivāḻvil kaṇavaṉatu kātaluḷḷam taṉṉiṉ nīṅki vēṟu - makaḷirpāl cellumāyiṉ, am makaḷirkku ataṉiṉum mikka varuttam taruvatu vēṟu kiṭaiyātu. ulaka nāṭukaḷil eṅku nōkkiṉum makaḷirkkuriya iv viyalpu oṉṟākavē uḷḷatu, mēlai nāṭukaḷiluḷḷa ekiptu, apicīṉiyā mutaliya nāṭukaḷilum kīḻ nāṭṭuc cīṉam, cappāṉ nāṭukaḷilum uḷḷa makaḷir eppōtum kaṇavaṉuṭaiya maṉa niṟaivaiyē kuṟik
kōḷākak koṇṭu oḻukuvar. cila nāṭṭu makaḷir tām ceyyum
ovvoru ceyalum kaṇavaṟku uvakai tarukiṟatā eṉpatai aṭikkaṭi kēṭṭu aṟintu koḷvar. ataṉāl, kaṇavaṉatu kātalaṉpu piṟa makaḷirpāl cellātu. kāttoḻukuvatai avarkaḷ kaṇṇum karuttumākak koṇṭiruntaṉar. uruciya nāṭṭup paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷum, amerikka nāṭṭuc cevvintiyarum āṭavariṉum makaḷirait tāḻntavarākak karuti oḻukupavarāyiṉum, avar nāṭṭu makaḷirkkuk kaṇavaṉpāl parattataimai tōṉṟiṉ peritum varuntuvatai avar nāṭṭu varalāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu. cumēriyā nāṭṭil makaḷiriṭaiyē parattataimai yoḻukkam peritum paraviyiruntatu; eṉiṉum, maṉaiyaṟam pūṇṭa makaḷir kaṇavaṉ parattaṉāvatu kāṇa mikavum añci yoḻukukiṉṟaṉar.
mēlē kāṭṭiya paḻaṅkuṭikaḷōṭu evvakaiyilum piṟpaṭāta toṉmai vāynta tamiḻ makaḷir vilakkallarākaliṉ, kaṇavaṉatu parattataimai maṉaiyuṟaiyum makkaḷukku mikka varuttattai viḷaivittatu. taṉṉaik koṇṭa kaṇavaṉ taṉṉai
yoḻiyap piṟamakaḷiraik kaṉaviṉum karutāta pērāṇmaiyuṭai
yaṉākap peṟiṉ peṇṭir puttēḷir vāḻum ulakil peruñ ciṟappup peṟuvar eṉṟu poruḷ koḷḷattakka vakaiyil, "peṟṟāṟ peṟiṟ peṟuvar peṇṭir peruñciṟappup puttēḷir vāḻum ulaku" eṉṟa tirukkuṟaḷ amaintirukkiṟatu. kaṇavaṉ parattaṉāyiṉ atu taṉakkuc ciṟumai payappatōṭu taṉ kuṭikkum paḻi tarum eṉṟa karuttāl tamiḻ makaḷir avaṉ uḷḷam anneṟiyiṟ celvatai virumpavillai. "pūvōraṉaiyar makaḷir, vaṇṭōraṉaiyar āṭavar" eṉṟa karuttoṉṟum annāḷil makkaḷiṭaiyē nilaviyiruntatu. eṉṟālum makaḷiruḷḷam kaṇavaṉatu kātal piṟamakaḷirpāl paravuvataic ciṟitum uṭaṉpaṭavillai.
ippōtu paḻaṉiyeṉa vaḻaṅkum ūrkku muṉṉāḷil tiruvāvi naṉkuṭi eṉṟum paḻaṅkālattil potiṉi yeṉṟum peyarkaḷ vaḻaṅkiṉa. ataṉarukē āykkuṭi yeṉṟa ōr ūruḷatu; ataṉ uṇmaip peyar āvikuṭi eṉpatu, ataṉait talainakarākak koṇṭa ap pakutiyil āviyar eṉpār vāḻntaṉar. ataṉāl ataṉai vaiyāvi nāṭu eṉpar; paṇṭai nāḷil aṅkē perum
pēkaṉ eṉṟa aracaṉ āṭci purintāṉ. avaṉai vaiyāvik kōpperumpēkaṉ eṉpatu vaḻakkam; avaṉuṭaiya maṉaivi peyar kaṇṇaki eṉpatu. orukāl avaṉukku vēṟoru peṇṇiṉ toṭar puṇṭāyiṟṟu. ataṉāl kaṇṇakikku maṉakkavalai periyatāyiṟṟu. pēkaṉ aruḷ uḷḷam paṭaitta peruntakai; kāṭṭil vāḻum mayil oṉṟukkup pōrvai yītta pukaḻāḷaṉ; "paṭāam maññaikku ītta eṅkō, kaṭāa yāṉaik kalimāṉ pēkaṉ" eṉṟu cāṉṟōr avaṉaip pārāṭṭuvatu vaḻakkam. avaṉukku aricil kiḻār, kapilar, paraṇar eṉṟa cāṉṟōr palar iṉiya naṇparāvar. kaṇṇaki avarkaṭkup pēkaṉatu parattaimaiyaic colli avaṉai anneṟiyiṉiṉṟum nīkkumāṟu muyaṉṟāḷ. orukāl avaḷ maṉaikkup pōnta paraṇar eṉṟa nallicaic cāṉṟōrkku,
"	empōl orutti nalaṉ nayantu eṉṟum
	varūum eṉpa viyaṅku pukaḻp pēkaṉ
	olleṉa olikkum tēroṭu
	mullai vēli nallūrāṉē" 1
eṉṟu muṟaiyiṭṭaṉaḷ. avarkaḷum avaḷatu vēṇṭukōṭku iṇaṅki, pēkaṉaik kaṇṭu vēntē, nī maṉaiyaṟam māṇpuṟac ceytal vēṇṭum; nī iṉṟiravē niṉ maṉaiyuṟaiyum kaṇṇakipāl ceṉṟu avaḷ eyti varuntum tuyarattaip pōkkiyaruḷal vēṇṭum eṉpārāy,
"	aṟañ ceytīmē aruḷvey yōyeṉa
	iḵtuyām iranta paricil; aḵtu iruḷiṉ
	iṉamaṇi neṭuntē rēṟi
	iṉṉātu uṟaivi arumpaṭar kaḷaimē."
eṉṟu vēṇṭiṉar. avaṉum avvaṇṇamē ceytu aṟavāḻkkaiyil nilai peṟuvāṉāyiṉāṉ. avaṉatu vaiyāvi nāṭṭiṉ pukaḻ vicaya nakara vēntar kālam varait tamiḻakattil ciṟantu niṉṟatu; ak kālattil vaiyāvi vaiyāpuri eṉac citaintu perum piṟitāki maṟaintatu. niṟka, kōvalaṉ maṉaiviyākiya kaṇṇakiyār tam kaṇavaṉ cilampu tiruṭiya kaḷvaṉ eṉṟu paḻi tūṟṟappaṭṭup pāṇṭi vēntaṉāl kolaiyuṇṭāṉ eṉpatu kēṭṭu ataṉāl tam celvak kuṭikkuṇṭāṉa pukaḻ mācu paṭuvatu niṉaintu vēntaṉ muṉceṉṟu vaḻakkuraitta varalāṟu nāṭaṟinta toṉṟu.
varalāṟṟuk kālattukku muṉpiruntē tamiḻ makkaḷ pōr maṟattukkup peyarum māṇpum piṟaṅkap peṟṟavar. kirēkka, pāpilōṉiya ekiptiya makaḷiraip pōlat tamiḻ makaḷir pōrp pukaḻaip peritum virumpiṉar. pōril puṟaṅkoṭuttuk kuṭikkup paḻi viḷaittāṉ taṉ vayiṟṟiṟ piṟanta makaṉ eṉiṉ avaḷatu maṟavuḷḷam ciṟitum poṟātu. pōrukkuc ceṉṟirunta iḷaiyōṉ oruvaṉaip paṟṟi avaṉ tāyiṭam pōntu poyyāka, "aṉṉāy, niṉmakaṉ pakaivar toṭutta pōril mutuku tantu ōṭiṉaṉ" eṉṟu ūravar alar kūṟiṉar; atu kēṭṭatum, avaṉ tāyiṉ maṟavuḷḷam kotittatu; "ūravar kūṟuvatu uṇmaiyāyiṉ, avaṉ vāy vaittup pāluṇṭa eṉ mārpai aṟutteṟivēṉ" eṉṟu vañciṉam kūṟip pōrk kaḷam ceṉṟu aṅkē iṟantu kiṭakkum vayavar uṭalaṅkaḷaip puraṭṭik kāṇaluṟṟāḷ avaṟṟiṉ iṭaiyē, mutukiṟ puṇpaṭātu mukattilum mārpilum puṇpaṭṭuc citaintu vēṟupaṭṭuk kiṭanta avaṉ uṭampiṉaik kaṇṭu pēruvakai koṇṭāḷ. itaṉaik kākkai pāṭiṉiyār eṉṟa perumāṭṭi,
			"mutiyōḷ ciṟuvaṉ 
paṭaiyaḻintu māṟiṉaṉ eṉṟupalar kūṟa
maṇṭamarkku uṭaintaṉa ṉāyiṉ, uṇṭaeṉ
mulaiyaṟut tiṭuveṉ yāṉeṉac ciṉaiik
koṇṭavāḷoṭu paṭupiṇam peyarāc
ceṅkaḷam tuḻavuvōḷ citaintuvē ṟākiya
paṭumakaṉ kiṭakkai kāṇūu
īṉṟa ñāṉṟiṉum perituvan taṉaḷē."
eṉṟu pāṭik kāṭṭukiṉṟār. vēṟoruttiyiṉ tantai muṉporukāl naṭanta tumpaip pōril uyir koṭuttup pukaḻ koṇṭāṉ; avaḷ kaṇavaṉ muṉṉāḷ naṭanta karantaip pōril niraikāvalil uyir koṭuttāṉ. maṟunāḷ pōr nikaḻcci terivikkum paṟaiyōcai kēṭṭatum taṉ oru makaṉaiyum pōrk kōlam ceytu vēṟpaṭaiyaik kaiyil tantu cerumukam celka eṉa viṭuttāḷ; itaṉai,
"	iṉṟum, ceruppaṟai kēṭṭu viruppuṟṟu mayaṅki
	vēlkaik koṭuttu veḷitu virittuṭīip
	pāṟumayirk kuṭumi eṇṇey nīvi
	orumaka ṉallatu illōḷ
	cerumuka nōkkic celkeṉa viṭumē."
eṉṟu cāṉṟōr eṭutturaikkiṉṟaṉar.
ivvāṟu tammuṭaiya maṉaikkaṇ uḷḷa āṭavar pōril maṟam kuṉṟātu porutu pukaḻ peṟutalai virumpum tamiḻmakaḷir pōriṟ puṇpaṭṭuvarum vayavarkku maruttuvam ceyvatil mikka māṇpuṟukiṉṟaṉar. kiḻinta puṇkaḷait taittalum maruntiṭutalum iṉiya paṇṇil icaipāṭutalum maruttuvat tuṟaiyil kaiyāḷap
paṭukiṉṟaṉa. puṇṇuṟṟa maṟavarkku maruttuvam ceyyappeṟum maṉaikaḷaippēy mutaliya tīkkōḷ vantu tākkāvaṇṇam maṉaiyiṟaippil īra ilaiyum vēppilaiyum cerukappaṭṭirukkum. perum puṇ uṟṟavar uḷḷam ulakiyal toṭarpil viṭutalai peṟṟut tuṟakkaviṉpattil nāṭṭam koḷḷumāṟu kāñcip
pāṭṭukaḷ iṉiya muṟaiyil pāṭappaṭum.
pōrttuṟaiyilum piṟavaṟṟilum pukaḻmikku viḷaṅkiya kātaṟ kaṇavaṉ iṟantupaṭiṉ, avaṉ pirivāṟṟāta tamiḻmakaḷir avaṉuṭaṉē uyir tuṟappatuṇṭu. kaṇavaṉōṭē tāmum uyiriḻantāl maṟupiṟappil ak kaṇavaṉukkē maṉaiviyāyp piṟattal kūṭum eṉṟu paṇṭait tamiḻmakaḷir niṉaittoḻukiṉar. "immai māṟi maṟumaiyāyiṉum, nīyākiyar emkaṇavaṉai; yāṉākiyar niṉ neñcu nērpavaḷē" eṉṟu makaḷir kūṟuvatākac cāṉṟōr pāṭuvatu itaṟku ēṟṟa cāṉṟātal kāṇka. itaṉāl iṟanta kaṇavaṉai erikkum īmattil kātalaṉpuṭaiya makaḷir uṭaṉ vīḻntu uyirkoṭukkum ceyalai mēṟkoṇṭaṉar. uyirkoṭuppōruḷ mikka iḷamai nalam paṭaittavar tīppāytalai virumpāta cāṉṟōrum uṇṭu. pūtap pāṇṭiyaṉ iṟantatu kaṇṭa avaṉ uyirkkātaliyāṉa peruṅkōp peṇṭu avaṉuṭaiya īmattīyil tāṉum vīḻntu cātalukkut tuṇintāḷāka, āṅkirunta cāṉṟōr avaḷai vilakki aṟivurai pala kūṟuvarāyiṉar. avarkaḷai vekuṇṭu,
"	peruṅkāṭṭup paṇṇiya karuṅkōṭṭu īmam
	numakkuari tākuka tilla emakkuem
	peruntōḷ kaṇavaṉ māynteṉa arumpaṟa
	vaḷḷitaḻ aviḻnta tāmarai
	naḷḷirum poykaiyum tīyum ōraṟṟē."
eṉṟu peruṅkōp peṇṭu avalittu araṟṟiṉāḷ. uyir
koṭukkum āṟṟal illāmaiyālō cāṉṟōr palar vilakkiyataṉālō kaṇavaṉai iḻantu uyir tāṅki yiruppōr kaimmai nōṉpu mēṟkoṇṭu kaṇavaṉukku vaḻipāṭu ceytu oḻukuvar. itaṉait tumpaiyūrc cokiṉaṉār eṉṟa cāṉṟōr,
"	aṭaṅkiya kaṟpiṉ āynutal maṭantai
	uyarnilai yulakam avaṉpuka āra
	nīṟāṭu cuḷakiṉ cīṟiṭam nīkki
	aḻuta lāṉāk kaṇṇaḷ
	meḻukum āppikaṇ kaluḻnī rāṉē."
eṉṟu kuṟikkiṉṟār.
koḻunaṉai yiḻanta kulamakaḷir mēlē kuṟittavāṟu uṭaṉuyir viṭutal, kaimmai mēṟkoḷal eṉṟa iraṇṭaṉuḷ oṉṟaṉai mēṟkoḷvarēyaṉṟi maṟumaṇam ceytukoḷvatu ilar. paḻantamiḻ makaḷir maṟumaṇam ceytukoḷḷaṟkup paṇṭaip poruḷilakkaṇa nūlār vitikkavum illai! maṟumaṇam ceytu koṇṭa varalāṟum tamiḻ nūlkaḷil itu kāṟum kāṇappaṭa
villai. immaiyiṟ pirinta kaṇavaṉai maṟupiṟappil peṟalām eṉṟa karuttunilavum camutāyamātaliṉ, paḻantamiḻiriṭaiyē maṟumaṇam nikaḻtaṟku vāyppu ētu?
"	alarnta virinī ruṭukkai yulakam peṟiṉum
	aruneṟi yāyar makaḷirkku
	irumaṇam kūṭutal illiyal paṉṟē."
eṉṟa mullaip pāṭṭu itaṟkut takka cāṉṟākum. kaḷavu neṟiyil kātaluṟṟuk kaṟpu neṟiyil maṇam ceytu koṇṭu kaṇavaṉum maṉaiviyum oruvarkkoruvar vāḻkkait tuṇaiyāy oḻukum makkaḷātaliṉ maṟumaṇa nikaḻcci paḻantamiḻar vāḻvil iṭam peṟātāyiṟṟu.
maṇantu koṇṭa kaṇavaṉ pōr mutaliyavaṟṟāl iṟuti eytātu mutumai yeytuṅkāl, avaṉ uḷḷattē itukāṟum nukarntu pōnta nukarccikaḷil uvarppum veṟuppum piṟantu tuṟavu mēṟkoḷḷum vēṭkaiyai yuṇṭupaṇṇum. ikkaruttaiyum ceyalaiyum kāṇum cāṉṟōr avarkku vīṭu pēṟṟukkuriya meyyuṇarvaiyum tavattiṉ ceyal muṟaiyaiyum aṟivuṟuttuvar. ataṉāl, avarkaḷ tamakkut tuṇaiyākiya maṉaiviyuṭaṉ tuṟavu mēṟkoṇṭu meyyuṇarntu, avā aṟuttu, yāṉ eṉatu eṉṟa cerukkaṟṟu, aṟivu nūlkaḷaik kaṇṭu cemporuḷākiya kaṭavuḷiṉpa vāḻvukku uriyarāvar. itu paṟṟiyē tolkāppiyar, "aṟampuri cuṟṟamoṭu kiḻavaṉum kiḻattiyum, ciṟantatu payiṟṟal iṟantataṉ payaṉē" eṉṟu vaṟpuṟuttiṉar. iṟattal eṉṟatu vayatu mutirtal.
oruvaṉukku orutti yeṉṟa muṟaiyil tamiḻ makkaḷ ulaku iṇaippuṇṭiruntamai paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷ uraikkum uṇmai
yākum. āyiṉum, annāḷil nāṭukaṭkiṭaiyē aṭikkaṭi nikaḻnta pōrkaḷāl āṇ makkaḷ tokai kuṟaintatu; pōril īṭupaṭāmaiyum pātukākkap paṭutalumākiya ceyalkaḷāl makaḷir tokai mikuntatu. peṇṭiraik kollalākātu eṉpatu paḻan
tamiḻar pōraṟam. itē nilai mēlaināṭukaḷilum nilaviyatāl, mikuntu niṉṟamakaḷirai maṉaiviyākavum urimai makaḷirākavum koḷvatu avarkaḷ nāṭṭu vēntarkaṭkum, celvarkaṭkum ōrāṟṟāl kaṭamaiyum marapum āyiṉa. antac cūḻnilaiyē tamiḻ nilattum iruntamaiyiṉ, mikai makaḷiraip parattaiyareṉak kuṟippārāyiṉar. param eṉṟa col mikutiyeṉṟu poruḷ taruvatu. immakaḷir ulakil vāḻtaṟkuriyarātalāl, avaratu vāḻvukku vēṇṭuvaṉa utavuvatu āṇmaiyuṭaiya āṭavarkku aṟamākum. avvāṟu utavumiṭattum utavi peṟumiṭattum irutiṟattārkkum toṭarpuṇṭāka, ataṉ vaḻiyākak tōṉṟiya makaḷir parattaiyarāyp perukiṉar. oruvaṉ oruttiyai maṇantu vāḻntu varuṅkāl avaḷpāl makappēṟu illaiyāyiṉ vēṟoruttiyai maṇantu koḷvatu avarkku amaivatāyiṟṟu. iraṇṭām maṉaiviyaip "piṉ muṟaiyākkiya perum poruḷ vatuvai" eṉṟu tolkāppiyar kuṟikkiṉṟār. iruvarkku mēlum maṉaiviyar uḷarāyiṉ avaraik kāmak kiḻattiyareṉa vakuttu muṟai ceytaṉar paṇṭait tamiḻar. nāḷum perukivanta mikai makaḷir tām iṉitu vāḻtaṟkuriya poruḷ vēṇṭi āṭal pāṭal aḻaku eṉṟa tuṟaikaḷil payiṟci peṟattoṭaṅkiṉar. avaruḷ poruḷ oṉṟē karuti ataṉait taruvōr yāvarāyiṉum varaiyaṟaiyiṉṟit tamatu peṇmaiyai viṟṟu oḻukiṉōr varaivil makaḷirākak karutappaṭṭaṉar. eñciya parattaiyar tammai nayanta āṭavaṉatu kātalaṉpu oṉṟē kuṟikkoṇṭu oḻukiṉamaiyiṉ avaraik kātaṟparattaiyareṉap piṟkālattār peyar kuṟittaṉar. avaraic cila āṭavar tām uṟaiyum  ūrakattē irutti maṉaiviyaraip pōlap pēṇiṉar. avvāṟu pēṇappaṭṭōr iṟparattaiyar eṉappaṭuvarāyiṉar. ēṉaiyōr parattaiyar cērikkaṇ uṟaintamaiyiṉ avaraic cērip parattaiyareṉavum parattaiyar eṉavum uraittaṉar. iṟparattaiyar kāppiyak kālattil urimai makaḷireṉavum kuṟikkappaṭṭaṉar. ivvakaiyil tamiḻ makaḷir paṇṭai nāḷil kulamakaḷireṉavum parattaiyareṉavum irukūṟāy iruntamai teḷiyappaṭum.
oruvaṉ maṉaiviyāṉa kulamakaḷ vayiṟṟil piṟakkum makkaḷukkē avaṉatu uṭaimaiyil urimaiyuṇṭu; parattaiyarkkup piṟakkum makkaṭku avvurimai illai. itaṉai,
"	yāṇar ūraniṉ māṇiḻai makaḷirai
	emmaṉait tantunī taḻīiyiṉum avartam
	puṉmaṉat tuṇmaiyō aritē; avarum
	paintoṭi makaḷiroṭu ciṟuvarp payantu
	naṉṟi cāṉṟa kaṟpōṭu
	empā ṭātal ataṉiṉum ilamē."1
eṉṟu cāṉṟōr kūṟuvatāl iṉitaṟiyalām.
maṇavāḻkkai iyalil makaḷir ivvāṟu iru vēṟu vakaiyil iyaṉṟu vāḻntaṉarāyiṉum, iyal icai kūttu mutaliyavaṟṟilum, aṟivu araciyal mutaliya tuṟaikaḷilum otta urimai koṇṭiruntaṉar. ataṉāl araciyaṟ kuṟippukaḷum aṟivu nilaipeṟṟa ilakkiyaṅkaḷum, tamiḻ makaḷiriṉ cālpiṉai oru vēṟṟumaiyum tōṉṟātavāṟu kuṟittuḷḷaṉa. ōḷavaiyār, kākkaipāṭiṉiyār, nacceḷḷaiyār, veṇṇik kuyattiyar/ nappacalaiyār eṉap pala makaḷir nallicaip pulamait tuṟaiyil ciṟappuṟṟu viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. oḷavaiyārum, nacceḷḷaiyārum aracarkaṭku araciyaṟ cuṟṟamāyiruntu aṟivuraiyum aṟavuraiyum nalkiyuḷḷaṉar.
makaḷirkkuriya ic ciṟappum urimaiyum ki.pi. patiṉāṉkām nūṟṟāṇṭu varaiyil iruntuḷḷaṉa. ak kālattē vaṭaārkkāṭu māvaṭṭattup paṭaivīṭṭut talainakarākak koṇṭatoru ciṟṟaracu ciṟantu niṉṟatu. avvaraciṉar tammaic campuvarāyar eṉṟu kuṟippar; avarkaḷ cōḻap pēraraciṉ kīḻp perumai peṟṟavar. avaruḷ etirilic cōḻa campuvarāyaṉ eṉpavaṉ āṭcik kālattil vantavācikku aṇmaiyil uḷḷa teḷḷāṟu eṉṟa ūril kaṇṭaṉ iḷaṅkaṇṇaṉ eṉpavaṉ vāḻntuvantāṉ. avaṉuṭaiya maṉaivi karuṇaiyāṭṭi kaṟpāl tāṉ vāḻum ūravar taṉ kuṭiyaip pukaḻumāṟu vāḻntu vantāḷ. iḷaṅkaṇṇaṉ oru nāḷ kaṭkuṭiyāl aṟivu mayaṅki ūravar palarai vaitāṉ eṉṟu kuṟṟaṅkaṇṭa "makācapaiyār" ūrvāriyat talaimaipūṇṭa karuṇaiyāṭṭikku muṟaiyiṭa, avar "kaṇavaṉeṉṟum pārātu ēḻunāḷ tirumūla nāyaṉārkku viḷakkēṟṟumāṟu" paṇittu, ataṉai "avaṉ peṇṭāṭṭi eṉṟa paricu muṭṭāmē viḷakkēṟṟi"t tamatu kaṭamaiyai āṟṟiṉār eṉpatu varalāṟu. itaṉāl, ūrvāriyaṅkaḷil talaimai tāṅkum urimai makaḷirkkum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭiruntatu teḷivākiṟataṉṟō?
innilaiyil mēlaināṭṭu āṅkilēyarum piṟarum amerikka nāṭṭavarum inta nūṟṟāṇṭiltāṉ makaḷirkku araciyal urimai tantaṉar. itai nōkkiṉ paḻantamiḻ makaḷir ēṉai ulaka nāṭukaḷil vāḻnta makaḷirai nōkka - mika uyarnta nilaiyil vaittut tamiḻarāl matikkap peṟṟamai, itaṉāl iṉitu viḷaṅkum. iṉṉōraṉṉa ceytikaḷai maṟantamaiyāl iṭaikkālat tamiḻmakkaḷ makaḷirai aṭimaiyākki iruḷil kiṭattit tammaiyum kuruṭarākavum ūmaiyākavum ceviṭarākavum keṭuttuk koṇṭaṉar. tamatu nāṭu, moḻi mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ varalāṟu tuṟantu maṟantu keṭum oru camutāyam nāḷaṭaivil keṭṭu aṭimaic cēṟṟil aḻuntum eṉpatataṟku iṉṟaiya tamiḻc camutāyam takkatoru eṭuttukkāṭṭāka viḷaṅkuvatait tamiḻakam naṉku uṇartal vēṇṭum. avvuṇarvu tamiḻ vāḻvu taḻaippataṟku ākkamum araṇumām eṉpatu uṟuti.



10. ēṭṭil illāta ilakkiyam
- paḻamoḻikaḷ -
nam tamiḻakattil ēṭṭil eḻutappaṭāmal makkaḷ pēccu vaḻakkilēyē cila ilakkiyaṅkaḷ nilavukiṉṟaṉa. avai poruḷ ceṟinta ciṟu ciṟu coṟkaḷāl iyaṉṟuḷḷa paḻamoḻikaḷākum. avai ēṭṭil eḻutappaṭāviṭṭālum 'ēṭṭil eḻutappaṭṭuk kaṟṟavarkaḷāl pārāṭṭappaṭṭum, ilakkiya vakaikaḷil oṉṟākak karutappaṭṭum, tamiḻp periyōrkaḷāl pōṟṟappeṟṟum varukiṉṟaṉa.' avaikaḷaip paḻamoḻi eṉpatōṭu mutumoḻi eṉṟum mūturai eṉṟum cāṉṟōr vaḻaṅkuvar.
'appaṉaippōlap piḷḷai', eṉpatu nāṭṭil vaḻaṅkum paḻamoḻikaḷil oṉṟu. itaṉait tolkāppiyarē tam tolkāppiyattil 'tantaiya roppar makkaḷ eṉpatāṉal antamil ciṟappiṉ makappaḻittu neruṅkalum," eṉṟu kaṟpiyal cūttiram āṟil kuṟittuḷḷār. "kumpiṭṭa teyvam koṭumai ceyyumā," eṉpatoru paḻamoḻi. itaṉai nallantuvaṉār eṉṟa cāṉṟōr "vaḻipaṭṭa teyvantāṉ valiyeṉac cārntārkaṭku aḻiyumnōy kaimmika aṇaṅkākiyatu pōla," (kali) eṉṟu kūṟiyirukkiṉṟār. "iṟaikkiṟa ūṟṟut tāṉ curakkum," eṉpatu maṟṟoru mutumoḻi. itai nam tiruvaḷḷuvar "maṟaippēṉmaṉ yāṉ iḵtōr nōyai iṟaippavarkku ūṟṟunīr pōla mikum," eṉṟu tamatu tirukkuṟaḷil amaittu vaḻaṅki yirukkiṉṟār. ivvaṇṇam tamiḻilakkiya ulakil pukaḻpeṟṟa cāṉṟōr aṉaivarum eḻutā ilakkiyamākiya immutumoḻikaḷai uyarnta maṇikaḷākak karuti tāṅkaḷ eḻutiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷākiya aṇikalaṉkaḷil vaittu iḻaittu aḻaku ceytirukkiṉṟārkaḷ.
ippaḻamoḻikaḷ nuṭpamum curukkamum eḷimaiyumākiya iṉiya nalaṅkaḷai yuṭaiyavākum. ivaikaḷaik kaṇṭa tolkāppiyar ivaikaḷaiyum ceyyuḷ vakaiyil oṉṟāka eṭuttu nuṇmaiyum curukkamum oḷiyuṭaimaiyum eṉṟivai viḷaṅkattōṉṟik kuṟitta poruḷai muṭittaṟku "varūum ētu nutaliya mutumoḻi eṉpa," eṉṟu ilakkaṇam kūṟiyuḷḷār. mēlum avar "mutumoḻi eṉpa," eṉṟataṉāl tolkāppiyar kālattukku muṉpiruntē cāṉṟōrkaḷ inta mutumoḻikaḷaik kaṇṭu pārāṭṭiyirukkiṉṟaṉar eṉṟu aṟiyalām.
intap paḻamoḻikaḷ ellām uṇmai nikaḻccikaḷaik kuṟippavai. ivaṟṟil poyyō, puṉainturaiyō kiṭaiyā. aḻakiya collāṭciyum piṟitumoḻital mutaliya aṇinalamum ivaṟṟiṉ taṉicciṟappākum. oruvar vēṟoruvaraik kēṭṭu yātēṉum oṉṟaik kaṭaṉāka vāṅkukiṟapōtu mukam oruvāṟu makiḻcciyākavum, ataiyē koṭuttavar kēṭṭu vāṅkumpōtu tarupavar mukam oruvāṟu vēṟupaṭṭum iruppatum uṇmai nikaḻcciyām. ituvē "kēṭkiṟapōtu pacappu, koṭukkiṟapōtu kacappō," eṉṟa paḻamoḻiyāka vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. itaṉai ippōtum kāṇkiṉṟōm. 2000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉpu viḷaṅkiyirunta pālai pāṭiya peruṅkaṭuṅkō eṉpavar "uṇkaṭaṉ vaḻimoḻintu irakkuṅkāl mukaṉumtām, koṇṭatu koṭukkuṅkāl mukaṉum vēṟākutal, paṇṭum ivvulakat tiyaṟkai aḵtu iṉṟum, putuvataṉṟē pulaṉuṭai māntīr," (kali) eṉṟu pāṭiyuḷḷār.
inta mutumoḻikaḷiṭattē uṇmaiyum teḷivum iruppatu
koṇṭē cumār 1500 āṇṭukaṭku muṉpu muṉṟuraiyaraiyaṉār eṉṟa cāṉṟōr oruvar 400 paḻamoḻikaḷai eṭuttu ovvoṉṟiṉ karuttaiyum ovvoru veṇpāvil vaittup paḻamoḻi nāṉūṟu eṉṟa peyarvaittu oru nūl pāṭiyirukkiṉṟār. atu patiṉeṇ kīḻkkaṇakku eṉṟa nītinūl tokaiyil oṉṟāy irukkiṟatu. mēlum, ippaḻamoḻikaḷ makkaḷuṭaiya camutāyam, poruḷātāram, vāṇikam, kaittoḻil, camayam, kalvi, araciyal mutaliya pala tuṟaikaḷilum amaintirukkiṉṟaṉa.  "kulaṅkeṭṭavarōṭu campantam ceytālum kuṇam  keṭṭa varōṭu campantam ceyyakkūṭātu." "cetta piṇattaiyum cīriṭṭaḻu," mutaliyaṉa camutāyattil vaḻaṅkuvaṉa.
"paṇamillātavaṉ piṇam" "paṇam pattum ceyyum," mutaliyaṉa poruḷ paṟṟi vaḻaṅkum paḻamoḻi. "kappalēṟippaṭṭa kaṭaṉ koṭṭai nūṟṟut tīrumā?"  "tiraikaṭa lōṭiyum tiraviyam tēṭu," mutaliyaṉa vāṇikattil vaḻaṅkum mutumoḻikaḷ. "vallavaṉukkup pullum āyutam," "uḻaippāḷikku oruttar cōṟupōṭa vēṇṭiyatillai," eṉpavai mutaliyaṉa toḻil cārpāka nilavum mūturaikaḷ. "vitaip paḻutu mutaṟpaḻutu" "pārkkāta payirum kēṭkāta kaṭaṉum pāḻ," eṉpavai mutaliyaṉa uḻavupaṟṟi vaḻaṅkum ulakurai. "avaṉē avaṉē, eṉpataiviṭac 'civaṉē civaṉē' eṉpatu mēl", "iravellām irāmāyaṇam kēṭṭuviṭṭu viṭintapiṉ cītaikku irāmaṉ ciṟṟappaṉ eṉṟār pōla," eṉpaṉa camayam paṟṟi vaḻaṅkum paḻamoḻi. "tōṭṭimutal toṇṭai māṉ varai," eṉpatu iṭaikkālac cōḻapāṇṭiyar kāla araciyal paṟṟiyum  "ṭillikku rācāvāṉālum tallikkup piḷḷai," yeṉpatu vicayanakara vēntar kālattum mukammatiya vēntar kālattum nilaviya araciyal paṟṟiyum "nāṭṭukku nallaturai vantālum tōṭṭikkup pulcumai pōkātu," "paraṅkikkut teriyumā caṭaṅkum cāttiramum," eṉpavai mutaliyaṉa mēṉāṭṭavar kālattu araciyal paṟṟiyum uṇṭāki nilavum paḻamoḻikaḷ. araciyal cīrtiruttam vanta piṉ tērtalukku niṉṟu ōṭṭup peṟupavarkaḷaip paṟṟi "ōṭṭukkup pōṉāyō ōṭuvāṅkap pōṉāyō!" eṉpatu mutaliya paḻamoḻikaḷ uṇṭākiyiruk
kiṉṟaṉa.
inta paḻamoḻikaḷai nōkkuvōmāyiṉ, paḻaṅkālak kuṟippukaḷil pala kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṭaikkālap pāṇṭivēntaruḷ 12, 13-nūṟṟāṇṭil aracupurinta vīrapāṇṭiyaṉ kālattil tirukkoṭuṅkuṉṟattup pakutiyil vāṇātirāyar eṉpavar vantu kuṟumpuceytu ūrkaḷaic cūṟaiyāṭic ceṉṟaṉar. kōyil cottukkaḷaip pātukāppataṟku akkālattil taṉittaṉik kaḻakaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. avaṟṟil kōyiṟ paṇiceyyum civappirāmaṇarkaḷum vayirākikaḷumiruppar. appōtu tirukkoṭuṅkuṉṟattuk kōyilil poṉṉappa aṇukka vaṉṟoṇṭa vayirāki eṉpavaṉ iruntu vantāṉ. cūṟaiyāṭa vanta vāṇātirāyarkkum vaṉṟoṇṭa vayirākikkum caṇṭai uṇṭāyiṟṟu. kōyil cottukkaḷaik kākkum vakaiyil vaṉṟoṇṭa vayirāki mikka valiyuṭaṉ pōrceytu vāṇātirāyarait tōṟṟōṭac ceytu tāṉum puṇpaṭṭu veṟṟi peṟṟu viḷaṅkiṉāṉ. ūravar avaṉ puṇṇaiyāṟṟi avaṉukkuc ciṟappuc ceytārkaḷ eṉṟu iṭaiyāttūrk kōyil kalveṭṭeṉṟu kūṟukiṟatu. (P.S.Ins.380) inta vayirākikaḷukku vīramuṣṭi eṉṟu peyar vaḻaṅkum. kōyil paṇiceypavaruḷ vayirākikaḷ kuttukkum caṇṭaikkum muṉṉiṉṟu kātta ceytikaḷ pala kalveṭṭuk kaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivvaralāṟṟai “uṇpāṉ tiṉpāṉ civappirāmaṇaṉ, kuttukku niṟpāṉ vīramuṣṭi,” eṉṟa paḻamoḻi kuṟittu niṟkiṟatu.
paṇṭaināḷil vīrarkaḷ pōrceytu puṇpaṭṭu iṟantu pōvarāyiṉ, avarkaḷuṭaiya peyaraiyum avar peṟṟa perumaikaḷaiyum oru kallil eḻuti, atai nīrāṭṭip palapēr cellakūṭiya vaḻikaḷilum āṟṟaṅkaraiyilum niṟutti vaippatu vaḻakkam. antak kallaik kal niṉṟa vīraṉuṭaiya maṉaivi nīrāṭṭi nellum pūvum corintu atait taṉ kaṇavaṉākavē eṇṇi vaḻipaṭuvāḷ. itai “olimeṉ kūntal oṇṇuta larivai, naṭukal toḻutuparavum,” eṉap puṟanāṉūṟu mutaliya caṅka nūlkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. itupaṟṟiyē “kalleṉṟālum kaṇavaṉ,” eṉṟa paḻamoḻi uṇṭāyiṟṟu. mēlum, kollaikaḷilum vayalkaḷilum katirmuṟṟi
varum payirkaḷukkuk kāvalākap pullum vaikkōlum koṇṭu āḷpōla uruvam ceytu vayalvarappukaḷil niṟuttuvatu vaḻakkam. ataṉaip pullāḷ eṉpar. ataṉiṭattil vīrac ceykai oṉṟum illaiyāyiṉum tulaiyil varupavarkkum iravil varupavarkkum āḷpōlat tōṉṟi ōraḷavu kāvalāvatupaṟṟi appullāḷai niṟuttivaippar. ivvāṟē pōrkkuriya āṇmaiyillāmal vaḻippaṟiceytu vāḻpavaraiyum pullāḷ eṉpatu vaḻakkam. itaic cāṉṟōr “pullāḷ vaḻaṅkum puḷḷalai vaippiṉ pulam,” eṉṟu patiṟṟuppattil kuṟittiruppataik kāṇalām. taṉṉaik koṇṭavaṉ nēriya vīramillāta pullāḷāyiṉum avaṉaiyum kaṇavaṉākak koṇṭu oḻukavēṇṭiyatu peṇkaṭku vakutturaikkum muṟai, immuṟai iraṇṭaiyum cērttuk “kalleṉṟālum kaṇavaṉ, pulleṉṟālum puruṭaṉ,” eṉṟa paḻamoḻitōṉṟi vaḻaṅki varuvatāyiṟṟu.
“āvatum peṇṇālē aḻivatum peṇṇālē,” eṉpatu oru paḻamoḻi. maturaiyil kaṇṇakiyiṉ kāṟcilampai viṟkac ceṉṟa avaḷ kaṇavaṉāṉa kōvalaṉaik kaḷvaṉ eṉṟu kolaittaṇṭaṉaik koṭuttāṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ. ataṉaiyaṟinta kaṇṇaki, pāṇṭivēntaṉ avaikkaḷam ceṉṟu taṉ cilampaik kāṭṭit taṉ kaṇavaṉ “kaḷvaṉallaṉ,” eṉpatai nilai nāṭṭiṉāḷ. kōvalaṉ kaḷvaṉeṉṟa paḻiyillātavaṉāṉāṉ. pāṇṭiyaṉ tāṉ ceytatu kuṟṟameṉṟu uṭaṉpaṭṭuk kīḻē vīḻntu uyirviṭṭāṉ. avaṉuṭaiya maturai nakar tīppaṭṭaḻintatu. pāṇṭiyaṉ kulamum vēraṟṟup pōyiṟṟu. itaṉaiyaṟinta vēntaṉāṉa cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ vaṭanāṭu ceṉṟu kalkoṇṭu vantu kaṇṇakikku vaṭivam ceytu, kōyilamaittu vaḻipāṭu ceytāṉ. itaṉai aṭip paṭaiyākak koṇṭē “āvatum peṇṇālē aḻivatum peṇṇālē,” eṉṟa paḻamoḻi tōṉṟuva tāyiṟṟu. itaṉai vaṟpuṟuttuvataṟku vēṟoru ceytiyuṇṭu. koṅku nāṭṭil mēṟku malaittoṭariṉ aṭippakutiyil naṉṉaṉ eṉṟoru talaivaṉ vāḻntu vantāṉ.  poḷḷāccit tālukāviluḷḷa āṉaimalai eṉṉum ūrkkup paḻaṅkālattil naṉṉaṉūr eṉṟē peyar vaḻaṅkiṟṟeṉa avvūrk kalveṭṭoṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu. (Annual Report on South Indian epi. Madras No. 214 of 27-28) avvūril avaṉoru māmarattai vaittu vaḷarttu vantāṉ atu kāyttup paḻukkat toṭaṅkiyatum ataṟkuk kāvalum ēṟpaṭuttiṉāṉ. anta maram iruntatu āṟṟaṅkarai. orunāḷ oru celvaṉuṭaiya makaḷ āṟṟil kuḷippataṟkuc ceṉṟāḷ. avaḷ kuḷittuk koṇṭirukkaiyil naṉṉaṉuṭaiya mā marattiliruntu naṉṟāyp paḻukkāta māṅkāy oṉṟu tāṉē utirntu taṇṇīril mitantukoṇṭu appeṇṇarukē vantatu.  ataṉai avaḷ eṭuttut tiṉṟāḷ. ataik kaṇṭa kāvaṟkāraṉ ceṉṟu naṉṉaṉukkut terivikkak koṭiya vaṉāṉa naṉṉaṉ avaḷaik koṉṟuviṭumāṟu kaṭṭaḷai yiṭṭāṉ. ataiyaṟinta appeṇṇiṉ tantai avaḷ niṟaiyaḷavu poṉṉum 81-yāṉaikaḷum tantu avaḷaik kolaiceyyātu viṭṭuviṭumāṟu vaṇaṅki vēṇṭiṉāṉ. naṉṉaṉ ciṟitum irakkamiṉṟik koṉṟuviṭṭāṉ. avaṉai aṉṟumutal cāṉṟōr “peṇkolaipurinta naṉṉaṉ,” eṉṟu puṟam paḻittaṉar. avaṉ marapiṉaraiyum ikaḻntaṉar. anta naṉṉaṉ vaḻiyum piṉpu aḻintupōyiṟṟu. itu mutal “āvatum peṇṇālē aḻivatum peṇṇālē,” eṉṟa paḻamoḻi nilai peṟṟu varuvatāyiṟṟu.
“āriyak kūttāṭiṉālum kāriyattilē kaṇ,” eṉpatoru mūturai. iyal, icai, nāṭakam eṉṟa pirivil nāṭakam eṉpatu kūttu. itu tamiḻkkūttu āriyakkūttu eṉa iru vakaippaṭum. “āriyam tamiḻeṉum cīrnaṭam iraṇṭiṉum,” eṉṟu aṟiñar kūṟuvar. āriyakkūttāṭu vōr āriyanāṭṭiliruntu vantu kaḻaikkūttarpōl kayiṟṟiṉmēlniṉṟu pakkattē paṟai koṭṭa aḻakāka āṭuvar eṉak kuṟuntokaiyil perumpatumaṉār pāṭiya pāṭṭil aṟikiṉṟōm. avarkaṭku vēntarum celvarum poṉṉum poruḷum paricilākat taruvar, avaṟṟaip peṟṟuk
koṇṭu avarkaḷ tam āriyanāṭṭiṟkuc celvar. tamiḻkkūttarkku nilam viṭuvatu vēntarkkum celvarkkum marapu. piṉpu tamiḻkkūttarum āriyak kūttāṭat toṭaṅkiṉar. ataṟkuriya paricilākap poṉṉum poruḷum peṟuvataiyē viṭuttu nilam peṟuvataiyē tamiḻkkūttar virumpiṉar. tamiḻkkūttar ivvaṇṇamē paricil peṟṟu vāḻnta tiṟattaik kumpakōṇattu nākanāta cāmikōyil kalveṭṭum, tiruvāvaṭutuṟaik kōyil kalveṭṭum kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. (Annual Report No. 90 of 32 and No. 120 of 1925) ataṉāl tamiḻkkūttar āriyakkūttāṭiṉālum kāriyamākiya kūttāṭṭuk kāṇipeṟum nāṭṭamē yuṭaiyarāyiṉar. atu paṟṟiyē “āriyakkūttāṭiṉālum kāriyattil kaṇ,” eṉṟa paḻamoḻi vaḻaṅkivaruvatāyiṟṟu.
tiruñāṉacampantar cīkāḻit tirukkuḷakkaraiyil niṉṟu civañāṉap pāluṇṭatu mutal “aḻutapiḷḷai pāl kuṭikkum,” eṉṟa paḻamoḻiyum, avar varalāṟu maṭṭil periyapurāṇattil tiruñāṉacampantar purāṇattilum tirunāvukkaracu purāṇattilum kalantu pātiyaḷavukkumēl iruppatāl “piḷḷai pāti periyavar pāti” eṉṟa paḻamoḻiyum, ciṟuvayatil kaṟkappaṭṭu, āyuḷ muḻutum payaṉpaṭṭuvarum ciṟappupaṟṟit tirukkuṟaḷaiyum nālaṭiyāraiyum aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭu, “nālum iraṇṭum collukkuṟuti” eṉṟa paḻamoḻiyum, kaṟṟavar, kallātavar eṉṟa irutiṟattārukkum kamparāmāyaṇam ilakkiya iṉpam taruvatupaṟṟi “kalviyiṟ periyavar kampar,” eṉṟa paḻamoḻiyum kamparāmāyaṇam tavira vēṟu nūlkaḷaik kallātavarum kavipāṭum pāvalarāka viḷaṅkuvatupaṟṟik “kampar vīṭṭuk kaṟṟuttaṟiyum kavipāṭum,” eṉṟa paḻamoḻiyum tōṉṟi nilavukiṉṟaṉa. kaṟṟuttaṟi = kaṟṟuvarum māṇavaṉ. itu piṉpu kaṭṭuttariyeṉa maruviviṭṭatu; ēṭukaḷilum kaṭṭut tariyeṉṟē kāṇap paṭuvatāyiṟṟu. ataṉāl, “eḻutiṉavaṉ ēṭṭaik keṭuttāṉ paṭittavaṉ pāṭṭaik keṭuttāṉ,” eṉṟa paḻamoḻiyum meyyuraiyāy nilavuvatāyiṟṟu.
ivvāṟu ilakkiyattuṟai paṟṟiyum, icai nāṭakam paṟṟiyum, “virutta vaittiyam pāliya cōtiṭam,” eṉa maruttuvam, cōtiṭam mutaliya tuṟai paṟṟiyum paḻamoḻikaḷ pala nūṟṟukkaṇakkāka nāṭṭavar nāvilum nayampeṟṟut tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟaikkaṇṭa mēṉāṭṭu ārāycci yāḷaruḷ oruvarāṉa Charles. E. Gover. M.R.A.S. eṉpavar “uyarnta karuttukkaḷ niṟainta paḻamoḻikaḷum nāṭṭup pāṭalkaḷum potuvākat tamiḻ teluṅku moḻikaḷilum ciṟappākat tamiḻil mikuntum uḷḷaṉa. ittuṟaiyil uḻaittu ivaikaḷai veḷiyiṭṭup potumakkaḷ kaṇṭu matikkavum tirāviṭar maṉappāṉmaiyiṉ viḷaivai aṟintukoḷḷavum ceyvatu paḻuttapulavarkaṭkum ciṟanta nākarikam paṭaitta araciyalārukkum takutiyām” eṉṟa karuttamaiya,
"There is a great mass of (thoughts) ready to hand in Tamil and Telegu folk-literature, especially in the former. To raise these to public estimation, to exhibit the true products of the Dravidian mind would be a task worthy of the ripest scholar and the most enlightened government (Introduction to folk songs of southern India printed in 1871) eṉṟu kūṟiyiruppatu aṟiñar karutat takkatu.



11. camaṇa muṉivar tamiḻt toṇṭu
nam tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ ilakkaṇa ilakkiya neṟiyiṉ vaḷarcciyiṉai varalāṟṟu muṟaiyāl ārāykiṉṟavar, ivvaḷarcci kuṟittuc camaṇa muṉivar ceytuḷḷa toṇṭukaḷaik kāṇātupōka muṭiyātu. tamiḻ vaḷarccikkeṉac ciṟanta toṇṭu purinta tamiḻ makkaḷaiviṭac camaṇa muṉivarē talai ciṟantu niṟkiṉṟaṉar eṉṟu palarum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. avar ceyta toṇṭiṉai oru ciṟitu ārāyiṉ, avar toṇṭu muṟṟum tam camaya neṟiyiṉait tamiḻnāṭṭil nilaipeṟuvittaṟkuc ceyta camayat toṇṭākavē tōṉṟukiṉṟamaiyiṉ, ataṉaip pulappaṭukkum kuṟippāl ikkaṭṭurai eḻutappeṟukiṉṟatu.
1. camaṇa muṉivar varalāṟu
camaṇa muṉivar eṉpavar caiṉa camaya voḻukkattai mēṟkoṇṭu, tuṟavikaḷāy, naṉñāṉam, naṟkāṭci, nalloḻukkam ciṟantu, viṉaittoṭarpu keṭumāṟu muyalum cāṉṟōrāvar. itu paṇṭaik kālattil vaṭanāṭṭil tōṉṟiya putuc camayam eṉṟu cilar kūṟuvar. camayak kalaiyil valluṉarākiya irātā
kiruṭṭiṉaṉ eṉṉum pērāciriyar, "iḵtu intiya nāṭṭiṟkē urittākiya vaitika camayattiliruntu putitāy kiḷaitta camayam" eṉkiṉṟār. ic camayam vaṭanāṭṭil caiṉa camayam eṉṟum, teṉṉāṭṭil camaṇa camayam eṉavum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. caiṉaruḷ, illaṟattōr cirāvakar eṉṟum, tuṟavu pūṇṭu iṉpattu viruppum, tuṉpattu veṟuppum ilarāy iraṇṭaṉaiyum oppa matittoḻukum cāṉṟōr camaṇar eṉavum kūṟappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷ, putta camayattai nāṭeṅkum parapputaṟkuriya peru muyaṟciyiṉaic ceyta pēraracap peruntakaiyākiya acōka maṉṉarālum naṉku matikkap peṟṟavarkaḷ. ic camaṇarkaḷ nam tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ pukuntu tam camaya neṟiyiṉaip parappiyataṉāl, ivarkaḷatu camayamākiya caiṉa camayam ivarkaḷ peyarāl camaṇa camayam eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭuvatāyiṟṟu.
2. camaṇa muṉivar varaviṉkaṇ
tamiḻ irunta nilai
tamiḻnāṭṭil, ātiyil tamiḻ makkaḷē vāḻntu vantaṉar; avarkaḷpāl taṉit tamiḻē nilaviyiruntatu. piṉṉar, vaṭanāṭṭu makkaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ kuṭi pukuntaṉar. avar vaḻaṅkiya vaṭamoḻiyum avarōṭu pōntu tamiḻnāṭṭil iṭam peṟṟatu. vaṭavarum tamiḻarum vāḻkkaiyil oruṅku iyaintu ulavat toṭaṅkiyatum, vaṭamoḻiyum tamiḻiṭaik kalantu vaḻaṅkap
peṟuvatāyiṟṟu. camaṇar tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ pukunta kālattut tamiḻariṭai vaṭavar vaḻakkum, vaṭamoḻi vaḻakkum nilaviyiruntaṉa. tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ maṉappāṉmaiyum, vaṭavar vaḻakkiṉaiyum, vaṭamoḻi vaḻakkiṉaiyum mēṟkoṇṭoḻukutaṟkaṇ pērārvamum, pērūkkamum koṇṭu iruntatu.
nam nāṭṭiṟkup putiyarāy, putu vaḻakkum, putu voḻukkum, putu moḻiyum uṭaiyarāyp pōnta mēṉāṭṭu makkaḷiṉ kūṭṭuṟavāl, nam nāṭṭavarkku avar moḻi vaḻakkiṉum, naṭaiyoḻukkiṉum vēṭkaiyum viḻaivum peruka, ataṉāl nam nāṭṭu moḻi nilai tāḻvuṟṟataṉṟō? aṅṅaṉamē, vaṭavar koṇarnta moḻi vaḻakkiṉaiyum, naṭaiyoḻukkiṉaiyum kaṇṭa tamiḻar avaṟṟiṉpāl eḻunta vēṭkaiyāl, tamiḻ vaḷarcciyiṟ karuttūṉṟātu tāḻntaṉar. vaṭavaratu vaitika camayak kōṭpāṭukaḷai mēṟkoṇṭaṉar. vaṭavaratu vaṭamoḻi uyarntōrkkallatu uṇarttappaṭāteṉṉum vaṭavar vakutta muṟaiyāl, ikkālatu āṅkilam pōlat tamiḻariṭam mutaliṭam peṟātāyiṟṟu. āṉāl, vaṭamoḻiyait tamiḻoṭu viravip pēcum neṟiyiṉai maṭṭum kaiviṭātu pēṇivantaṉar. ataṉāl, vaṭacol viravāta taṉit tamiḻ nūlkaḷ arukiṉa; vaṭacol viraviya nūlkaḷ kūṭiya aḷaviṟ perukiṉa. ilakkaṇaṅkaḷum vaṭa coṟkaḷ tamiḻoṭu naṭaipeṟṟu iyalutaṟku iṭam vakuttu amaippavāyiṉa. "vaṭacoṟkiḷavi vaṭaveḻuttorīi, eḻuttoṭu puṇarnta collākummē" eṉṟu tolkāppiyam kūṟuvatāyiṟṟu.
ivvaṟu vaṭavar kūṭṭuṟavāl, tamiḻar tam tāymoḻi vaḷarccikkaṇ ūkkam kuṟaintiruppataiyum, vaṭamoḻik kaṇ viruppum, vaitika camayak kataikaḷil vēṭkaiyum mikkuṟṟiruppataiyum, camaṇa muṉivar teḷiya vuṇarntaṉar. tamiḻar, vaitika camayappaṟṟu mikuntu atupaṟṟiya kataikaḷaik kēṭṭu, avaṟṟiṟku vēṟāka varum camaṇa camayak karuttukkaḷaiyum kataikaḷaiyum mēṟkoḷḷutaṟkut tāḻttaṉar. ivvāṟu tampāl vēṟupaṭṭu niṟkum tamiḻar kūṭṭuṟavaip peṟutaṟkuriya neṟiyil camaṇa muṉivar muyalvārāyiṉar. tamiḻarkku vaṭanūṟ kataikaḷil viruppu mikkiruntamaiyiṉ, tāmum vaṭanūṟkaḷaiyum, vaṭanūl marapukaḷaiyum tamiḻiṉkaṭ koṇarumukattāl tamiḻar ātaravaip peṟalāyiṉar. inneṟikkup peruṅ karuviyāka viḷaṅkuvatu iḷañ ciṟārkkuk kalvi payiṟṟuvikkum perun toṇṭēyākum eṉṟu uṇarntaṉar. ataṉāl tām taṅkiyirunta paḷḷikaḷil kūṭaṅkaḷ amaittu, avaṟṟuḷ iruntu ciṟuvarkkuk kalvik koṭai puriyalāyiṉar. annāḷ toṭṭē kalvi payilum iṭaṅkaḷ paḷḷikkūṭaṅkaḷ eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭuvavāyiṉa.
3. tamiḻit toṇṭu
camaṇa muṉivarkaḷiṉ toṇṭu tēvāra kālattukku muṉṉaiyatum, piṉṉaiyatum eṉa iruvakaippaṭum, tēvāra kālattum, ataṟku muṉṉarum irunta camaṇa muṉivar peruṅ kalviyum, aṟam uraikkum amaitiyum nirampit tuṟavaṟamē cīritāka mēṟkoṇṭu iruntaṉar. ivarkaḷ neṟiyiṉaik kaṭintu vātupurinta caivap periyōrkaḷāṉa ñāṉacampantar mutaliyōr ivarkaḷiṉ peruṅ kalviyiṉaiyum, aṟavuraikaḷaiyum cuṭṭi irukkiṉṟaṉar. "amaṇarum kuṇṭarum cākkiyarum nayamuka uraiyiṉar," "ōtiyum kēṭṭum uṇar viṉaiyilātār uḷkalākātōr iyalpiṉaiyuṭaiyār" eṉṟum, ivarkaḷ, aṟam uraitta perumaiyiṉai, "pāṭuṭaik kuṇṭar cākkiyar camaṇar payiltarum aṟavurai", "aṟaṅkāṭṭum amaṇarum cākkiyarum", "kaṭumali uṭaluṭai amaṇarum kañciyuṇ cākkiyarum, iṭum aṟavurai" eṉa ñāṉacampantap perumāṉ kūṟiyaruḷiyirukkiṉṟār. ivaṟṟāl tēvāra kālattiṟku muṉṉar irunta camaṇa muṉivar tam camaya aṟaṅkaḷai makkaḷukku uṇarttuvatē tamatu kaṭaṉākak koṇṭiruntaṉar eṉpatu teḷiya viḷaṅkukiṟatu. akkālattiṟku muṉṉar irunta tamiḻ nūlkaḷai nōkkiṉ, avaṟṟuṭ camaṇa muṉivar ceytaṉavākak kāṇappaṭuvaṉa yāvum aṟanūlkaḷēyāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. nālaṭiyār, aṟaneṟiccāram, ciṟupañcamūlam mutaliya palavum aṟam kūṟuvaṉavēyākum.
akkālattē avarkaḷ, varalāṟu kūṟum tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ mikac ceytilar; oṉṟiraṇṭu ceytārāyiṉum avai tamiḻ vaḷaṅ keḻumiyavāyt tamiḻar maṉattaik kavvuvaṉavāy illai. avarkaḷ ulaka vāḻkkaiyilveṟuppum, tuṟavu neṟiyil viruppum
mikku iruntamaiyiṉ tamiḻar vāḻkkaiyil nilaviya akapporuḷ nalaṅkaḷum, puṟapporuḷ tuṟaikaḷum neṭunāḷkāṟum avarkaḷ maṉattil iṭampeṟṟu, avarkaḷ aṟivai ēvalkoḷḷavillai. ataṉāl, avarkaḷ tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ ilakṇat tuṟaiyiṟ pukuntu, ataṉ pakutikaḷuḷ oṉṟākiya ceyyuḷiyalil peruntoṇṭu purivārāyiṉar.
kaṟṟavar kallātavar eṉṟa irutiṟattārkkum aṟavurai vaḻaṅkumiṭattum uraiyum pāṭṭumē mika vēṇṭaṟpālaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉataiviṭap piṉṉaiyatē kallātavaraiyum eḷitiṟ piṇikkum vāyppuṭaiyatākum. ākavē, ataṟkuriya ceyyuḷiyalil perumpāṭupaṭṭup pala putuneṟikaḷaiyum iyalkaḷaiyum kāṇpārāyiṉar. yāpparuṅkala viruttiyuraiyiṟ kāṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷa ceyyuḷiyal nūlkaḷuṭ pala, camaṇa muṉivarāṟ ceyyap peṟṟiruppataṟku ituvē kāraṇamākum. icai nūlum nāṭaka nūlum kāmam payakkum ciṟumaiyuṭaiyavai eṉpatu ammuṉivar kaḷiṉ karuttu, ataṉāl attuṟaiyai nekiḻppatōṭu avvicai nāṭakaṅkaḷil paṟṟuṇṭākāmaiyum vaḷarttu vantaṉar. tamiḻar tāmum, tam tamiḻaiyē maṟantu, vaṭanūṟ kataikaḷilum, vaṭavar vaḻakka voḻukkaṅkaḷilum vēṭkai mītūrntu niṉṟamaiyiṉ, avai pēṇaṟpāṭiṉṟi iṟantaṉa.
tolkāppiya nūluṭ kāṇappaṭāta pāṭṭiṉaṅkaḷ ivarkaḷ ceyta ceyyuḷiyalkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟu paḻaiya tamiḻ neṟikaḷaip putuneṟiyiṟ koṇartaṟku muyaṉṟu peruntoṇṭu purinta ip periyōrkaḷ icai, nāṭakaṅ kaḷilum tam karuttaic celuttiyiruppiṉ, nam tamiḻ moḻiyil ettuṇaiyō icai nūlkaḷum nāṭaka nūlkaḷum tōṉṟi yirukkumē! icai nūlkaḷuṭ cila ivarkaḷāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu cilar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl, ataṉai uṇmaiyeṉat tuṇitaṟkuriya cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭaittila.
toṭakkattil tam camaya neṟikaḷai makkaṭku uraippatē poruḷākkik koṇṭu tamiḻnāṭeṅkum tirintu camaṇ paḷḷi kaḷaiyum, camaṇ pāḻikaḷaiyum ivarkaḷ niṟuvi vantaṉar. avai yāvum  aṟanilaiyaṅkaḷāka iruntaṉavēyaṉṟi, aṟivuc cuṭar vaḻaṅkum tamiḻ nūl nilaiyaṅkaḷāka illāmaiyāl, tēvārap periyōrkaḷ kālattil, ivaratu toṇṭu ivarkaṭkup pōtiya araṇ ceyyātāyiṟṟu. camayappōr nikaḻccikaḷil, ivarkaṭkup pōtiya paṭaiyākak kūṭiya tamiḻ nūlkaḷ illai. ataṉāl ivarkaḷ eḷitil vellap peṟṟaṉar. tamiḻilakkaṇaṅkaḷāka, ivarkaḷ ceyta avinayam, intirakāḷīyam, parimāṇam mutaliyavai camaya nūlkaḷākavō camaya aṟanūlkaḷākavō payaṉpaṭavillai.
tēvāra kālattil ic camaṇa muṉivarkaḷuk kuṇṭākiya tōlvi ivarkaṭkup piṟkālattil aḻiyāp pukaḻaip payappatāyiṟṟu. ivarkaṭkut tam toṇṭil irunta kuṟaipāṭukaḷ naṉku pulaṉāyiṉa. tamiḻar kūṭṭattil nilaviya camaya nūlkaḷaip payiṉṟu, avaṟṟaippōla ilakkiyaṅ kaḷaiyum, ciṟunūlkaḷaiyum ceyyat toṭaṅkiṉar. iḷañ ciṟār mutal periyōr īṟākap palarkkum payaṉpaṭattakka tamiḻ nūlkaḷai iyaṟṟat talaip paṭṭaṉar. tamiḻt toṇṭiṉait tam camayat toṇṭiṟkuk karuviyākak koḷḷum karuttiṉarākaliṉ, iḷaiñarkkeṉa iyaṟṟiya ciṉṉūlkaḷilun tam camayak karuttukaḷaip pukuttu miṭamaṟintu pukuttit tam karuttu muṟṟa niṉaintaṉar.
ciṟuvar kaṟkum kaṇakkiyal vāypāṭukaḷil, tam camayak karuttai nuḻaittaṟku iṭamillāmaiyāl, avaṟṟiṟkuk kāppuc ceyyuḷ yāttu, ataṉkaṇ camayavuṇarvaip pukaṭṭiṉar. "vaṉmatimukkuṭaiyāṉmalaraṭitoḻa, nellaṇi lakkam neñciṉil varumē" eṉpatu nellilakkavāypāṭu. "arukaṉai amalaṉai acalaṉaiyaṭitoḻa, ciṟukuḻi muppatum cintaiyil varumē" eṉpatu ciṟukuḻi vāypāṭu. ivvāṟu kalvi payiṉṟu varum ciṟuvarkaṭkut tamiḻ nūlkaḷaik kaṟpittaṟkut tōṟṟu vāyāka nikaṇṭukaḷ ceyyat toṭaṅkiṉar. ivar kālattukku muṉpiruntavai nūṟpā vaṭivil perunūlkaḷāka iruntaṉa. avai, icaiyoṭu viravi, putuppāviṉattiṟ ceyyappaṭiṉ kaṟpārkku ūkkam mikum eṉṟu karutiṉar. ataṉāl avarkaḷ cūṭāmaṇi nikaṇṭu mutaliya nūlkaḷaic ceytaṉar. itaṉkaṇṇum tam camayak karuttukkaḷai vēṇṭumiṭaṅkaḷiṟ kāṭṭiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. kāppuc ceyyuḷ. "muṭivil iṉpattu mūvā mutalvaṉaip pōṟṟi"c ceytatu ulakaṟintatu. pakavaṉ eṉṉum collukkup poruḷ kūṟumiṭattu, "pakavaṉē īcaṉmāyōṉ paṅkayaṉ ciṉaṉē puttaṉ" eṉat tokutturaittaṉar. paṉṉiraṇṭāvatu tokutik kaṇ, "colvakai eḻutteṇṇellām tollaināḷ ellaiyāka, nalvakaiyākkum piṇṭi nāṉmukaṉ nāḷum tīmai, velviṉai toṭaṅkacceytu vīṭaruḷvōṉ tāḷ pōṟṟi" ceykiṉṟār. piṉṉar, nāṉilaṅkaḷ, nālvakaiyaṅkaṅkaḷai virittuk kūṟaluṟuvār, iṭaiyē, camaṇa camayak karuttākiya nāṟkati vakaiyiṉai nuḻaittu, "nāṟkati umpar makkaḷ colliya vilaṅkiṉoṭu narakar" eṉak kūṟiṉar. ivvāṟē caivarkaḷ kūṟum, civaṉukkuriya eṇvakaik kuṇaṅkaḷaik kūṟumiṭattu, arukaṉukkuriya eṇvakaik kuṇamum, iṟaivaṉukku ākāveṉat tam camaya nūlkaḷ vilakkiya eṇvakaik kuṟṟaṅkaḷaiyum kūṟiyirukkiṉṟaṉar.
tamiḻnāṭṭil nilaviya tolkāppiyam mika virintu paranta nūlāyk kiṭantamaiyiṉ, ataṉaic ciṟuvarkaḷ eḷitil kaṟṟaṟkuriya nilaiyil ciṉṉūlum naṉṉūlum iyaṟṟiyirukkiṉṟaṉa. ciṉṉūl ceyta kuṇavīrapaṇṭitar eṉṉum periyār vaṭanūṉ marapukaḷai oru ciṟitē taḻuvi, tam camayap poruṭkuriya vaṇakkaṅ kūṟic cellukiṉṟār. naṉṉūl ceyta pavaṇanti muṉivar curuṅkiya cilavāya coṟkaḷāl mika aḻakiyatāka itaṉaic ceytuḷḷār. itaṉkaṇ, ivar tam camayak karuttaik kāppuc ceyyuḷiṟ kāṭṭiyatōṭu nillātu, eḻuttiṉ ilakkaṇattilum pukuttiyiruk kiṉṟār. āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār " eḻutteṉappaṭupa akara mutal ṉakara viṟuvāy muppaḵteṉpa" eṉat toṭaṅkiṉarēyaṉṟi, eḻuttāvatu  iḵtu eṉa ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiṟṟilar. avvilakkaṇaṅ kūṟappukunta pavaṇantiyār, "moḻimutaṟ kāraṇamām aṇuttiraḷ oli eḻuttu" eṉṟu moḻikiṉṟār. itaṉkaṇ "aṇuttiraḷ oli" eṉṟatu camaṇa camayak karuttu. itaṉaiyē eḻuttukkaḷiṉ piṟappuk kūṟumiṭattum, "niṟaiyuyir muyaṟciyiṉ uḷvaḷi turappa eḻum aṇuttiraḷ" eṉac ciṟuvar maṉattil naṉkupatiyak kūṟiyirukkiṉṟār. ittuṟaik kaṇ āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār, "akatteḻu vaḷiyicai ariltapa nāṭi, aḷapiṟkōṭal antaṇar maṟaittē; aḵtivaṇnuvalātu eḻuntu puṟatticaikkum, meyteri vaḷiyicai aḷavu nuvaṉṟiciṉē" eṉṟē kūṟiṉar. tolkāppiyar kūṟiya taṉaiyē avinayaṉārum, kuṇavīrapaṇṭitarum, puttamittiraṉārum kūṟiṉar. itaṉālum piṟkālac camaṇa muṉivarkaḷiṉ karuttut tam tamiḻt toṇṭu camayat toṇṭiṟkuk karuviyātal vēṇṭum eṉpatu teḷivākum. itaṉaiyaṟintē ilakkaṇa viḷakkam iyaṟṟiya vaittiyanāta tēcikar, "moḻik kāraṇamām nātakāriyavoli eḻuttu" eṉak kūṟiṉārātal vēṇṭum.
 iṉi, ivarkaḷ ceytaruḷiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷ ciṟṟilakkiyam, pērilakkiyam eṉa iruvakaippaṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ. ciṟṟilakkiyaṅkaḷ palavum antāti, kalampakam mutalākap palatiṟappaṭum. avai yāvum camayap poruḷkaḷaiyum, camaṇa camayac cāṉṟōrkaḷaiyum pukaḻntu pāṭum ciṉṉūlkaḷākum. tirukkalampakam, tirunūṟṟantāti mutaliyavai camaṇ camayattiṉar peritum pōṟṟip paṭikkum tīviya centamiḻ nūlkaḷ. nariviruttam pōlum kataic ceyyuḷ nūlkaḷum, mērumantara purāṇam, yacōtara kāviyam pōlvaṉavum camaṇa camayap poruḷkaḷaiyum varalāṟukaḷaiyum viḷakkum naṟṟamiḻ nūlkaḷ.
cintāmaṇi mutalākak kūṟappaṭum peruṅkāppiyaṅkaḷum, cūḷāmaṇi mutalākak kūṟappaṭum ciṟukāppiyaṅkaḷum tamiḻ moḻikkup potuvāka vaittuk kūṟappaṭuvaṉa. ciṟu kāppiyamaintaṉuḷ, mēlē kāṭṭiya yacōtara kāviyamum oṉṟeṉak kūṟappaṭum.
ciṟukāppiyamaintum tamiḻmoḻikkuriya potuk kāppiyaṅ kaḷākak karutappaṭum centamiḻ nalam ciṟantavaiyāyiṉum, ivai yāvum camaṇa camayak karuttukkaḷaiyē muṟṟum kūṟic celkiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ cūḷāmaṇi eṉpatu talaiyāyatu. itaṉai iyaṟṟiya periyār tōlāmoḻittēvar. ivar peyarum, cīvaka cintāmaṇi ceyta tiruttaktēvar peyarum iyaṟpeyaralla eṉṟu karutukiṟēṉ. ivarkaḷaippōlat tamiḻt toṇṭu purinta camaṇa muṉivar peyarellām vaṭamoḻip peyarākavē irukkiṉṟaṉa. kuṇavīrapaṇṭitar, amutacākarar, maṇṭalapuruṭar, vāmatēvar eṉpaṉa palavum vaṭamoḻip peyarkaḷē. ākavē, ivarkaḷ tamiḻ nūl payiṉṟu, tamiḻ vēṭkai mikuntu, tamiḻarpōlat tāmum tamiḻarākavē iyaintu niṟkum tamiḻ nala mikutiyāl, tammait tamiḻp peyarāl tiruttakkatēvar. tōlāmoḻittēvar eṉpavarākak kūṟiṉā rātal vēṇṭum. ivarkaṭkup palavāṇṭukaṭkup piṟpōnta kāṉsṭāṉṭiṉas pescci eṉpārum, tamiḻt toṇṭu puriyum cālpu eytiya vaḻit tammai vīramāmuṉivar eṉak kūṟikkoṇṭatu, eṉ karuttiṟku ātaravu tarukiṉṟatu. iv vīramāmuṉivarum tēmpāvaṇi eṉṉum kāviyamum, toṉṉūl eṉṉum ilakkaṇamum piṟavum ceytu tamiḻt toṇṭu purintirukkiṉṟār. cīvakacintāmaṇi yeṉpatu peruṅkāppiya maintaṉuḷ talaiyāyatu. ēṉai nāṉkaṉuḷ cilappatikārattai iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ eṉṉum caivarum, maṇimēkalaiyaic cīttalaic cāttaṉār eṉṉum tamiḻarum  eḻutiṉar. ēṉai iraṇṭum iṟantuviṭṭaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ maṇimēkalai putta camayak karuttukkaḷait taṉpāṟ koṇṭatu. cilappatikārattum camaṇa camayak karuttukkaḷ kāṇappaṭum. āyiṉum camayat toṇṭiṟkum tamiḻt toṇṭiṟkum otta ilakkiyamāy viḷaṅkuvatu cīvakacintāmaṇiyē yākum.
cūḷāmaṇi, camaṇa camayattavarum ēṉaic camayattavarum oppappōṟṟum takutipuṭaiyatāy irukkiṟatu. itaṉai eḻutiya āciriyar, tamiḻnāṭṭil nilaviya puṟaccamayap poruḷkaḷaiyum camayap poruḷaiyum kaṟṟu, tam camaya nūlkaḷilum, puṟaccamaya nūlkaḷilum ottavāy uḷḷa kataip pakutikaḷaik kaṇṭu, avaṟṟait tamiḻ makkaṭku uṇartti avar tam ātaravu peṟuvatai nāṭiya nallaṟiñarāvar. itaṉāl, ivar eḻutiya innūl iṉṟukāṟum naṉku pōṟṟap peṟṟu varukiṟatu. cīvakacintāmaṇiyum ippeṟṟit tāy caivar oruvarāl uraikāṇavum peṟṟirukkiṟatu.
tōlāmoḻit tēvar eḻutiya cūḷāmaṇiyiṉkaṇ, tiviṭṭaṉ, vicayaṉ eṉṟa iraṇṭu araca kumārarkaḷ varalāṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. ivarkaḷ varalāṟu vaitika camayanūṟ kataikaḷuḷ varum varalāṟṟaip peritum ottirukkiṟatu. iṭaiyiṭaiyē camaṇa camayak karuttu viravi niṟkiṟatu. eṉiṉum, cintāmaṇi pōla, vaitika camayaneṟi niṟpavarum virumpattakka attuṇai uvamanalam uṭaiyataṉṟu eṉṟē kūṟavēṇṭum.
kaṇṇaṉ kaṭalvaṇṇaṉ eṉṟum, palarāmaṉ vaḷai vaṇṇaṉ eṉṟum tamiḻ nūlkaḷ kūṟum. "pālniṟa uruviṟ paṉaik koṭi yōṉum, nīlniṟa uruviṉ nēmiyōṉum eṉṟiru perun teyvamum uṭaṉiṉṟāṅku" (puṟam 508) eṉa varum tiviṭṭaṉaik kaṇṭa makaḷir, "kaṭaloḷi maṇivaṇṇaṉ kaṉaviṉil vantematu, uṭalakam veṟuvitāy uḷḷam vavviṉāṉ" (kumāra 32) eṉṟum, vicayaṉaik kaṇṭavar "vārvaḷai vaṇṇaṉ em maṉattuḷāṉ piṟar, ērvaḷar neṭuṅ kaṇukku ilakkamallāṉāl" (33) eṉṟum kūṟiṉār eṉpar. itaṉāl niṟamottal kūṟiyavāṟām.
kaṇṇaṉ kañcaṉ viṭutta kuvalayā pīṭattai aṭarttāṉ; tiviṭṭaṉ accuvakaṇṭaṉ viṭutta ciṅkattai vāypiḷantāṉ. kaṇṇaṉ peruki vanta maḻaiyait taṭuppatu kuṟittuk kuṉṟiṉai yeṭuttuk kuṭaiyākap piṭittāṉ; tiviṭṭaṉum pulavar ōtiya varalāṟṟiṟkēṟpak koṭimā cilai eṉṉum kuṉṟattai yeṭuttup pukaḻ niṟuviṉāṉ.
"	erimaṇi kaṭakakkai iraṇṭu mūṉṟiyap
	perumaṇi nilampila mākak kīḻnuḻait
	tarumaṇi neṭuvarai yataṉai ēntiṉāṉ
	tirumaṇi neṭumuṭic celvaṉ eṉpavē" 
eṉac cūḷāmaṇi kūṟukiṟatu.
kaṇṇaṉ piṉṉaip pirāṭṭiyai maṇantāṉ; tiviṭṭaṉ cuyampirapai eṉṉum vittiyātara makaḷai maṇantāṉ. kaṇṇaṉ cicupālaṉai āḻi yeṟintu veṉṟāṉ; tiviṭṭaṉ ayakkirīvaṉai āḻi yeṟintu veṉṟāṉ. ivvāṟu tiviṭṭaṉ ceyal palavum kaṇṇaṉ ceyalkaḷōṭu ottiruttalai, 
"	kōṭicilai yeṭuttāṉ kōḷarimāvāy pōḻntāṉ
	āṭiyal yāṉai ayakkirīvaṉai aṭittāṉ
	vīṭiṉ maṇiyaruvi veṇmalaiyum kaippiṭittāṉ
	vāṭalil pūṅkaṇṇi māmēka vaṇṇaṉē."
"	valampurivāy vaittāṉ vārcilaikaik koṇṭāṉ,
	calampuri caṇṭai talaipaṇippuk kaṇṭāṉ,
	polampuri tāmaraiyāḷ poṉṉākam tōyntāṉ,
	kalampurivaṇṭaṭakkaik kārmēka vaṇṇaṉē" 
eṉṟa pāṭṭukkaḷāl iṉitu kāṇalām.
iṉi, ipperiyōrkaḷuḷ tiruttakkatēvar ēṉai yōraik kāṭṭilum ulakiyalil mikka tērcci yuṭaiyavarāka viḷaṅkiyirukkiṟār. tamiḻnāṭṭil tām parappak karutum camayap poruḷ kuṟitta inta nūl, tamiḻnāṭṭil paraviyirukkum camayap poruḷaip puṟaṅkūṟuvatāyiruppiṉum, tam camayap poruḷaiyē niralpaṭak kūṟuvatāyiruppiṉum, tamiḻarkaḷāl ātarikkappaṭuvatu illaiyām eṉpatai naṉku uṇarntiruntaṉar. am mati nuṭpattāl, tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ payiṉṟirunta vaitika camayak katai nikaḻccikaḷai innūṟkaṇ uvamamukattāṟ payila vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷār. ataṉāl innūl camayak koḷkaiyāl vēṟupaṭṭa tamiḻar palarālum ātarikkappeṟṟa teṉpatu nāṭṭil vaḻaṅkum katai kaḷāl nām aṟikiṉṟōm. ivviyalpiṉaik kaṭaippiṭiyāmai yāṟṟāṉ. tēmpāvaṇi, cīṟāppurāṇam mutaliya tamiḻariṭaiyē naṉku vaḻaṅkātirukkiṉṟaṉa pōlum. ivviyalpiṉai mūṉṟu iyalkaḷāṟ kāṭṭi ikkaṭṭuraiyai muṭikkiṉṟōm.
tēvar kālattē, tamiḻar tēvarkaḷ imaiyā nāṭṭam uṭaiyareṉṟum, avarkaṭkum acurarkaṭkum pakaimaiyiruntu vantateṉṟum, ataṉāl tēvarkaḷ aṭikkaṭi tuṉpuṟṟaṉareṉṟum, tēvarkaḷ tirumāliṉ utaviyāl kaṭal kaṭaintu iṉiya amutam peṟṟu makiḻntaṉareṉṟum kēṭṭiruntaṉar. an nikaḻccikaḷait tiruttakkatēvar tām camayat toṇṭu kuṟittup pāṭiya cīvakacintāmaṇiyiṉkaṇ kuṟittirukkiṉṟār. kaṭṭiyaṅ kāraṉ caccantaṉaik kollak karuti amaiccarōṭu cūḻnta kālattil amaiccaruḷ tarumatattaṉ eṉpāṉ kūṟṟil vaittu, "iṟaṅku kaṇṇimaiyār viḻittēyiruntu, aṟaṅkaḷ vavva ataṉpuṟam kākkalār" (20408) eṉṟum, tēvarkaḷ kaṭal kaṭaintu amutu peṟṟa ceytiyai, patumukaṉ kōvintaiyai maṇantu kūṭik kūṟum nayappuraiyil, “tīmpāṟ kaṭalait tiraipoṅkak kaṭaintu tēvar, tāmpāṟ paṭutta amutō” eṉṟum, akkaṭal nākam kayiṟākak koṇṭu kaṭaiyap paṭṭateṉpatai cīvakaṉ kāntaruvatattaiyai maṇantakālaiyil, kaṭṭiyaṅkāraṉatu ēvalāl, accīvakaṉai etirtta vēntar avaṉāl alaippuṇṭa ceytiyai viḷakkutaṟku uvamaiyāka niṟutti, “aruvarai nākañcuṟṟi āḻiyāṉ kaṭaiyavaṉṟu, karuvarai kuṭaiyappaṭṭa kaṭaleṉak kalaṅki vēntar...uṭaiyaluṟṟār” eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟār.
iraṇṭāvatāka, murukaṉ tāmaraippūvil tōṉṟiyavaṉ eṉṟum, avaṉ malaināṭṭukkuriya kaṭavuḷ eṉṟum, avaṉ ēntiya vēl valiciṟantatu eṉṟum, avaṉ āṭavariṟ pērāṟṟalilum  pēraḻakilum mēmpaṭṭavaṉ eṉṟum tamiḻar camayavulaku kūṟip pārāṭṭi vantatu. ic ceyalkaḷait tiruttakka tēvar, “pūviṉuṭ piṟanta tōṉṟal puṇṇiyaṉ” eṉṟum, “tāmarai amarar mēvarat tōṉṟiya aṇṇal” eṉṟum, “maṇaṅkoḷ pūmicai maivarai maintaṉō” eṉṟum, “amaruḷ āṉātu, okkiya murukaṉ eḵkam ōriraṇṭaṉaiya kaṇṇāḷ” eṉṟum, “cempoṟ kaṭampaṉ cevvēlum” eṉṟum, “katampaṉē murukaṉ vēṟpōr” eṉṟum, “tiruvarai mārpaṉ tiṇtēr maññaiyē murukaṉtāṉ” eṉṟum,  “vaḷḷal māt taṭintāṉ aṉṉa māṇpiṉāṉ”  eṉṟum, “muntu cūrtaṭinta murukaṉ nampi eṉpār” eṉṟum, ivai pōlap piṟavāṟum kūṟic ciṟappittirukkiṉṟār. ivaṟṟai iṭaṅkūṟi viḷakkaluṟiṉ kaṭṭurai viriyum.
iṉi, civaperumāṉaik kūṟumiṭattup “pōkamīṉṟa puṇṇiyaṉ” eṉṟum, “kāriyuṇṭik kaṭavuḷ”  eṉṟum, “aṟavāḻi yaṇṇal” eṉṟum, piṟavum kūṟiyum pārāṭṭuvar. paramaṉ kaṇṇutalāl kāmaṉaik kāynta ceyti “kaṇṇutaṟ kaṭavuḷ cīṟak kaṉalerippaṭṭa kāmaṉ” eṉpataṉālum, purameritta varalāṟṟiṉai,  “kaṭimatil mūṉṟumeyta kaṭavuḷ” eṉpataṉālum, “kaṇaiyeri yaḻalam peyta kaṇṇutal mūrtti yottāṉ” eṉpataṉālum, maṅkai paṅkiṉaṉāy niṟkum nilaiyiṉai, “piṟaiyaṇi yaṇṇal koṇṭa peṇṇorpāl koṇṭa tottār” eṉpataṉā lum piṟavāṟṟālum pukaḻntuḷḷār.
iṉi, tirumāl, paramaṉ purameritta kālattil ampāyiṉa ceytiyai, “pōkamīṉṟa puṇṇiya ṉeyta kaṇaiyēpōl, mākamīṉṟa māmatiyaṉṉāṉ vaḷarkiṉṟāṉ” eṉṟum, avaṟkuk karuṭappuḷ ūrtiyeṉpatai, “āḻiyāṉ ūrtippuḷ” eṉṟum, avaṉ cakkarappaṭaiyuṭaiyaṉ eṉpataṉai itaṉālum peṟa vaittum, tirumāl kaṇṇaṉāyt tōṉṟiya kālattu, pāratap pōril caṅkōcai eḻuppiya ceytiyai, “ceṅkaṇmāl teḻikkappaṭṭa valampurit turuvaṅ koṇṭa, caṅku” eṉṟum, kaṇṇaṉaik kōṟaṟkup pōnta kuvalayāpīṭameṉṟa yāṉaiyai avaṉ aṭartta nikaḻcciyiṉai, “mallal nīrmaṇi vaṇṇaṉaip paṇṭornāḷ, kolla vōṭiya kuñcaram pōṉṟatac, celvaṉ pōṉṟaṉaṉ cīvakaṉ” eṉṟum, tirumāl irāmaṉākiya kālattu, avaṉ marāmara mēḻum eyta vil vaṉmaiyai, “marāmara mēḻum eyta vāṅkuvil taṭakkai valvil, irāmaṉai vallaṉ eṉpatu icaiyalāṟ kaṇṭatillai” eṉa uvamittum makiḻkiṉṟār.
mēlum, īṇṭuk kūṟiya civaperumāṉum, tirumālum, tām paravum arukamūrttiyē eṉavum, avviruvaraiyum paravuvōr, arukaṉaip paravutal vēṇṭum eṉpār,
“	malarēntu cēvaṭiya māleṉpa mālāl
	alarēnti añcali ceytañcap paṭuvār
	alarēnti añcali ceytañcap paṭumēl
	ilarē malareṉiṉum ēttāvā ṟeṉṉē
“	kaḷicēr kaṇaiyuṭaiya kāmaṉaiyum kāynta
	aḷicēr aṟavāḻi aṇṇal ivaṉeṉpa,
	aḷicēr aṟavāḻi aṇṇal ivaṉēl,
	viḷiyāk kuṇattuti nām vittālāṟeṉṉē.”
eṉṟu moḻintirukkiṉṟār.
iṉi, ivar kāṭṭi makiḻvikkum tamiḻ nūṟ collāṭci kaḷum, tamiḻar vaḻakkavoḻukkaṅkaḷum īṇṭu eṭuttuk kāṭṭaluṟiṉ, iḵtōr perum parappiṉatākum. ivvāṟu, tam camayap poruḷai muṟṟa viritturaikkum peruṅkāppiyam pāṭat toṭaṅkiyavar, ataṉait tamiḻ nāṭṭavar peritum virumpiyēṟṟup pēṇattakka viraku aṟintu amaitta amaiti, avaratu tamiḻ māṇpulamaiyiṉai naṉku viḷakkukiṉṟatu. mēlum, ivar kāṭṭiya neṟiyiṉai ivarkkup piṉ vantōr kaṭaippiṭittirupparēl, camayap pūcalkaḷum, piṟa tīmaikaḷum uḷavātaṟ kētuvillaiyākiyirukkum.
itukāṟum kūṟiyavāṟṟāl, camaṇamuṉivar caiṉa camayattuc cāṉṟōr eṉpatum, avarkaḷ nam tamiḻnāṭṭil tam camaya neṟiyai uṇarttap pukuntapōtu, tamiḻar, ipperiyārkaṭkup pala āṇṭukaḷ muṉpē vanta vaṭavar kūṭṭuṟavāl tam tamiḻccamaya neṟi maṟantu, vaṭavar camayak koḷkaiyilum, vaṭanūṉ marapu kaḷilum vēṭkaiyuṟṟut tam tamiḻ vaḷarcciyaik karutātu niṉṟaṉar eṉpatum, āyiṉum camaṇa muṉivarkaḷ tēvāra kālam varaiyil tamiḻt toṇṭu puritaṟkaṇ ūkkaṅ koḷḷātiruntaṉar eṉpatum, piṉpu akkālattu vātaṅkaḷil tōlviyuṟṟataṉ payaṉāl, tamiḻt toṇṭu puriyat toṭaṅki, ciṉṉūlkaḷum perunūlkaḷum ceytaṉar eṉpatum, ilakkaṇaṅkaḷiṉ cila putuneṟik karuttukkaḷaip pukuttiyum, tām ceyta nūlkaḷuḷ vēṇṭumiṭaṅkaḷil tam camayak karuttukkaḷ nuḻaittum camayat toṇṭu virittaṉar eṉpatum. atuvē nam tamiḻukkup perun toṇṭāyiṟṟeṉpatu piṟavumākum. inneṟiyaik kaippaṟṟiya piṉ vanta kiṟistavap periyārkaḷum tamiḻt toṇṭu purintaṉar. ikkālattu ēṉaic camayattavarkaḷum immuṟaiyai mēṟkoḷvār kaḷāyiṉ, taṅkaḷ camayaneṟi vaḷam peṟuvatōṭu nam tamiḻ moḻiyum ciṟappeytum eṉpatu uṟuti.
12. intiya nāṭṭil iculām 
ceyta iṉiya toṇṭu
nām vāḻum ivviṉiyanāṭu intiya nāṭu eṉpatil namakku iṉpam eḻukiṉṟatu. itaṉkaṇ vāḻum nām aṉaivarum intiyar eṉṟu eṇṇumpōtu, nam uḷḷattil iṉpak kiḷarcci eḻumpiyarumpukiṟatu. namatu camayam, vāḻkkai muṟai, oḻukkam eṉpaṉavaṟṟai niṉaintu eḻutumpōtu nammaiyaṟiyāta uvakai kaṉintu ūṟukiṟatu. ittuṟaic ciṟappukku mutaliṭamāy, nām iruntu vāḻtaṟkut tāyakamāy viḷaṅkum nam intiya nāṭu oruvakaiyil varuttattaiyum nalkukiṉṟatu, nam kūṭṭattil camayak kāyccal, vakuppup pūcal uyarvu tāḻvu, tīṇṭutal tīṇṭāmai mutaliya tīkkōḷkaḷ niṉṟu nām peṟum vāḻkkai yiṉpattaic citaikkiṉṟaṉa. piṟacamayaṅkaḷum, piṟacamayattavarkaḷum ceytuḷḷa naṉmaikaḷaik kāytal, uvattal iṉṟi naṭunilaiyilniṉṟu kāṇavoṭṭātu, ivaikaḷ maṟaikkiṉṟaṉa; maṟaittaṉavar eṉpataip paṟṟi iṉi niṉaippatu payaṉil ceykaiyākum, maṟaikkātirukkumāṟu ceytalē ceyyaṟpālatākum.
nallataṉ nalaṉum tīyataṉ tīmaiyum kāṇpatē nam aṟiviṉ kaṭamaiyākum. uyariyapoṉ mikattāḻnta maṇṇoṭu kalantu kāṇappaṭukiṟataṉṟō? mikavuyarnta pālum orō vaḻit tīmaipayakkak kāṇkiṉṟōmallamō? naṉporuḷ iḻintōriṭattum, iḻi poruḷ uyarntōriṭattum peṟukiṉṟōm; keṭuporuḷ naṇpar vāyilum, naṉ poruḷ pakaivarpālum kiṭaik kiṉṟaṉavē! ivaṟṟaiyuṭkoṇṭu, nam tiruvaḷḷuvap peruntakai, “epporuḷ yār yār vāykkēṭpiṉum apporuḷ meyp poruḷ kāṇpatu aṟivu” eṉa moḻintatu poyyā moḻi yaṉṟō! ivvuṇmaiyaik kaṟpatum, piṟarkkuk kaṟpippatum ceyyum nām, piṟa camayattavar ceyta nalaṅkaḷaiyaṟintu pārāṭṭuvatu namakkuk kaṭamaiyākum. nammilum, “cīṟāppurāṇam” eṉṉum tīviya centamiḻk kāviyamum, “nākūrppurāṇam” eṉṉum nalañ ceṟi purāṇamum peṟṟa tamiḻaṟiñar, iculām camayattavar nam tamiḻkkāṟṟiya peruntoṇṭiṉai yaṟintu makiḻum peruntakaikaḷātaliṉ, avarkaḷ, iccamayattavar nam intiya nāṭṭiṟkē ceytuḷḷa perum paṇikaḷait terintu pōṟṟum ciṟappuṭaiyavar eṉak karutukiṉṟēṉ.
iculām camayattavar mucalmāṉkaḷ eṉavum, makamatiyar eṉavum nam nāṭṭil vaḻaṅkappeṟuvar. avarkaḷ mēṟ koṇṭirukkum iculām camayamum mukamatiyamatam eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum. iccamayam, puttacamayam, camaṇacamayam pōla, intu camayattiṉiṉṟu muḷaitta kiḷaiccamayamaṉṟu; itu, ciṟitu kālattukku muṉpu nam nāṭṭiṟkup pōntu paraviyirukkum kiṟittava camayattukkum muṉṉar nam nāṭṭiṟkup pōntu ciṟappeytiya camayamākum. nam nāṭṭuc camaya nūlkaḷuṭ kūṟappaṭum aṟuvakaippaṭṭa akaccamayam, puṟaccamayam, akappuṟac camayam, puṟappuṟaccamayam eṉṟa camayat tokaivakai virikaḷil, iḵtu iṭampeṟāmaikkum ituvē kāraṇamākum. itaṟkum kiṟittava camayattukkum mutaṟcamayam yūtacamayam eṉṟu kūṟupa. avyūtacamayam, intiyanāṭṭiṟ piṟantu taḻaitta camayamaṉṟu; ataṉāl, ataṉaip paṟṟi nam nāṭṭuc camayak kaṇakkarkaḷ ārāycci ceytilar. piṟkālattup pōnta camayap pulavarkaḷum avvarāycci yiṉaic ceyyātoḻintaṉar. ceytiruppā rāyiṉ, nam camūkattil pirippiṉṟi vāḻum caiṉar, puttar mutaliyavarkaḷaippōla, iccamayattavarkaḷum nammoṭu pirippaṟak kalantukoṇṭiruppar. āṉāl, itaṟku vēṟu kāraṇam kūṟupavarum uṇṭu. āyiṉum aḵtu attuṇaic ciṟappāka illai. ataṉaiyum piṉṉark kūṟuvōm.
iculām eṉpatu oruteyvak kōṭpāṭṭaik kaṭaip piṭiyākavuṭaiya peruñ camayamākum. puṟaccamayaṅkaḷaiyum palateyvak kōṭpāṭukaḷaiyum evvakaiyilum ēlātatu; param poruḷ oṉṟait tavirap piṟa evaṟṟaiyum vaṇaṅkutaṟku makkaḷaip paṇiyāta paṇpu mēmpaṭṭatu; kiṟittava camayamum innilaiyil itaṉōṭu otta iyalpuṭaiyatu. ivai yiraṇṭaṟkum tāyc camayamāka irunta yūtacamayampōla, ivaiyuṇarttum iyavuḷ, ēṉait teyvaṅkaḷ uḷaveṉum eṇṇutaṟkum iṭantarāta uyarkaṭavuḷ eṉṉiṉ atu mikaiyākātu. “pallōrum paramporuḷ oṉṟē uṇṭu; ataṉpāl parivukoṭu paracivāḻtal ellōrum ceykaṭaṉām” eṉṟē meymmaineṟiyai ulakil vaṟpuṟuttiya peruneṟi itaṟkē uṇṭu. ettiṟattu makkaḷaiyum uyarvu, tāḻvu iṉṟi oppa eṇṇiyoḻuka vēṇṭum eṉṟa uyarneṟiyil oppaṟṟa camayam iculām camayamē. kaṭavuḷiṉ aruṭkaṇṇiṉmuṉ makkaḷ vēṟṟumai yillai eṉṟa cen neṟiyai ulakil niṟuvi, yāvarum ataṉaik kaṭaippiṭittoḻukac ceyta mutaṟperumai itaṟkē uṇṭu.
iccamayam, paṇṭaik kālattil, kiṟittu piṟanta cumār aṟunūṟu yāṇṭukaṭkuppiṉ, arapiya nāṭṭiṟ piṟanta makamatu eṉṟa perumakaṉārāl mutaṉmutalākak kāṇap peṟṟatu. akkālattu, avvarapiya nāṭṭu makkaḷiṭaiyē poruntāc ceykaikaḷum, tiruttāk koḷkaikaḷum palavāy malintu, uruva vaḻipāṭṭiṉ peyarāl, an nāṭṭiṟkup pēriḻiviṉaic ceytu vantaṉa. aruḷ vaḷḷalākiya ipperiyār avaṟṟaik kaṇṭu uḷam poṟātu iccamayavuṇmaiyiṉaik kaṇṭu, am makkaḷai nalvaḻiyil teruṭṭit tiruppaveṇṇiṉār. avar muyaṟci toṭakkattil avaratu arumaiyuyirkkē iṉṉalai viḷaivikkat toṭaṅkiṟṟu. avarkaḷ viḷaitta tīmai yiṉaip poṟāta avar, muṭivil, annāṭṭiṉ cīriya nakaraṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟākiya mekkā nakaraiviṭṭu nīṅki metiṉā eṉṉum nakarukkuc ceṉṟār. ataṟkuḷ annāṭṭavaruṭpalar, avar teruṭṭiyacamayak koḷkaiyai mēṟkoṇṭu, avar kāṭṭiya neṟiyil oḻukattalaippaṭṭaṉar. avarkaṭku makamatiyar eṉṟum, apputiya camayattiṟku makamatiya camayam eṉṟum peyarkaḷ muṟaiyē vaḻaṅkalāyiṉa. mekkāvai nīṅki avar, metiṉāvukkuc ceṉṟa nikaḻcci, iṉṉum. ijirā eṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. avar vaḻaṅkiya arumai moḻikaḷ, “kurāṉ” eṉṉum peyaruṭaiya arumaṟaiyil poṟikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. makamatiyarkaḷ, ammoḻikaḷ paramporuḷ, makamatu napiyiṉ vāyvaḻiyāka vaḻaṅkiyavai eṉṟu karutukiṉṟaṉar. intukkaḷ, taṅkaḷ nāṉmaṟaikaḷai āṇṭavaṉē aruḷiṉāṉ eṉṟum, ataṉāl avaṉukku “vētaṅkaḷ moḻinta pirāṉ” eṉappeyarum kūṟupavarākaliṉ, avarkaṭku itaṉkaṇ putumaiyoṉṟum tōṉṟātu. “aruḷneṟi” yākiya accamayamum, aruḷ neṟiyai uṇarttum iyalpu paṟṟi “iculām” camayam eṉṟē vaḻaṅkappeṟṟatu. ic camayam vaḷara varaḷa, arapiyar iṭaiyil irunta tīneṟikaḷum tīyoḻukkaṅkaḷum nīṅkiṉa; avarkaḷ aṉaivarum uṭaṉ piṟantāreṉavuṇarntu tammuḷ oṟṟumaiyuṇṭu paṇṇik koṇṭaṉar. taṅkaṭkup perunalam ceyta iccamayavuṇarvai nilavulakil yāṇṭum parappa vēṇṭu meṉṟa vēṭkaimikku, oru nūṟu yāṇṭukaḷukkuḷ, pāracīkam, turukkittāṉam, āpkaṉittāṉam eṉṟa iṉṉōraṉṉa nāṭṭu makkaḷ aṉaivaraiyum makamati camayattavarākkik koṇṭaṉar. pāracīkarum, nam intiya nāṭṭiṉ vaṭapakutiyil akkālattu malkiyirunta āriyarkaḷaip pōla, tīyiṉaik kaṭavuḷākak karuti vaḻipaṭum vaḻakkuṭaiyarāyiruntavarkaḷ. avarkaḷuṭ cilar immakamatiya camayattaimēṟ koḷḷātavarāy, intiyanāṭṭuṭ pukuntu kuṭiyēṟiṉar. makamatiyarum neṭuṅkālam varaiyil, ip pāracīkaruṭaṉum, tārttaruṭaṉum, ēṉaiya vaṭapulatta varuṭaṉum pōr nikaḻttiya vaṇṇamēyiruntamaiyiṉ, nam intiya nāṭṭil, ippoḻutaikkuc cumār toḷḷāyiramāṇṭu kaṭkumuṉ varamāṭṭātu niṉṟaṉar; piṉṉarē, avarkaḷ nam intiya nāṭṭiṟku varalāyiṉar.
ivarkaḷ, innāṭṭiṟku varutaṟku muṉpē pāracīkaruṭ cilar pōntu kuṭiyēṟi viṭṭaṉar eṉṟu muṉpu kūṟiyuḷḷōm. avarkaṭku muṉ vēṟupala nāṭṭiṉarum vantu iṅkē kuṭipukuntiruntaṉar. avarkaṭkum, makamatiyarkaṭkum periyatoru vēṟupāṭu uṇṭu. muṉṉē vantavarkaḷ tam camaya vuṇarvai nāṭṭiṟ parappavēṇṭu meṉṟa nōkkamuṭaiya varallar; ivarkaṭkup piṉṉarp pōnta airōppiyarum avvāṟu vantavarallar; ivarkaḷ maṭṭil camayat toṇṭiṉaik kuṟikkōḷākak koṇṭu vantaṉar eṉpatu pala cāṉṟukaḷāl viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. mēlum ivarkaṭku muṉṉē pōntavar, nam nāṭṭiṟ kuṭi pukuntu vāḻntirunta ēṉai makkaḷōṭu kūṭi, orumaiyuṟṟu, avarkaṭkuriya oḻukkam, vaḻipāṭu mutaliya neṟikaḷait tāmum ēṟṟu, intiyarōṭu intiyarāy iyaintiruntaṉar. avarkaḷ yavaṉar, skitiyar, maṅkōliyar, pārttiyar mutaliya palarāvar. avarkaḷ nāṭcellac cella, intukkaḷuḷ intukkaḷāy, tam peyar, moḻi, camayam, oḻukkam, uṭai, maṉappāṉmai eṉṟa palavaṟṟiṉum oṉṟiviṭṭaṉar. kiṟittu piṟappataṟku iru nūṟuyāṇṭukaṭku muṉ innāṭṭiṟkup pōnta kirēkka tūtar oruvar, innāṭṭiṟ palaviṭaṅkaḷukkuc ceṉṟār. avar tantai (Dion) eṉṟum, avar peyar hīliyō ṭōras (Helio dorus) eṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. avar oru camayam vaṭanāṭṭiluḷḷa tirumāl kōyil oṉṟiṟkuc ceṉṟu, avarai vaḻipaṭṭu, ammaṭṭil nillātu, oru “kōpuramum” eṭuppittār. piṉṉar, avar oru vaiṇava pākavatarāy, tirumālai vaḻipaṭṭu vantār. ivaraip piṉpaṟṟip pōntavar palarum, ivvaṇṇamē vaḻipāṭu ceyvatu vēṟṟunāṭṭu makkaṭkum iyaintatākum eṉṟu karutiṉar. icceyti pēs nakari (Bas nagar) luḷḷa tūṇiṟ cetukkap peṟṟirukkum kalveṭṭiṉāl namakkut terikiṉṟatu.
makamatiya camayattiṉar, nam nāṭṭiṟ pukuntapōtu, ikkaruttu nam nāṭṭil nilaviyiruntatāyiṉum, ivarkaḷ ataṉai mēṟ koḷḷavillai. iculām camayam kūṟum “allā” tirumāliṉ palvēṟu piṟappukkaḷuḷ oṉṟām eṉṟum, ic camaya vuṇmaikaḷai yulakiṟku vaḻaṅkiyaruḷiya makamatu perumāṉait tirumālaṭiyāruḷ oruvareṉṟum kūṟi, intukkaḷ ivarkaḷait tammuḷ oruvarāka iyaittuk koḷḷa muyaṉṟirukkiṉṟaṉar. vaṭamoḻiyuḷ vallunar, “allōpa niṣat” eṉṟoru nūlai yeḻuti, makamatiya vēntaruṭ ciṟantavarāṉa akpar eṉṟa peyar koṇṭa aracar perumāṉai, “avatāra mūrtti” eṉṟum collat toṭaṅkiṉar. āyiṉum, makamatiyar, ivai, tam camayak karuttukaṭku muraṇātal kaṇṭu, ivaṟṟai aṟavē ēlārāyiṉar. avarkaḷ, intukkaḷāṟ ceyyappaṭum “caṭaṅku” kaḷaiyum uṭaṉ paṭṭilar. “pala teyva vaḻipāṭu ceypavar tūyarallar; tūyatallāta yātum kāpāvil  pukuvatum tītu” eṉṟu kurāṉ moḻivatai avar viṭātu pōṟṟiṉar.
ivvāṟu, tiṇṇiya camayavuṇarviṉaik koṇṭirunta taṉāl, ivarkaḷ, nam nāṭṭiṟ pukuntu kuṭiyēṟiya piṉpum, tamatu vaḻakkam, oḻukkam, peyar, moḻi eṉṟa palavakaiyilum intuk kaḷiṟ pirintē yiruntu varalāyiṉar. orē nāṭṭiṟ piṟantu vaḷarntu vāḻntu vanta pōtum, avar innāṭṭavarōṭu kalappiṉṟiyē iruntaṉar. iv vēṟupāṭṭai nīkkik kalappaiyuṇṭu paṇṇum tuṟaiyil emmuyaṟciyum payaṉpaṭātu pōyiṟṟu. intiya nāṭṭiṟ pukuntu kuṭiyēṟi vāḻntuvarum intiya makamatiyar aṉaivarum, tam camayavuṇarvu nekiḻātu pōṟṟi vantaṉar. ataṉāl, avar tam uḷḷam, avaratu camayam piṟanta nāṭṭaiyē nāṭi niṟpatāyiṟṟu; nāṭōṟum, avarkaḷ ceyyum kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭṭilum, toḻukaiyilum avartam mukam arapiyanāṭṭu mekkāvaiyē nōkki niṟkiṟatu; avartam maṉappāṉmai, oḻukkamuṟai, āṭci muṟai, camayak kalvi ākiya yāvum arēpiyā, ciriyā, pāracīkam, ekiptu eṉṟa innāṭṭu iyalpiṉavākavē irukkiṉṟaṉa. ivar kaḷuṭaiya camaya moḻi, camaya cakāptam, ilakkiyam, āciriyaṉmār, aṭikaṇmār, kōyilkaḷ eṉṟayāvum orē vakaiyākavē irukkak kāṇkiṉṟōm. ivarkaḷ, innilavulakil eppuṟattil vāḻiṉum, ivaiyellām oru peṟṟiyavāy iruppatu kāṇappaṭumē tavira avvannāṭṭu ellaikku akappaṭṭu, āṅkē aṭaṅki oṭuṅkiyiruttalaik kāṇṭal aritu.
ivartam varukaikkum, ivarkaṭku muṉpōnta ēṉaiyōr varukaikkum irunta vēṟupāṭukaḷuḷ mēlē kāṭṭiyatu talai ciṟantatākum. itaṉōṭu oppap piṟitoru vēṟupāṭum uṇṭu. nam nāṭṭiṟku vanta makamatiyarkaḷ, āyirattu irunūṟu mutal āyirattu aṟunūṟām āṇṭuvaraiyil, araciyal, camūkaviyal eṉṟa ellāvaṟṟilum, pōrttiṟamum nāṭōṭikaḷāy nilai yiṉṟit tiriyum tiṟamumē nirampap koṇṭiruntaṉar. pācaṟaiyiṟ ṟaṅkum aracar tāṉaipōlavē, makamatiya araciyaṟ ṟalaivarkaḷum āṅkāṅku iruntuvantaṉar. oru nilaiyāyt taṅki, nāṭu nakaraṅ
kaḷaic cīrceytu, araciyaliṉ nilaimaiyai niṟuvi, nāṭukāttal, tīyarai yoṟuttal ākiya ceyalkaḷōṭu camūkattuṟaiyilum nalam pala ceyyat toṭaṅkiyatu piṟkālattiltāṉ nikaḻvatāyiṟṟu. kalappu maṇam, camayakkāyccaliṉmai, ottavurimai mutaliya pala naṉmaikaḷum piṉṉarē uṇṭāyiṉa.
uyarntōrkaḷiṭattil orukuṟai uṇṭākumāyiṉ, atu viraivil ulakaṟiyap parantuviṭum. avvaṇṇamē, oruvaṉ ceyta kuṟṟam ulakaṟiyat tōṉṟi nilaipeṟu māyiṉ, avaṉ - pāl oruvakaic ciṟappuṇṭeṉpatu tuṇintukoḷḷalām. ammuṟaiyē, makamatiya maṉṉarkaḷ kālattuc ciṟuceyalkaḷ cila, intiya nāṭṭu varalāṟṟuc ciṟu nūlkaḷuṭ kāṇappaṭumāyiṉ, avaṟṟaik koṇṭē, avarpāl amaintirunta perumai iṉitu uṇarappaṭukiṉṟatu. taṉ uyiraik kollutaṟkup pōnta araca puttiraṉ oruvaṉaip poṟuttu vīraṉākkic ciṟappitta āṇṭakai pāpar vēntarai aṟiyātār yāvar? intu camayattavaraiyum makamatiya camayattavaraiyum oppamatittu uyarnalam purinta aracar peruntakaiyāṉa akparai aṟiyātavar aṟivu peṟātavar allarō? apperun takaikaḷ āṟṟiyuḷḷa nalam palavum eḻutappukiṉ, ikkaṭṭurai varampiṉṟi virivatallatu avartam pukaḻnalam aṭaṅkiviṭum eṉa eṇṇuvatu ciṟumaiyaṉṟō? innalamaṉaittiṟkum aṭippaṭaiyākavuḷḷatu iculām camayattiṉ iyalpē eṉpatai maṟattal ākātu.
intiyanāṭṭiṉ teṉpakutiyākiya nam tamiḻnāṭṭai āṇṭa muṭiyuṭaivēntar mūvaruḷ cōḻarkaḷ kālattil, nilappaṭaiyuṭaṉ kaṭaṟpaṭaiyum iruntu nāṭukāttuvanta ceyti nām aṉaivarum aṟintatākum. “kāntaḷūrccālai kalamaṟut taruḷiya” irācarācaṉ mutaliya cōḻamaṉṉarkaḷ, kaṭaṟpaṭai koṇṭu ceṉṟu pakaippulaṅkaḷai veṉṟaṉar. avarkaṭkup piṉ vanta cōḻamaṉṉarkaḷ vīḻcciyaṭaiyavē, kaṭaṟpaṭaiyum keṭṭaḻintatu. piṟanāṭṭu makkaḷuṭaiya kūṭṭuṟavum avarkaḷ kālattiṟkuppiṉ kiṭaippatu aritākiyatu. āyiṉum ikkaṭaṟ paṭaiyum, piṟanāṭṭu makkaḷuṭaiya kūṭṭuṟavum im makamatiya maṉṉar perumakkaḷāl maṟuvalum uṇṭāyiṉa.
nam intiyanāṭṭiṉ vaṭapakutiyil vāḻnta makkaḷ, putta camaya kālattil ēṉai nāṭukaḷiṟ paravi avaṟṟōṭu cīriya toṭarpu peṟṟiruntaṉar. eṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil attoṭarpai nekiḻttu, tam nāṭṭellaikkuḷḷēyē aṭaṅkiyamaintu viṭṭaṉar. ataṉāl, innāṭu taṉṉilaiyil aṭaṅki, piṟavaṟṟōṭu toṭarpiṉṟit taṉippatāyiṟṟu. paṉṉiraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil, iculām camayatvariṉ peruntuṇaiyāl, attoṭarpiṉai mīṭṭum nam nāṭu peṟṟatu; āyiṉum attoṭarpu paṇṭē yiruntatu pōṉṟillai. putta camaya, kālattil nam nāṭṭavar vēṟu nāṭukaṭkuc ceṉṟatupōla, ikkālattil celvatu oḻintaṉar. pala āyirakkaṇak kiṉarāṉa vēṟṟu nāṭṭumakkaḷ nam nāṭṭiṟku varattoṭaṅkiṉar. makamatiya camayattavar māttiram vēṟu nāṭukaṭkuc ceṉṟu varuvatai mēṟkoṇṭiruntaṉar. ivar tam araciyal, patiṉeṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil taḷarcci yaṭaintu vīḻvuṟṟatu nām aṟivō maṉṟō? atuvaraiyil, vaṭamēṟkuk kaṇavāykaḷiṉ vaḻiyākap pokārā, cāmalkaṇṭu, pālku, kurācāṉ, kivāricum, pāracīkam eṉṟa nāṭukaḷiliruntu makkaṭ kūṭṭam nam nāṭṭiṟku vantu koṇṭiruntatu. annāḷil āpkāṉittāṉamum ṭilli vēntariṉ āṭciyiltāṉ aṭaṅkiyiruntatu. patiṉēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil, jahāṅkīr eṉṉum maṉṉaratu āṭcikkālattil, nam intiya nāṭṭiliruntu pōlaṉ kaṇavāy vaḻiyāka āṇṭu tōṟum 14000  oṭṭakac cumaiyaḷaviṉavāya poruḷ, kantakār, pāracīkam eṉṟa nāṭukaṭkuc ceṉṟaṉa. “mēlaikkaṭaṟ karaiyil irunta paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷ (Sea ports) veḷināṭṭuk kaṭaṟ pōkku varavukku vāyil tiṟantiruntaṉa. kīḻaikkaṭaṟkaraip paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷuḷ, macūlippaṭṭiṉam āyirattu aṟunūṟṟēḻām āṇṭuvaraik kōl koṇṭāvai yāṇṭa cultāṉkaḷukku uriyatāyiruntatu. ataṟkup piṉpē atu mukalāya vēntarkaṭku uriyatāyiṟṟu. appaṭṭiṉattiliruntu, ilaṅkai, cumittirai, cāvakam, cīyam, cīṉam eṉṟa nāṭukaṭku vāṇipam pēṇi, vaṅkaṅkaḷ pala ceṉṟa vaṇṇamiruntaṉa eṉa mukalāyarāṭci (Moghul administration) eṉṟa nūl kūṟukiṉṟatu.
vaṭa nāṭṭil mukalāya muṭimaṉṉarkaḷiṉ āṭci nilaviya kālam irunūṟuyāṇṭukaḷēyākum; āyiṉum, akkālattil nam nāṭṭiṟku uṇṭākiya nalaṅkaḷ palavākum. pala vēṟu vakai moḻi vaḻaṅkum nāṭṭiṟku araciyal moḻi yoṉṟu potuvāka vēṇṭappaṭuvatāyiṟṟu. aḵtu annāṭṭu moḻikaḷuḷ oṉṟāyiṉ, piṟamoḻi pēcuvōr kūṭṭattu amaiti uṇṭākātu eṉavuṇarntu, immaṉṉarkaḷ tamatu pāracīka moḻiyiṉaiyē araciyal moḻiyāka vakuttaṉar. piṉṉar atu palarkkum tāy moḻiyām nilaimaiyiṉaiyum aṭaivatāyiṟṟu. ataṉāl āṭcimuṟaiyum nāṇayac celāvaṇiyum orumuṟaiyāy naṭai peṟalāyiṉa. iṉṟum, kaccēri, jillā, tālukā, pirkkā, tāciltār, ucūr eṉa vaḻaṅkum coṟkaḷ palavum avartam āṭcineṟiyiṉ orumai muṟaiyiṉai viḷakkum aṭaiyāḷaṅ kaḷākum. nāṭṭu makkaḷiṭai rekṭā eṉappaṭum intuttāṉi moḻi vaḻaṅki vantatu.
ivarkaḷ kālattil, vintavaraikku vaṭakkiluḷḷanāṭu, pala ciṟṟaracark kurimaiyuṟṟu, pala ciṟu nāṭukaḷākap pirintu, eppoḻutum pōrum pūcalum nikaḻum amarkkaḷamā yiruntatu, avarkaḷaiyaṭakki, araciṟkuriya amaiti peṟuvittatōṭu. avar tam nāṭṭil amaitiyum iṉpamum nilavuvittaṉar. tam camayattai nāṭṭil parapputal vēṇṭum eṉṟa karuttu ivartam maṉattil mikkiruppiṉum, aracu kaṭṭil ēṟi makkaṭku nalam ceyvatum ciṟappuṭaippaṇi yākaliṉ, ivarkaḷ attuṟaiyiṉum cīriya aṟamē purintu pōntaṉar. annāṭṭil, perumpālum oru neṟippaṭṭa āṭcimuṟaiyum, potu moḻiyum nilavacceyta ipperumakkaḷiṉ tiruppaṇiyāl, iṭantōṟum moḻi, uṭai mutaliyavaṟṟil vēṟupaṭṭuk kiṭanta intiya makkaḷ, tava camūka voḻukkam, uṭai mutaliya avaṟṟuḷḷum orumai neṟiyai mēṟkoṇṭaṉar. nāṭṭil amaitiyum iṉpamum nilavavē, makkaḷ tam tāymoḻiyilum mikka payiṟci koṇṭu ciṟanta ilakkiyaṅkaḷ ceytaṉar. iṉpamum, amaitiyum urimaiyum nilavum nāṭṭiltāṉ uyarnta ilakkiyaṅkaḷum, ariya karuttuk kaḷum piṟakkum eṉa aṟiñar kūṟuvar. ivarkaḷ kālattil ivai mikap peruki nāṭṭiṟkuc ciṟappu nalkiṉa. tāṉ aṭainta mutumaiyiṉait tāṉpeṟṟa makaṉukku nalki, avaṉatu kaṭṭiḷamaiyait tāṉ peṟṟu makiḻnta aracarkaḷ intukkaḷil iruntaṉar. tam varalāṟṟiṉait tāmē varainta aracar pāpar perumāṉ. nāṭṭil vaḻaṅkiya nāṇayaṅkaḷ, cakaṭakkāl pōl makkaḷ kaiyakattu nillātu  peyarum nīrmaiyavāyiṉum, avarkaḷāl kātalittup pōṟṟappaṭum iyalpaṟintu avaṟṟiṉ vaḻiyāka aṟattaip parappi niṟuviya aṟivunalam ivarkaḷpāl iyalpākavē amaintu kiṭantatu. ‘nallatu ceyvatu āṇṭavaṉukku aḷaviṟanta uvappait taruvatu; naṭunilai tiṟampātu naṉṉeṟiyiṟ ceṉṟōr keṭuvatilar; keṭṭār eṉa yām kēṭṭatumillai” eṉap poṟitta nāṇayaṅkaḷ ivarkaḷ kālattil aṟaneṟi vaḻaṅki makkaḷai aṟivuṭaiyarākkiṉa. “ayiṉ akpari” eṉṟa araciyal nūl akpar eṉṉum aracar peruntakai kālattil piṟanta teṉpatu ciṟumakārum aṟinta ceyti yākum.
pōrttuṟaiyil palatiṟamāṉapayiṟcikaḷum, nākarikattil ciṟappum, kalviyil perumāṇpum nalkiya ivar tam kālattil, kaittoḻilum, ciṟpamum piṟavum ulaku pukaḻ uyarvu peṟṟaṉa. intiya cīṉakkaivēlai yamaippukkalanta intucāracaṉ kait tiṟaṅkaḷ nāṭṭil vaḷam peṟṟaṉa. putiya putiya ciṟpat toḻilkaḷ aracar ātaravu peṟṟaṉa. “pukai mukantaṉṉa mācil tūvuṭai” eṉavum, “nōkku nuḻaikallā nuṇmaiyapūk kaṉintu, aravuriyaṉṉa aṟuvai” eṉavum paṇṭait tamiḻc cāṉṟōr ceytutaviyatu pōṉṟa nuṇ viṉaiyuṭaikaḷum, melliya paṭṭāṭaikaḷum ivarkaḷ kālattē vaḷampeṟṟu ulaku pukaḻum oṇporuḷāyc ciṟantaṉa. kampaḷikaḷum, piṉṉalkaḷum, kiṉkāp (Kin khab)  eṉpaṉavum, “nuraikavarntaṉṉa meṉpūṅkampala”ṅkaḷum pukaḻvōr pulavaraiyiṟantu viḷaṅkiṉa.
inta iculām camayap perumakkaḷāl nam nāṭṭil viḷainta nalaṅkaḷ palavum eḷitil kaṇṭukōṭaṟkeṉa oruvāṟu tiraṭṭit tantu ikkaṭṭuraiyiṉai muṭittuk koḷḷukiṉṟōm.
1.	cōḻa vēntarkaḷiṉ vīḻccikkup piṉ keṭṭaḻintirunta kaṭaṟpaṭaiyum piṟanāṭṭuk kūṭṭuṟavum paṇṭēpōl  mīṭṭum tōṟṟam peṟṟuc ciṟantaṉa.
2.	vintiyavaraikku vaṭakkil uḷḷa nāṭṭil, ivartam viṉait tiṭpam, āṭci neṟi, naṉmaṉap paṇpu eṉpavaṟṟāl amaitiyum iṉpamum nilavuvavāyiṉa.
3.	tām veṉṟu aṭippaṭutti nāṭeṅkum oru taṉmaiyum, oruneṟiyum poruntiya āṭcimuṟai ivarkaḷ kālattē nāṭṭil naṭaipeṟuvatāyiṟṟu.
4.	uyar vakuppiṉaruḷ camaya vēṟṟumai yiruntateṉiṉum, camūkavoḻukkam, uṭaimutaliyavaṟṟil makkaṭkiṭaiyē oṟṟumai yeyti yiruntatu. camaya vēṟṟumaiyum kāyccalum pūcalumāy māṟiyatu pēraracap peruntakaikaḷ, kālattil illai.
5.	intuttāṉiyeṉṟum, rekṭā eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum moḻi potumoḻiyāyiṟṟu; araciyaṟ potumoḻi pāracika moḻi.
6.	avvannāṭṭut tāymoḻikaḷ, amaitiyum iṉpamum nilaviyataṉ payaṉāy vaḷampeṟṟaṉa. varalāṟu kūṟum nūlkaḷum, araciyal nūlkaḷum nalliṭam peṟṟut tikaḻntaṉa.
7.	pala teyvak koḷkaiyum vaḻipāṭum kūṟum camayaneṟi kaṭkiṭaiyē oru teyvak koḷkaiyum vaḻipāṭum aṟiñar maṉattuḷ vērūṉṟi viḷaṅkiṉa. ataṉāl, cila camayaṅkaḷ cīriya tiruttam aṭaintaṉa.
8.	pōrmuṟaiyilum, nākarikavaḷarcciyilum palvakai nalaṅkaḷ ivarkaḷāl nam nāṭu peṟṟuc ciṟappaṭaintatu.
9.	intucāracaṉ kaittiṟaṅkaḷum, ciṟpat tiṟaṅkaḷum peru makkaḷiṉ ātaravu peṟṟaṉa. poṉṉum maṇiyum ceṟinta nuṇmāṇkaiviṉaikaḷ, ivarkaḷ kālattiṟ peṟṟatu pōl piṟa ekkālattum mēmpāṭu peṟavillai yeṉiṉ, aḵtu iṟappap pukaḻum pukaḻcciyākātu.
10.	paṭṭiṉum paruttiyiṉum āya uṭaivakaikaḷ neytalum, kampala necavum piṟavum inta islām camayattavar nam intiya nāṭṭil ceyta iṉiya toṇṭiṉāl mēṉmaiyaṭaintaṉa.
ikkūṟiya nalaṅkaḷ aṉaittiṟkum uriyarāy viḷaṅkum ipperumakkaḷiṉ naṟpukaḻ innilavulakil eṉṟum niṉṟu nilavuka eṉa vāḻtti ikkaṭṭuraiyai ivvaḷavil niṟuttukiṉṟōm.

13. tamiḻakam - vaṭavellai
